Kaḷaviyal (TP3)

((last modification: 2004 september 30th))

R 65,T 5

((3. kaḷavu iyal))

|ivvōttu |eṉṉa peyarttō |eṉiṉ, kaḷaviyal |eṉṉum peyarttu; kaḷavoḻukkam |uṇarttiṉamaiyāṟ peṟṟa peyar. |aḵtātal |īṇṭu |uraikkiṉṟataṉāl payaṉ |iṉṟām; kaḷaveṉpatu |aṟam |aṉmaiyiṉ [|eṉil], |aṟṟaṉṟu; kaḷavu |eṉṉum coṟ kaṇṭuḻiyellām |aṟappāṟpaṭāteṉṟal |amaiyātu. kaḷavāvatu, piṟarkkuriya poruḷ maṟaiyiṟ kōṭal. |iṉṉataṉṟi, |ottārkkum mikkārkkum potuvākiya kaṉṉiyarait tamar koṭuppak koḷḷātu, kaṉṉiyar tam |iccaiyiṉāl tamarai maṟaittup puṇarntu piṉṉum |aṟanilaivaḻāmal niṟṟalāl |iḵtu |aṟameṉappaṭum. |aṉtātal |iccūttirattāṉum viḷaṅkum.

|aḵtaṟṟāka, mēlai |ottiṉōṭu |ivvōttiṟku |iyaipu |eṉṉai yeṉiṉ, mēl kaikkiḷai mutaṟ peruntiṇai |iṟuvāyāka |eḻutiṇai |oti |avaṟṟiṉ puṟattu nikaḻun tiṇaikaḷum vāyāka |eḻutiṇai |oti |avaṟṟiṉ puṟattu nikaḻun tiṇaikaḷum |otip pōntār. |avveḻutiṇaiyiṉum |orutalai vēṭkaiyākiya kaikkiḷaiyum, |oppilkūṭṭamākiya peruntiṇaiyum |oḻittu |iruvaraṉpum |otta nilaimaiyākiya naṭuvaṇ |aintiṇaikkaṇṇum puṇarppum piritalum |iruttalum |iraṅkalum |ūṭalumākiya |uripporuḷ kaḷavu kaṟpu |eṉṉum |iruvakaik kaikōḷiṉum nikaḻumātaliṉ, |avviruvakaik kaikōḷiṉuṅ kaḷavākiya kaikōḷ |ivvōttiṉuḷ |uṇarttutalāṉ |avaṟṟiṉ piṉ kaikōḷ |ivvōttiṉuḷ |uṇarttutalāṉ |avaṟṟiṉ piṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. |itu naṭuvaṇaintiṇaikkaṇ nikaḻum poruṭpākupāṭāyiṉ, |akattiṇaiyiyaliṉ piṉ vaikkaṟpālatu |eṉiṉ, |āṇṭu vaikkak karutiṉ ``veṭci tāṉē kuṟiñciyatu puṟaṉē' [puṟattiṇai- 59] |eṉṉum māṭṭēṟu peṟātām |ataṉiṭaik kaḷaviyalum kaṟpiyalum kiṭattalāṉ |eṉka.

maṟṟum, |aḵtu yāṅkaṉam |uṇarttiṉārō |eṉiṉ,
``kāmap puṇarcciyum |iṭantalai paṭalum
pāṅkoṭu taḻā |alun tōḻiyiṟ puṇarvumeṉṟu
|āṅkanāl vakaiyiṉum |aṭainta cārvoṭu
maṟaiyeṉa mo#āḻitaṉ maṟaiyōr |āṟē'' (ceyyuḷiyal. 178)
|eṉpataṉāṉ |innālvakaiyum |itaṉuḷ |uraikkappaṭukiṉṟateṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. kāmappuṇarcci yeṉiṉum, |iyaṟkaip puṇarcciyeṉiṉum, muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci yeṉiṉum, teyvap puṇarcci yeṉiṉum |okkum. |ivaiyellām kāraṇappeyar. |aḵtāvatu, |ottār |iruvar tāmē kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam. |iṭantalaippāṭāvatu, |iyaṟkaip puṇarcci puṇarnta talaimakaḷ piṟṟaiñāṉṟum |avviṭattuc ceṉṟu |etirppaṭutal. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamāvatu, |ippuṇarcci pāṅkaṟku |uraittu, nī yemakkut tuṇaiyāka vēṇṭumeṉṟa |avaṉ kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟu talaimakaḷ niṉṟa nilaiyai yuṇarttac ceṉṟu kūṭutal. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamāvatu, |ivaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar |ikkūṭṭam nīṭaccēṟal vēṇṭit tōḻiyai |irantu piṉṉiṉṟu |avaḷ vāyilākak kūṭutal. |ivai nāṉkum |immuṟaiyē nikaḻum |eṉṟu koḷka. |iṉi |immuṟai nikaḻātu |iṭaiyīṭu paṭṭuvarum. |aḵtāmāṟu, talaimakaḷ |etirppaṭṭuḻi |aṉpuṭaiyār |ellārkkum |iyaṟkaip puṇarcci muṭṭiṉṟik kūṭutal |ulakiyal |aṉmaiyāṉ talaimakaḷai yā#ātāṉum |oriṭattu |etirppaṭṭa talaimakaṉ |avaḷ kātaṟkuṟippuṇarntu niṉṟu, kūṭṭattiṟku |iṭaiyīṭu |uṇṭāyuḻipum |āṇṭuc ceṉṟa vēṭkai taṇiyātu niṉṟu, muṉṉai ñāṉṟu kaṇṭāṟ pōlap piṟṟai ñāṉṟum kāṇṭāṟ pōlap piṟṟai ñāṉṟum kāṇalākumō |eṉa |āṇṭuc cēṟalum, talaimakaḷum |avvāṟē vēṭkaiyāṉ |aṭarppuṇṭu |āṇṭuvarutalum |ākiyavaḻip puṇarcci nikaḻum. |āṇṭu |āyattārāṉātal piṟarāṉātal |iṭaiyīṭapaṭṭuḻit taṉvaruttattiṉaip pāṅkaṟku |uṇartti |avaṉ talaimakaḷ niṉṟuḻi yaṟintu kūṟa, |āṇṭucceṉṟu puṇarum. |avviṭattum |iṭaiyīṭu paṭṭuḻit tōḻivāyilāka muyaṉpuṟeytum. |ivvāṟum, |orōvoṉṟu |iṭaiyīṭupaṭṭu varutalum |uḷavām. |avvāṟāyiṉ, |iyaṟkaip puṇarcci |iṭaiyīṭupaṭṭuḻi varainteytal takkataṉṟō |eṉiṉ, varainteytuntiṟam nīṭṭikkumāyiṉ vēṭkai niṟuttalāṟṟātār puṇarcci karuti muyalpa. |ivvāṟu cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷ vantaṉavum |uḷavō |eṉiṉ, cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷum |ivvāṟu poruḷkoḷḷa |ēṟpaṉa |uḷa. |avaiyāvaṉa :-
``maruntiṉ tīrā maṇṇiṉ |ākātu
|aruntava muyaṟciyiṉ |akaṟṟalum |aritē
tāṉcey nōykkut tāṉmarun tākiya
tēṉ|imir naṟaviṉ tēṟal pōla
nītara vanta niṟai |arun tuyaramniṉ
|āṭukoṭi maruṅkiṉ |aruḷiṉ |allatu
piṟitil tīrāteṉpatu |eḻumō neñcacē
nāṭuvaḷaṅ koṇṭu pukaḻnaṭutal vēṇṭittaṉ
|āṭumaḻait taṭakkai |aṟuttumuṟai ceyta
poṟkai naṟuntārp puṉaitērp pāṇṭiyaṉ
koṟkai |am peruntuṟai kuṉitirai tokutta
viḷaṅkumuttu |uṟaikkum veṇpal
paṉmāṇ cāyal paratavar makaṭkē.''

|itaṉuḷ ``|āṭukoṭi maruṅkiṉ |aruḷiṉ |allatu piṟitil tīrātu'' |eṉpataṉāṉ |iyaṟkaip puṇarcci |iṭaiyīṭu paṭṭup puṇarccikarutik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. ``mayilkol maṭavāḷkol mānīrt tiraiyuḷ payiltatōr teyvaṅkol kēḷir--kuyil payirum kaṉṉi |iḷañāḻal pūmpoḻil nōkkiya kaṇṇiṉ varuntum|eṉ neñcu.'' (tiṇaimoḻi-49) |itaṉuḷ |aiyanilaiyaip pāṅkaṟku |uraittaliṉ puṇarcci |iṉṟāyiṟṟu. ``koṇṭal māmaḻai kuṭakkērpu kuḻaitta ciṟukōl |iṇara peruntaṇ cāntam vakaicēr |aimpāl takaipeṟa vārip pularviṭat tutirta tukaḷpaṭu kūḻaip peruṅkaṇ |āyam |uvappat tantai neṭuntēr vaḻaṅkum nilavumaṇal muṟṟattup pantoṭu peyarum parivi lāṭṭi |aruḷiṉum |aruḷāḷ |āyiṉum peritaḻintu piṉṉailai muṉiyalmā neñcē |eṉṉatū |um |aruntuyar |avalan tīrkkum marunta piṟitillai yāṉ|uṟṟa nōykkē.'' (naṟṟiṇai-140) |itaṉuḷ `|aruḷiṉum |aruḷāḷāyiṉum' |eṉṟamaiyāl kūṭṭamiṉmaiyum, piṉṉilai muṉiyal' |eṉṟamaiyāl |irantu piṉṉiṟpāṉākat tuṇintamaiyum, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattu |iyaṟkaip puṇarccikku |oruppaṭṭamaiyum |uṇark. ``naṟavukkamaḻ |alari naṟavu vāyvirintu |iṟaṅkitaḻ kamaḻum |icaivāy neytaṟ kaṇṇit talaiyar karuṅkaip paratavar |oṇcuṇaṅ kiḷamulai |oruñāṉṟu puṇariṉ nuṇkayiṟ ṟuṟuvalai numaroṭu vāṅkik kaitai vēli |ivvūrc ceytūṭ ṭēṉō ciṟukuṭi yāṉē.'' periyīreṉac cēṭpaṭuttavaḻik kūṟiyatu. ``kūṟuvam kollō kūṟalam kolleṉak karanta kāmam kainniṟuk kallātu nayantunām viṭṭa naṉmoḻi nampi |araināḷ yāmattu viḻumaḻai karantu kārvirai kamaḻuṅ kūntaṟ ṟūviṉai nuṇṇūl |ākam poruntiṉaḷ veṟpiṉ |iḷamaḻai cūḻnta maṭamayil pōla vaṇṭuvaḻip paṭarat taṇmalar kūṭaccūl |amcilam poṭukki |añciṉaḷ vantu tuñcūr yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ |āṉṟa kaṟpiṟ cāṉṟa periyaḷ |ammā |arivaiyō |allaḷ teṉā|atu |ā |ay naṉṉāṭṭu |aṇaṅkuṭaic cilampaṟ kaviram peyariya vurukeḻu kavā |aṉ nērmalai niṟaicuṉai |uṟaiyuñ cūrmakaḷ mātō |eṉṉumeṉ neñcē.'' |itu tōḻiyiṟkūṭiya talaimakaḷ kūṟṟu. ``|avarai poruntiya paiṅkural |ēṉal kavari maṭamā ka tū|um paṭarcāral kāṉaka nāṭa maṟaval vayaṅkiḻaikku yāṉiṭai niṉṟa puṇai.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu 1) |itaṉāṉē muntuṟṟakūṭṭamiṉmai yuṇarka. |iṉi |oru kūṭṭamum nikaḻātu |āṇaṭu vantaṭaivēṭkai |iruvarkkum taṇiyātu niṉṟu varainteytalum |oṉṟu. |ivvakaiyiṉāṉ |ikkaḷavoḻukkam mūvakaippaṭum.

{SA TP89i}

  1. |iṉpamum poruḷum |aṟaṉum |eṉṟāṅku
  2. |aṉpoṭu puṇarnta |aintiṇai maruṅkiṉ
  3. kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅ kālai
  4. maṟaipōr tē |ettu maṉṟal |eṭṭaṉuḷ
  5. tuṟaiyamai nalyāḻt tuṇaimaiyōr |iyalpē.

|iṉi, |itaṉ talaiccūttiram |eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, kāmappuṇar/ccikku |ilakkaṇa vakaiyāṟ kuṟiyiṭutalai |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu, |eṉpatu cūttiram.

(|itaṉ poruḷ.) |iṉpamum poruḷu......kāṇuṅ kālailai |eṉpatu--|iṉpamum poruḷum |aṟaṉum |eṉṟu collappaṭṭu, |aṉpoṭupuṇarnta naṭuvaṇ |aintiṇaiyiṭattu nikaḻum kāmakkūṭṭattiṉai |ārāyuṅkālattu |eṉṟavāṟu.

|aṉpoṭu puṇarnta |aintiṇai |eṉṟataṉāl kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaiyai |oḻittu niṉṟa mullai kuṟiñci pālai marutam neytal |eṉak koḷḷappaṭum. |avai |aṉpoṭu puṇarntavāṟu |eṉṉai |aṉpiṉarākip puṇartalum piritalum |iruttalum |ūṭalum |iraṅkalum nikaḻttup |ākalāṉum, |avai nikaḻuṅkāl |attiṇaikku |uṟuppākiya |iṭamum kālamum karupporuḷum tuṇaiyāki nikaḻumākalāṉum, |ivai |iṉpoṭu puṇarntaṉa |eṉka. |aḵtēl, |aintiṇaip puṟattavākiya veṭci, vañci, |uḻiñai, tumpai, vākai |eṉpaṉavum |aṉpoṭu puṇarntaṉavām |eṉiṉ. |avai |aṉpuṭaiyār palarkūṭi nikaḻttupavai yākaliṉ, |aṉpoṭu puṇarntaṉavām.

``|aṟattiṟkē |aṉpucārpu |eṉpa |aṟiyār
maṟattiṟkum |aḵtē tuṇai'' (kuṟaḷ-76) |eṉpataṉālum koḷka.

|iṉi, |ivvaintiṇaiyum |iṉpamum poruḷum |aṟamum |āyiṉavāṟu |eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, puṇartal mutalākiya |aintuporuḷum |iṉpantarutaliṉ |iṉpamāyiṉa. mullai mutalākiya |aintiṇaikkum |uṟuppākiya nilamum kālamum karupporuḷum |ivaṟṟiṉ puṟattākiya veṭci vañci |uḻiñai tumpai |eṉpaṉavum vākayuḷ |oru kūṟum poruḷākayāṉum, poruḷāyiṉa. |ivvoḻukkaṅkaḷ |aṟattiṉvaḻi nikaḻtalāṉum, pālaiyākiyaya vākaippaṭalattuḷ |aṟanilai kūṟutalāṉum, |ivai |aṟamāyiṉa. |aḵtēl, kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaiyum |ivaṟṟiṉ puṟamākiya pāṭāṇpāṭṭum kāñciyum |aṟamutalākiya mūṉṟumaṉṟi |aṉpoṭu puṇartal vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, kāñci |aṉpoṭu puṇarāmaiyum, pāṭāṇpāṭṭu |aṉpoṭu puṇartal |orutalaiyaṉmaiyum |avvōttiṉuḷ kaṇṭukoḷka |ēṉaiyiraṇṭum |aṉpoṭu puṇarāmai mēṟcollappaṭṭaṉa. |iṉi |avai |aṟaṉum poruḷumāy |iṉpamākā: |aḵtēl, |aṟaṉum poruḷum |ākāmaiyum vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, kulaṉum kuṇaṉuṅ kalviyum |uṭaiyarākiya |antaṇar |eṉa vicēṭittavaḻi |ēṉaiyōrkku |immūṉṟu poruḷum |iyaital vēṇṭumeṉṉum niyamam |iṉmaiyiṉ |ēṟṟavaḻik koḷḷappaṭum

|iṉi, |aintiṇaimaruṅkiṟ kāmakkūṭṭam |eṉpatu puṇartal mutalākiya |uripporuḷum, |annilamum kālamum karupporuḷum, kaḷaviṉum kaṟpiṉum varutaliṉ, |avai |orōvoṉṟu |iruvakaippaṭum. |avaṟṟuḷ, puṇarcciyākiya |iruvakaiyiṉum kaḷavākiya kāmakkūṭṭam |eṉakkoḷka.

|iṉṉum |aṉpoṭupuṇarnta |aintiṇaimaruṅkiṟ kāmakkūṭṭam |eṉṟataṉāl |ellānilattiṉum kāmakkūṭṭam nikaḻappeṟum |eṉṟu koḷka. |ivvāṟātal cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattuk kāṇka.

maṟaiyōr tē|et...yōriyalpē |eṉpatu-maṟaiyōriṭat tōtappaṭṭa maṇam |eṭṭaṉuḷḷum tuṟaiyamai nalyāḻiṉai yuṭaiyarākiya tuṇaimaiyōr neṟi |eṉṟavāṟu.

maṟaiyōr |eṉṟatu |antaṇarai. tē|em |eṉṟatu |avaratākiya nūlai. maṇam |eṭṭāvaṉa: piramam, piracāpattiyam, |āriṭam, teyvam, kāntiruvam, |acuram, |irākkatam, paicācam |eṉpaṉa. piramamāvatu, kaṉṉiyai |aṇikalaṉ |aṇintu piramacāriya yiruppāṉoruvaṉukkuttāṉa mākak koṭuppatu. piracāpattiyamāvatu, makaṭkō#āṭaṟkuriya kōttirattār makaḷ vēṇṭiyavaḻi |irumutukuravarum |iyaintu koṭuppatu. |āriṭamāvatu, |oṉṟāṉum |iraṇṭāṉum |āvum |āṉēṟum vāṅkikkoṭuppatu. teyvamāvatu, vēḷvikku |āciriyarāy niṉṟār palaruḷḷum |oruvarkku vēḷvittī muṉṉark koṭuppatu. kāntiruvamāvatu |otta |iruvar tāmē kūṭuṅkūṭṭam. |acuramāvatu, villēṟṟiṉāṉātal tiripaṉṟi yeytāṉātal kōṭaṟkuriyaṉeṉk kūṟiyavaḻi |atu ceytāṟkuk koṭuttal. |irākkatamāvatu talaimakaḷ taṉṉiṉum tamariṉum peṟātu valitiṟkoḷvatu. paicācamāvatu, kaḷittārmāṭṭun tuyiṉṟārmāṭṭuṅ kūṭutal.

``|aṟaṉnilai |oppē poruḷkōḷ teyvam yāḻōr kūṭṭam |arum poruḷ viṉaiyē |irākkatam pēynilai |eṉṟik kūṟiya maṟaiyōr maṉṟal |eṭṭavai |avaṟṟuḷ tuṟaiyamai nalyāḻt tuṇaimaiyōr puṇarppiṉataṉ poruṇmai |eṉmaṉār pulamai yōrē.'' |eṉapataṉāluṅ koḷka. tuṟaiyamai nalyāḻt tuṇaimaiyōrāvārkantiruvar. |avar |iruvarākit tiritaliṉ tuṇaimaiyōr |eṉṟār. tuṇaiyaṉpāvatu |avar |oḻukalā ṟōṭottu makkaṇmāṭṭu nikaḻvatu. |īṇṭuk kāmakkūṭṭameṉa |otappaṭṭatu maṇavikaṟpamākiya |eṭṭaṉuḷḷuṅ kantiruvameṉṟavāṟu/. mālaicūṭṭal yātaṉuḷ |aṭaṅkumeṉiṉ, |atuvum |otta |aṉpiṉarāy nikaḻtaliṟ kantiruvappāṟpaṭum. |aṟaṉum poruḷum |iṉpamum |eṉṉātu, |iṉpamum voruḷum |aṟaṉum |eṉṟatu |eṉṉai |eṉaṉ, palavakai |uyirkaṭkum varum |iṉpam |iruvakaippaṭum. |avaiyāvaṉa, pōkam nukartalum vīṭupoṟutalum |eṉa. |avaṟṟuḷ vīṭupēṟu tuṟavaṟattil niṉṟārkkallatu |eytal |aritāyiṟṟu. pōkanukartal maṉaiyaṟattārk keytuvatu. |avareytum |iṉpamum |avviṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇamākiya poruruḷum |apporuṭkuc kāraṇamākiya |aṟaṉum |eṉak kāriya kāraṇam nōkkivaittār |eṉka. |itaṉāṟ colliyatu |īṇṭukkaḷaveṉṟōtap paṭukiṉṟa |oḻukkam |aṟampayavāta puṟaneṟiyaṉṟu; vētaviyākiya tantiram |eṉa vikaṟpamākiya neṟi kūṟiyavāṟu. (1)

{SA TP90i}

  1. |oṉṟē vēṟē |eṉṟiru pālvayiṉ
  2. |oṉṟi |uyarnta pāla tāṇaiyiṉ
  3. |otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa
  4. mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivarai |iṉṟē.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, |itu kāmakkūṭṭattiṉkaṇ talaimakaṉum talaimakaḷum |etirppaṭuntiṟaṉum |ataṟkuk kāraṇamum |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. (|i-ḷ.) |oṉṟē vēṟē |eṉṟiru pālvayiṉ |eṉpatu--|oruvaṉum |oruttiyumāka |illaṟañ ceytuḻi, |avviruvaraiyum maṟupiṟappiṉum |oṉṟuvittalum vēṟākkutalumākiya |iruvakai |ūḻiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu.

|oṉṟi |uyarnta pālatāṇaiyiṉ |eṉṟatu--|iruvaruḷḷamum piṟapputtōṟum |oṉṟu nalviṉaikkaṇṇē nikaḻnta |uḻiṉatu |āṇaiyiṉ |eṉṟavāṟu.

|uyarntataṉmēṟcellum maṉanikaḻcci |uyarnta pālāyiṟṟu. kāmanikaḻcciyiṉkaṇ |otta |aṉpiṉarāyk kūṭutal nalviṉaiyāṉ |allatu vārāteṉpatu karuttu.

|otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyuṅ kāṇpa |eṉpatu-- |oppu pattuvakaippaṭum. |avai, ``piṟappē kuṭimai |āṇmai |āṇṭōṭu |uruvu niṟutta kāma vāyil niṟaiyē |aruḷē |uṇarvoṭu tiruveṉa muṟaiyuṟak kiṟanta |oppiṉatu vakaiyē'' (meyp. 25) |eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiyaṟ cūttirattuḷ kūṟiya pattumām. |avaṟṟuḷ piṟappāvatu, |antaṇar, |aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar, |āyar, vēṭṭuvar kuṟavar, nuḷaiyar |eṉṟāṟpōla varuṅkulam. kuṭimaiyāvatu |akkulattiṉuḷḷār |ellāruñ ciṟappāka |ovvāmaiyiṉ |acciṟappākiya |oḻukkampaṟṟiya kuṭivaravaik kuṭimai |eṉṟār. ``piṟappokkum |ellā |uyirkkum ciṟappovvā ceytoḻil vēṟṟumai yāṉ'' (kuṟaḷ. 972) |eṉap piṟarum kulattiṉkaṇṇē ciṟappeṉpatu |oṉṟuṇṭeṉṟu kūṟiṉārākaliṉ. |āṇmaiyāvatu, |āṇmaittaṉmai. |aḵtāvatu, |āḷviṉaiyuṭaimaiyum vali peyarāmaiyumām. ``moḻiyā tataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal'' (marapiyal. 110) |eṉpataṉāl talaimakaḷ māṭṭup poṇmaiyum koḷḷappaṭum. |atu peṇṭirkku |iyalpākiya nāṇam mutalāyiṉavum peṇṇīrmaiyum. |āṇṭeṉpatu, |oruvariṉoruvar mutiyaraṇṟi |otta paruvattarātal |atu kuḻavipparuvaṅ kaḻintu patiṉāṟu pirāyattāṉum paṉṉiraṇṭu to. po. |i.-11 pirāyattāḷum |ātal. |uruvu |eṉpatu vaṉappu. niṟutta kāmavāyil |eṉpatu nilainiṟuttappaṭṭa puṇarccikku vāyil. |aḵtāvatu, |oruvar māṭṭu |oruvarkku nikaḻum |aṉpu. niṟai |eṉpatu |aṭakkam. |aruḷ |eṉpatu piṟar varuttattiṟkup puriyum karuṇai, |uṇarveṉpatu |aṟivutiru |eṉapatu celvam. |ippattu vakaiyum |otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum |etirppaṭuvar |eṉak koḷka.

mikkō......|iṉṟē |eṉpatu--|ikkuṇaṅkaḷāl talaimakaṉ mikkāṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭiyappaṭātu |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē, |ivaṟṟuḷ yātāṉum |oṉṟiṉāyiṉum talaimakaḷ mikkāḷāyiṉ |aintiṇaiyiṟ kaṭiyappaṭum |eṉṟavāṟām.

pālatāṇaiyiṉ...kāṇpa |eṉpatu--|oruvarai yoruvar kaṇṭuvaḻiyellām puṇarcci vēṭkai tōṟṟāmaiyiṟ pālatāṇaiyāṉ |oruvarai yoruvar puṇartaṟ kuṟippoṭu kāṇpa |eṉṟavāṟu. mikkōṉāyiṉum |eṉṟa |ummai |iṟantu taḻī|iya |eccavummai. |eṟṟukku, |etirmaṟaiyākki `|iḻintōṉāyiṉum kaṭiyappaṭātu' |eṉṟāt kuṟṟameṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, centamiḻ nilattu vaḻakkoṭu civaṇivarukiṉṟa perumaiyātaliṉ, |iḻintāṉoṭu |uyarntāṭkuḷatākiya kūṭṭamiṉmai peruvaḻakkātaliṉ |atu poruḷākak koḷḷām |eṉpatu. |īṇṭuk kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum |eṉa |oruvaṉum |oruttiyumu/pōlak kūṟiṉārāyiṉum, ``|orupāṟ kiḷavi yēṉaippāṟ kaṇṇum varuvakai tāṉē vaḻakkeṉa moḻipa'' (poruḷiyal-26) |eṉpataṉāṉum, |innūl |ulaka vaḻakkē nōkkutalāṉum |avar palavakaippaṭuvar. |aḵtāmāṟu |antaṇar |aracar vaṇikar viṉaiñar |eṉṉum nālvaroṭum |anulōmar |aṟuvaraiyum kūṭṭap patiṉmarāvar. |ivarai nālvakai nilattōṭu |uṟaḻa nāṟpatiṉmarāvar. |ivaraiyum |avvannilattiṟkuriya |āyar vēṭṭuvar kuṟavar paratavar |eṉṉuntoṭak kattārōṭu kūṭṭap palarāvar. |avaraiyum |uyirppaṉmaiyāṉ nōkka varampilarāvar.

{SA TP91i}

  1. ciṟantuḻi |aiyañ ciṟanta teṉpa
  2. |iḻintuḻi |iḻivē cuṭṭa lāṉa.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, |aiyam nikaḻum |iṭam |uṇar/ttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) |oruvaṉ |oruttiyaik kaṇṇuṟṟiḻi |avviru vakaiyum |uyarvuṭaiyarāyiṉ |avviṭattu |aiyam ciṟantatu |eṉṟu colluvar; |avar |iḻipuṭaiyarāyiṉ, |avviṭattu |eṉpatu mikuti, |aiyamikutalāvatu makkaḷuḷḷāḷ |allaḷ teyvamō |eṉa mēlāyiṉārōṭē |aiyuṟutal, ciṟantuḻi |eṉpataṟkut talaimakaḷtāṉ ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum; |avaḷaik kaṇṭa |iṭam |aiyappaṭutaṟkuc ciṟantuḻiyum koḷḷappaṭum. |uruvamikutiyuṭaiyaḷātaliṉ |āyattāriṭaik kāṇiṉum teyvam |eṉṟu |aiyuṟutal. |itaṉāṟ colliyatu |ulakattut talaimakaṉum talaimakaḷumāka nammāl vēṇṭappaṭṭār |antaṇar mutalākiya nāṉku varuṇattiṉum |āyar vēṭṭuvar kuṟavar paratavar |eṉṉum toṭakkattiṉum |akkulattārākiya kuṟunila maṉṉar māṭṭum |uḷarāvaraṉṟē; |avarellāriṉum celvattāṉum kulattāṉum vaṭivāṉum |uyarnta talaimakaṉum talaimakaḷumāyiṉōr māṭṭē |aiyam nikaḻvatu. |allātār māṭṭum |av viḻimarapiṉaiyē cuṭṭiyuṇarā niṟkumātalāṉ |eṉṟavāṟu. `kārārap peyta' |eṉṉum mullaik kaliyuḷ (kalit. 109) ``paṇṇit tamartan torupuṟan tai|iya kaṇṇi |eṭukkallāk kōṭēn takalalkul puṇṇilār puṇṇāka nōkku muḻumeyyuṅ kaṇṇaḷō |āyamakaḷ'' |eṉa |aiyamiṉṟic cuṭṭiyuṇarntavāṟu kāṇka. |iṉi |uyarpuḷvaḻi |aiyam nikaḻuvāṟu: ``|uyarmoḻik kiḷavi |uṟaḻuṅ kiḷavi |aiyak kiḷavi |āṭū |uviṟku |urittē'' (poruḷiyal-42) |eṉṟārākaliṉ, |aiyappaṭuvāṉ talaimakaṉ |eṉṟu koḷka. talaimakaḷ |aiyappaṭātatu |eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, |avaḷ |aiyappaṭuṅkāl teyvamōveṉṟu |aiyuṟal vēṇṭum. |avvāṟu |aiṟṟāl |accam varum. |aḵtu |etuvākak kāma nikaḻcci yuṇṭākātu. (3)

{SA TP92i}

  1. vaṇṭē |iḻaiyē vaḷḷi pūvē
  2. kaṇṇē |alamaral |imaippē |accameṉṟu
  3. |aṉṉavai piṟavum |āṅkaṇ nikaḻa
  4. niṉṟavai kaḷaiyuṅ karuvi |eṉpa.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, |aiyappaṭṭāṉ tuṇitaṟkuk karuvi |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) |eṇṇappaṭṭa vaṇṭu mutalākiya |eṭṭum piṟavumāki |avviṭattu nikaḻāniṉṟa |aiyam kaḷaiyum karuvi |eṉṟavāṟu. |aiyameṉpatu |atikārattāṉ vantatu. `nikaḻāniṉṟavai' |eṉpatu kuṟukiniṉṟatu. vaṇṭāvatu mayiriṉ |aṇinta pūvaic cūḻum vaṇṭu. |atu payiṉṟataṉ mēlallatu cellāmaiyiṉ |atuvum makkaḷuḷḷāḷeṉṟaṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. |iḻaiyeṉpatu |aṇikalaṉ. |atu ceyyappaṭṭateṉata tōṟṟutalālum, teyvappūṇceyyā |aṇiyātalāṉum |atuvum |aṟitaṟakuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. vaḷḷi |eṉpatu mulaiyiṉum tōḷiṉum |eḻutiya koṭi. |atuvum |ulakiṉ |uḷḷatākit tōṉṟutaliṉ (|atuvum) karuviyāyiṟṟu. |alamaral |eṉpatu taṭumāṟutal. teyvamāyiṉ niṉṟavaḻi niṟkum. |avvāṟaṉṟi, niṉṟuḻi niṟkiṉṟilaḷ |eṉṟu cuḻaṟciyum |aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. |imaip peṉpatu kaṇṇimaittal. teyvattiṟkuk kaṇ |imaiyāmaiyiṉ |atuvum |aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. |accameṉpatu |āṇmakkaḷaik kaṇṭu |añcutal. |atu teyvattiṟku |iṉmaiyāṉ |atuvum |aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiṟṟu. |aṉṉavai piṟavum |eṉṟataṉāṉ kālnilantōytal, viyarttal, niḻalāṭu koḷka. |ivai karuviyākat tuṇiyappaṭum |eṉṟavāṟu. kāṭci mutalākiya |ittuṇaiyum kaikkiḷaik kuṟippām. |itaṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka. |iṉik kuṟippaṟital kūṟukiṉṟārākiliṉ |akkuṟippu nikaḻumvaḻi |ivaiyellām |akamām. |eṉṉai? |iruvar māṭṭumotta nikaḻcci yātalāṉ. |ivai talaimakaḷ māṭṭup pulappaṭanikaḻātu. |āṇṭuk kuṟippiṟāṟ ciṟitu nikaḻumeṉṟu koḷka. |avai varumāṟu :- ``|uṇṭārkaṇ |allatu |aṭunaṟāk kāmampōl kaṇṭār makiḻceytal |iṉṟu'' (kuṟaḷ. 1090) |eṉavarum. piṟavum |aṉṉa.

{SA TP93i}

  1. nāṭṭam |iraṇṭum |aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
  2. kūṭṭi |uraikkuṅ kuṟippurai yākum.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ mēl talaimakaḷai |ittaṉmaiyaḷ |eṉat tuṇinta talaimakaṉ kuṟippaṟiyātu cāraluṟiṉ peruntiṇaippāṟpaṭu mākalāṉum |ik kantiruvaneṟikku |otta |uḷḷattārātal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, |āṇṭu |oruvarōṭoruvar collāṭutal marapaṉmaiyāṉum, |avaruḷḷak karuttaṟital vēṇṭutaliṉ, |ataṟkuk karuviyāya |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) nāṭṭam |iraṇṭum |eṉpatu--talaimakaṉ kaṇṇum talaimakaḷ kaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aṟivuṭampaṭuttaṟku |eṉpatu--|oruvar vēṭkaipōla |iruvarkkum vēṭkai |uḷatākuṅ kollō |eṉak kavarttu niṉṟa |iruvaratu |aṟiviṉaiyum |oruppaṭuttaṟku |eṉṟavāṟu. kūṭṭiyuraikkuṅ kuṟippuraiyākum |eṉpatu--tamatuvēṭkaiyoṭu kūṭṭi |oruvar |oruvarukku |uraikkuṅ kāmakkuṟippuraiyām |eṉṟavāṟu. |itaṉ poḻippu :- |iruvarkkuṅ kavarttu niṉṟa |aṟivai |oruppaṭuttaṟ poruṭṭu vēṭkaiyoṭukūṭṭik kūṟuṅ kāmakkuṟippuccol |iruvaratu nāṭṭamākum |eṉṟavāṟu. |ākum |eṉpataṉai nāṭṭam |eṉapataṉoṭu kūṭṭiyuraikka. |itaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``pāṉalan taṇkaḻip pāṭaṟintu taṉ|aimār nūlnala nuṇvalaiyāṟ koṇṭeṭutta--kāṉaṟ paṭupulāl kāppāḷ paṭaineṭuṅkaṇ nōkkam kaṭipollā |eṉṉaiyē kāppu.'' (tiṇaimālai nūṟ. 31) kaṇṇiṉāṉ |aṟipa |eṉṟavāṟu.

{SA TP94i}

  1. kuṟippē kuṟittatu koḷḷu māyiṉ
  2. |āṅkavai nikaḻum |eṉmaṉār pulavar.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭai yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) |oruvar kuṟippu |oruvar kuṟittataṉaik koḷḷumāyiṉ, |avviṭattuk kaṇṇiṉāṉ varuṅ kuṟippurai nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē, kuṟippaik koḷḷātavaḻi |akkuṟippurai nikaḻātu |eṉṟavāṟām. |itaṉāṟ colliyatu kaṇṭa kālattē vēṭkai muntuṟṟa vaḻiyē |ikkaṇṇiṉāṉ varuṅ kuṟippu nikaḻvatu; |allātavaḻi nikaḻātu |eṉṟavāṟu. |iṉik kuṟippu nikaḻumāṟum |ataṉ vēṟupāṭum meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ kūṟupa. |īṇṭum cila |utāraṇam kāṭṭuvam. ``nōkkiṉāḷ nōkki |iṟaiñciṉāḷ |aḵtavaḷ yāppiṉuḷ |aṭṭiya nīr'' (kuṟaḷ. 1093) |eṉavum, ``|acaiyiyaṟku |uṇṭāṇṭōr |ē|eryāṉ nōkkap pacaiyiṉaḷ paiya nakum'' (kuṟaḷ-1098) |eṉavum varum. piṟavum |aṉṉa. talaimakaṉ kuṟipput talaimakaḷ |aṟinta vaḻiyum kūṟṟu nikaḻātu, peṇmaiyāṉ.

{SA TP95i}

  1. perumaiyum |uraṉum |āṭū|u mēṉa.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, |itu talaimakaṟku |uriyatōr |ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) perumai yāvatu--paḻiyum pāvamum |añcutal |uraṉ |eṉpatu--|aṟivu. /|ivai yiraṇṭum |āṇmakaṉukku |iyalpu |eṉṟavāṟu. |itaṉāṉē mēṟcollappaṭṭa talaimakaḷatu vēṭkaik kuṟippuk kaṇṭa talaimakaṉ, |annilaiyē puṇarcciyai niṉaiyātu, varaintu |eytum |eṉpatu peṟutum. ``ceṉṟa |iṭattāl celaviṭā tī tori|i naṉṟiṉpāl |uyppa taṟivu'' (kuṟaḷ. 422) |eṉpavākaliṉ. talaimakaṉ |ivvāṟu kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``vēyeṉat tiṇṭatōḷ veṟikamaḻ vaṇar|aimpāl māṉveṉṟa maṭanōkkiṉ mayiliyal taḷarpolki |aycilppu |ariyārppa valirāḷi |iḻai|imaippak koṭiyeṉa miṉṉeṉa |aṇaṅkeṉa yātoṉṟum terikallā |iṭaiyiṉkaṭ kaṇkavarpu |oruṅkōṭa vaḷamaicāl |uyarciṟappiṉ nuntaitol |uṇarkūṭal /|iḷamaiyāṉ |eṟipantō ṭikattantāy kēḷiṉ; pūntaṇtārp pularcāntiṉ teṉṉavaṉ |uyarkūṭal tēmpāya |aviḻnīlat talarveṉṟa |amaruṇkaṇ |ēntukōṭ ṭeḻilyāṉai yoṉṉātārk kavaṉvēliṟ cēntunī yiṉaiyaiyāl |ottatō ciṉmoḻi; poḻipeyal vaṇmaiyāṉ |acōkantaṇ kāviṉuḷ kaḻikaviṉ |iḷamā#āviṉ taḷiraṉṉāy |ataṉtalaip paṇaiyamai pāymāṉtēr |avaṉceṟṟār niṟampāynta kaṇaiyiṉu nōyceytal kaṭappaṉṟō kaṉaṅkuḻāy; vakaiyamai taṇtārāṉ kōṭuyar poruppiṉmēl takaiyiṇar |iḷavēṅkai malaraṉṉa cuṇaṅkiṉāy matavali mikukaṭā |at tavaṉyāṉai maruppiṉum katavavāl takkatō kāḻkoṇṭa |iḷamulai; |eṉavāṅku, |iṉaiyaṉa kūṟa |iṟaiñcupu nilanōkki niṉaiyupu neṭi toṉṟu niṉaippāḷpōṉ maṟṟāṅkē tuṇaiyamai tōḻiyark kamartta kaṇṇaḷ maṉaiyāṅkup peyarntāḷeṉ |aṟivakap paṭuttē.'' (kalit. 57) ``|uṟukaḻi maruṅkiṉ |otamoṭu malarnta ciṟukaru neytal kaṇpōl māmalarp perun taṇ māttaḻai yirunta |alkul |aiya |arumpiya cuṇaṅkiṉ vai|eyiṟṟu maiyīr |oti vāṇutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ viḷaiyāṭ ṭāyamoṭu veṇmaṇal |utirtta puṉṉai nuṇtātu poṉṉiṉ noṇṭu maṉaipuṟan taruti yāyiṉ |eṉaiyatū |um |immaṉaik kiḻamai yemmoṭu puṇaril tī tum |uṇṭō māta rāyeṉak kaṭumpari naṉmāṉ koṭiñci neṭuntēr kaival pākaṉ paiyeṉa |iyakka |arivē yuṇkaṇ paṉivara loṭukkic ciṟiya |iṟaiñciṉaḷ talaiyē periya |evvam māmiva ṇuṟavē.'' (|akam. 230) |i vai |uḷḷappuṇarcciyāṉ varaital vēṇṭip pāṅkaṟku |uraittaṉa. (7)

{SA TP96i}

  1. |accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum muntuṟutal
  2. niccamum peṇmāṟ kuriya |eṉpa.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, |itu talaimakaṭku |uriyatōr |ilakkaṇam |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) |accamum nāṇum pētaimaiyum |immūṉṟum nāṭōṟu muntuṟutal peṇṭirkku |iyalpu |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē, vēṭkaiyuṟṟuḻiyum |accattāṉātal nāṇāṉātal maṭattāṉātal puṇarccikku |icaiyātu niṉṟu varainteytal vēṇṭumeṉpatu pōntatu. |ivvāṟu |iruvarum |uḷḷappuṇarcciyāl niṉṟu varain teyti meyyuṟum. |itaṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``tīmpāl kaṟanta kalamāṟṟik kaṉṟellān tāmpiṟ piṇittu maṉainiṟī|i yāytanta pūṅkarai nīlam puṭai tāḻa meyyacai |ip pāṅkaru mullaiyun tāya pāṭṭaṅkāl tōḻinam pulliṉat tāyar makaḷirō ṭellām |oruṅku viḷaiyāṭa |avvaḻi vanta kuruntampūṅ kaṇṇip potuvaṉmaṟ ṟeṉṉai muṟṟiḻai |ē|er maṭanallāy nīyāṭuñ ciṟṟil puṉaikō ciṟiteṉṟāṉ |ellānī peṟṟēmyā meṉṟu piṟarceyta |illiruppāy kaṟṟa tilaimaṉṟa kāṇ|eṉṟēṉ muṟṟiḻāy tātucūḻ kūntal takaipeyat tai|iya kōtai puṉaikō niṉakkeṉṟāṉ |ellānī |ētilār tantapūk koḷvāy naṉimikap pētaiyai maṉṟa periteṉṟēṉ mātarāy |aiya pitirnta cuṇaṅkaṇi meṉmulaimēl toyyi leḻutukō maṟṟeṉṟāṉ yāmpiṟar ceypuṟam nōkki |iruttumō nīperitum maiyalai mātō viṭukeṉṟēṉ taiyalāy colliya vāṟellām māṟumāṟu yāṉpeyarppa |allāntāṉ pōlap peyarntāṉ |avaṉainī |āyar makaḷir |iyalpurait tentaiyum yāyum |aṟiya |uraittīyiṉ yāṉuṟṟa nōyuṅ kaḷaikuvai maṉ.'' (kalit. 111) |itu talaimakaḷ |uḷḷappuṇarcciyiṉ |urimaipūṇṭiruntavāṟum varainteytak kūṟaluṟṟavāṟum kāṇka.

{SA TP97i}

  1. vēṭkai |orutalai |uḷḷutal melital
  2. |ākkañ ceppal nāṇuvarai |iṟattal
  3. nōkkuva |ellām |avaiyē pōṟal
  4. maṟattal mayakkañ cākkāṭu |eṉṟic
  5. ciṟappuṭai marapiṉavai kaḷaveṉa moḻipa.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻuṅkālam |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

mēl, ``perumaiyum |uraṉum |āṭū|u mēṉa'' |eṉavum, ``|accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum muntuṟutal niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriya |eṉpa'' (tol. kaḷa. 8) |eṉavum |otiyavataṉāṉ |uḷḷañ ceṉṟavaḻiyum meyyuṟupuṇarcci varainteyti nikaḻpa |eṉṟārām. |avvāḻi cākkāṭellaiyākiya meyppāṭuvariṉ meyyuṟṟup puṇarappeṟu meṉpatu |uṇarttiṟṟu. (|i-ḷ,) vēṭkai mutalākac cākkāṭu |īṟāka |otappaṭṭakāmac ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavāṟṟāṟ kaḷavu |āmeṉṟu colluvar |eṉṟavāṟu. |āṉum |āmum |eñci niṉṟaṉa. |ivaṟṟai |avattaiyeṉpa |aḵtēl, |avai pattuḷavaṉṟē; |īṇṭuraittaṉa |oṉpatāleṉiṉ, kāṭci vikaṟpamuṅ kūṟiṉār, |aḵtu |uṭpaṭap pattām. kāṭci vikaṟpamākiya |aiyamun tuṇivum mutalatu; vēṭkai |iraṇṭāvatu, |eṉṟu koḷka. vēṭkai yāvatu--peṟalvēṇṭu meṉṉum |uḷḷanikaḻcci. |orutalaiyuḷḷuta lāvatu--|iṭaiviṭātu niṉaittal. ``|uḷḷik kāṇpeṉ pōlvaṉ muḷḷeyiṟṟu |amiḻtam |ūṟuñ cevvāyk kamaḻakil |āram nāṟum |aṟalpōṟ kūntaṟ pēramar maḻaikkaṇ koṭicci mūral muṟuvaloṭu matai|iya nōkkē.'' (kuṟun. 286) melita lāvatu--|uṇṇāmaiyāṉ varuvatu. |ākkañceppa lāvatu--|uṟaṅkāmaiyum |uṟuva|otalum mutalāyiṉa kūṟutal. ``|oṇtoṭi |arivai koṇṭaṉaḷ neñcē vaṇṭimir paṉittuṟait toṇṭi |āṅkaṇ |uravukkaṭal |olittirai pōla |iravi ṉāṉun tuyilaṟi yēṉē'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 172) |eṉavarum. nāṇuvaraiyiṟatta lāvatu--nāṇ nīṅkutal. ``kāmam viṭuvoṉṟō nāṇviṭu nalneñcē yāṉō poṟēṉiv viraṇṭu.'' (kuṟaḷ- 1247) nōkkuva |ellām |avaiyē pōṟa lāvatu--taṉṉāṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa |ellāṟ tāṉ kaṇṭa |uṟuppuppōlutal. ``|oṅkeḻiṟ kompar naṭuvi teṉappulluṅ kāntaṭ kivarum karuviḷam pūkkoḷḷum māntaḷir kaiyil taṭavaru māmayil pūmpoḻi ṉōkkip pukuvaṉa piṉcellun tōḷeṉac ceṉṟu tuḷaṅkoḷi vēytoṭu nīḷkatup piḵteṉa nīr |aṟal |uṭpukum'' |eṉṟātpōlvaṉa. maṟattal--pittātal. mayakkamāvatu--mōkittal. kākkāṭu--cātal. |ivaṟṟuḷ cātal pattām |avattaiyātalāl, |oḻinta |eṭṭuṅkaḷavu nikaḻtaṟkuk kāraṇamām |eṉṟu koḷka. |itu talaimakaṭkum talaimakaṟkum |okkum. |ivaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka. (9)

{SA TP98i}

  1. muṉṉilai yākkal colvaḻip paṭuttal
  2. naṉṉayam |uraittal nakainaṉi |uṟā|a
  3. |annilai yaṟital melivuviḷak kuṟuttal
  4. taṉṉilai yuraittal teḷivakap paṭuttaleṉṟu
  5. |iṉṉavai nikaḻum |eṉmaṉār pulavar.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, |iḵtu |iyaṟkaip puṇarccikkuriyatōr tiṟaṉ |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. taṉiyiṉāl talaimakaḷai yetirppaṭṭa talaimakaṉ taṉatu perumaiyum |aṟivum nīkki vēṭkai mī tūrap puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉāṉāyiṉum, talaimakaḷ māṭṭu niṟkum |accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum nīkkatalum vēṇṭumaṉṟē, |avai nīṅkutaṟporuṭṭu |ivaiyellām nikaḻumeṉpatu. |ulakattuḷḷār |ilakkaṇamellām |uraikkiṉṟā rākaliṉ, |ivvāciriyar |uraikkiṉṟavāṟṟāk nikaḻtal porumpāṉmaiyākavum, ``colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻi'' (tol. kaḷaviyal-11) |eṉa |otutaliṉ, |ivaiyellām nikaḻtaliṉṟic ciṟupāṉmai vēṭkai mikutiyāṟ puṇarcci kaṭitiṉmuṭiyavum peṟumeṉavuṅ koḷka.

(|i-ḷ.) muṉṉilaiyākkal |eṉpatu--kāmakkuṟippuṇmai |aṟinta talaimakaṉ vēṭkaiyāṟ cāraniṉaittavaḻit talaimakaḷum vēṭkaik kuṟippuṭaiyā ḷāyiṉum kulattiṉ vaḻivanta |iyaṟkaiyaṉmaiyāṉ nāṇamum |accamum mītūra |akkuṟip pillātāraip pōl niṉṟavaḻi |avaḷai muṉṉilaiyākap paṭuttuc cila kūṟutal. ``|oḷḷiḻai makaḷirō ṭōraiyum |āṭāy vaḷḷitaḻ neytaṟ ṟoṭalaiyum puṉaiyāy viripūṅ kāṉal |oruciṟai niṉṟōy yārai yōniṟ ṟoḻutaṉam viṉavutum kaṇṭār kaṇṭā nalattai teṇṭiraip peruṅkaṭaṟ parappiṉ |amarntuṟai |aṇaṅkō |iruṅkaḻi maruṅkiṉ nilaipeṟ ṟaṉaiyō col|iṉi maṭantai |eṉṟaṉeṉ |ataṉ|etir muḷḷeyiṟṟu muṟuvalun tiṟantaṉa pallitaḻ |uṇkaṇum parantavāl paṉiyē.'' (naṟṟiṇai-155) colvaḻip paṭutta lāvatu--tāṉ collukiṉṟa colliṉ vaḻi |avaḷ niṟkumāṟu paṭuttuk kūṟutal. ``colliṟ colletir koḷḷāy yāḻaniṉ tirumukam |iṟaiñci nāṇuti katumeṉak kāmaṅ kaimmikiṉ tāṅkutal |eḷitō koṭuṅkēḷ |irumpuṟam naṭuṅkak kuttip puliviḷai yāṭiya pulavunāṟu vēḻattiṉ talaimarup pēyppak kaṭaimaṇi civantaniṉ kaṇṇē katava |alla naṇṇār |āṇṭalai matila rākavum muracukoṇṭu |omparaṇ kaṭanta |aṭupōrc ceḻiyaṉ perumpeyark kūṭal |aṉṉaniṉ karumpuṭait tōḷum |uṭaiyavāl |aṇaṅkē.'' (naṟṟiṇai-29) naṉṉaya muraitta lāvatu--talaimakaḷiṉatu nalattiṉai yuraittal. ``cēral maṭavaṉṉañ cēralnaṭai yovvāy cēral maṭavaṉṉañ cēralnaṭai yovvāy |ūrtirai vēli yuḻakkit tirivāḷpiṉ cēral maṭavaṉṉañ cēralnaṭai yovvāy.'' (cilap. kāṉal-23) nakainaṉi yuṟā|a |annilai yaṟitalāvatu--talaimakaṉ taṉ naṉṉaya muraittalaik kēṭṭa talaivikku |iyalpāka |akattu |uḷavākum makiḻvāl puṟantōṉṟum muṟuvaṟkuṟippu mikkuttōṉṟā |annilaiyiṉait talaivaṉ |aṟital. ``māṇ|iḻai pētai nāṟiruṅ kūntal |āṇamum |illāḷ nīr|uṟai cūruṭaic cilampiṟ kaṇaṅkāy muyaṉṟa ceṟiyiyal notumal nōkkaik kāṇmō neñcē vaṟitāl muṟuvaṟ keḻumiya nuṭaṅkumeṉ paṇaivēy ciṟukuṭi yōḷē.'' melivu viḷakkuṟutta lāvatu--talaivaṉ |akattuṟum nōyāṟpuṟattu nikaḻum taḷarviṉaik kuṟippāṉ |eṭuttukkūṟalum. |utāraṇam vantuḻik kāṇka. kuṟippāvaṉa: puṟattuṟuppā yavark kiṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa. taṉṉilai yuraitta lāvatu--|ap puṟanikaḻcciyiṉ poliviḻavaik kaṇṭa talaimakaḷmāṭṭut talaivaṉ taṉ |uḷḷa vēṭkaimītūrviṉai nilaippaṭakkūṟutal. ``colliṟ colletir koḷḷāy yāḻaniṉ'' |eṉṉum naṟṟiṇaippāṭṭuḷ, ``kāmaṅ kaimmikil tāṅkutal |eḷitō ... ... ... ... ... kaṭaimaṇi civantaniṉ kaṇṇē katava |alla.'' (naṟṟiṇai-39) |eṉat taṉṉilai yuraittavāṟu kāṇka. teḷivu |akappaṭutta lāvatu--talaivaṉ muṉṉilaiyākkal mutaliyāṉa kūṟip piṉṉar |iyaṟkaippuṇarcciyiṉai viḻaintu niṉṟāṉāka, |ap puṇarcciyiṉaik kūṟuvār/, muṉṉam |ottapaṇpuṭaimai |uḷḷattu |iruvarmāṭṭum vēṇṭutaliṉ talaimakaḷ paṇpiṉait talaivaṉ |aṟintu |atteḷivait taṉṉakappaṭuttut tērtal. ``yāyum yāyum yārā kiyarō'' |eṉṉum kuṟuntokaippāṭṭuḷ, ``|aṉpuṭai neñcan tāṅkalan taṉavē'' (kuṟaḷ. 40) |eṉa |iyaṟkaippuṇarcci muṉṉart talaivaṉ talaiviyar |uḷḷam |otta paṇpiṉaik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. |itukāṟum |iyaṟkaippuṇar/ccikkuriya tiṟaṉkūṟi, mēl |iyaṟkaip puṇarcci nikaḻumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. |aḵtēl, taṉṉilai yuraittalait teḷivakappaṭuttaluṭaṉ |iṇaittu meyyuṟupuṇarcci yākki piṉṉart tōṉṟun tuṟaiyākap paṭuttālō |eṉiṉ, |atu cāṉṟōr vaḻakkiṉṟātalāṉum, meyyuṟupuṇarcci muṉ, ``meytoṭṭup payiṟal'' (kaḷaviyal-11) mutaliyaṉa yāṇṭum nikaḻnatē talaivikku meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻumātalāṉum |aḵtaṉṟeṉka.

{SA TP99i}

  1. meytoṭṭup payiṟal poypā rāṭṭal
  2. |iṭampeṟṟut taḻā|al |iṭaiyūṟu kiḷattal
  3. nīṭuniṉain tiraṅkal kūṭuta luṟutal
  4. colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻit
  5. tīrāt tēṟṟam |uḷappaṭat tokai|ip
  6. pērāc ciṟappiṉ |irunāṉku kiḷaviyum
  7. peṟṟavaḻi makiḻcciyum pirintavaḻik kalaṅkalum
  8. niṟpavai niṉai|i nikaḻpavai |uraippiṉum
  9. kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum
  10. peṭṭa vāyilpeṟ ṟiravuvali yuṟuppiṉum
  11. |ūrum pēruṅ keṭutiyum piṟavum
  12. nīriṟ kuṟippiṉ nirampak kūṟit
  13. tōḻiyaik kuṟaiyuṟum pakutiyum tōḻi
  14. kuṟaiyavaṭ cārtti meyyuṟak kūṟalum
  15. taṇṭā tirappiṉum maṟṟaiya vaḻiyum
  16. collavaṭ cārttaliṟ pulliya vakaiyiṉum
  17. |aṟintōḷ |ayarppi ṉavvaḻi maruṅkil
  18. kēṭum pīṭuṅ kūṟalum tōḻi
  19. nīkkali ṉākiya nilaimaiyum nōkki
  20. maṭalmā kūṟum |iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nāṉkiṉiṭattum talaivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻumāṟum, kātalmikku |āṟṟāmai kaiyikappiṉ talaivaṉām |iyalum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) meytoṭṭup payiṟa lāvatu--perumaiyum |uraṉumuṭaiya talaimakaṉ teḷivakappaṭuttiyatu kāraṇamākak kātal veḷḷam puraṇaṭōṭat talaiviyiṉ meyyait tīṇṭiyap payiṟal ``tīṇṭalum |iyaivatu kollō māṇṭa villuṭai viḷaiyar kalliṭupu |eṭutta puṉkaṇ maṭamāṉ nērpaṭat taṉ|aiyar cilaimāṇ kaṭuvicaik kalainiṟat taḻuttik kurutiyoṭu paṟitta ceṅkōl vāḷi māṟukoṇ ṭaṉṉa |uṇkaṇ nāṟiruṅ kūntaṟ koṭicci tōṉē.'' (kuṟaḷ-272)

poy pārāṭṭa lāvatu--talaiviyiṉ |aimpāl mutaliya kaṭai kuḻaṉṟu citaiviṉṟēṉum |aḵtuṟṟatāka maruṅkuceṉṟu toṭṭāṉ |orkāraṇam poyyākap paṭaittu |uraittup pāraṭṭal.
``kaṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvarniṉ |aiyar
|uṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻkaimaṉ nīyum
miṭalpuk kaṭaṅkāta vemmulaiyō pāram
/|iṭarpuk kiṭukum |iṭaiyiḻaval kaṇṭāy.'' (cilap. kāṉal-17)

|iṭampeṟṟut taḻā|al |āvatu--poypārāṭṭal kāraṇamāt talaivimāṭṭu |aṇimaiyiṭam peṟṟut taḻuvakkūṟal. |utāraṇam:
``kolyāṉai veṇmaruppum kolval puliyataḷum
nallāṉai niṉṉaiyar kūṭṭuṇaṭu--celvārtām
|or |ampi ṉāṉeytu pōkkuvaryāṉ pōkāmai
|īr |ampi ṉāṉeytāy |iṉṟu.'' (tiṇaimālai-22)

|iṭaiyūṟu kiḷatta lāvatu-- nāṇ maṭaṉ nilaikkaḷaṉāk koṇṭa talaivi taṉ |aṟivu nalaṉ |iḻantu |oṉṟum |aṟiyātu |uyarttaṉaḷ. |aḵtu |okkumō |eṉiṉ, |okkum. putitāyppukkār, |ūṟṟuṇarvu |eṉṟumpayilāta tam melliyal meyyiṟ paṭa |aṟiviḻappiṉum |uḷ nekku |uyirkkum |eṉka. |atupaṟṟip pulaiyaṉ toṭu tīmpāl pōl kātal kūrak kompāṉum koṭiyāṉum cārnatāḷait talaivaṉ puṟam|occi niṟkavum |āṇṭum kalakkalām poḻutu kūṭāmaikku niṉaintu |iraṅkal.

kūṭutaluṟuta lāvatu--|iṅṅaṉamāyk kāṭci nikaḻviṉ piṉṉarp puṇarcci |eytalum.

|ituvarai |iyaṟkaippuṇarcciyāṅ kāraṇaṅkūṟik kūṭutaluṟutalāl meyyuṟupuṇarcci kūṟiṉār.|ivaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
``vīḻum |iruvarkku |iṉitē vaḷiyiṭai
pōḻap paṭā|a muyakku.'' (kuṟaḷ-1108)

|itu kūṭutaluṟutal. piṟa vantuḻik kāṇka.

colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻi |eṉpatu-- |iyaṟkaip puṇarccikkuk kaḷaṉāka mēṟkūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭaṉ |av viṉpan tiḷait talaiyum viraivāka |oṉṟāyp peṟṟa viṭattu. |itteyvap puṇrccikkup poruḷ kūṟuṅkāl, payiṟal, pārāṭṭal, taḻā |al, kiḷattal, |iraṅkal, |uṟutal, nukarcci, tēṟṟam |eṉṟu collappaṭṭa |irunāṉku kiḷaviyum |eṉa |eṇṇappaṭuttuka. ``meytoṭṭup payiṟal'' mutalākak ``kūṭuta luṟutal'' varai |iyaṟkaip puṇarccikkē |uriya kūṟi, ``colliya nukarcci'' mutal ``|irunāṉku kaḷivi'' varai |iṭantalaippāṭum cērttu |uṇarttiṉār. |aṟṟāyiṉ nukarcciyum tēṟṟamum |iyaṟkaip puṇarcciyaṉṟō, |iṭan talaippāṭāmāṟu |eṉṉai yeṉiṉ, naṉṟu kaṭāyiṉāy. meyyuṟupuṇarcciyiṉaip pāl kūṭṭum neṟivaḻippaṭṭup peṟṟārkku meytoṭṭup payiṟal mutal |aṟu tuṟaiyē |iṉṟiyamaiyāt tuṟaiyāka, |ēṉaiya |iraṇṭum |iṭantalaippāṭṭiṟkum cērnta tuṟaiyākaliṉ, potuppaṭa |iraṇṭaṟkum naṭuvē vaittuc ceppam |ākkiṉāreṉka. nukarcciyum tēṟṟamum |eṉpa pirittuk kūṭṭuka. tīrāttēṟṟa māvatu--|iyaṟkaippuṇarcciyuṭaṉ muṭiyāta teḷivu. ``vēṭṭapoḻutiṉ |avaiyavai pōlumē tōṭṭār katuppiṉāḷ tōḷ.'' (kuṟaḷ-1005) |iḵtu |iyaṟkaip puṇarccit tuṟaiyaṉṟu; |iṭantalaippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talaimakaṉ kūṟiyatu: nukarcci peṟṟatu. ``kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyum |aimpulaṉum |oṇtoṭi kaṇṇē |uḷa'' (kuṟaḷ-1101) |eṉpatō |eṉiṉ, |iyaṟkaippuṇarccikkaṇ nukarcci yuṟṟamai kūṟiṟṟu |eṉka. ``|emmaṇaṅ kiṉavē makiḻna muṉṟil naṉaimutir/ puṉkiṉ pūttāḻ veṇmaṇal vēlaṉ puṉainta veṟi |ayar kaḷantoṟuñ cennel vāṉpori citaṟi |aṉṉa |ekkar naṇṇiya |emmūr viyaṉ tuṟai nēr|iṟai muṉkai paṟṟic cūrara makaḷirō ṭuṟṟa cūḷē.'' (kuṟun-53) /|iḵtu |iyaṟkaippuṇarccip piṉṟaic coṟṟa tīrāt tēṟṟa vurai. ``|iṉṉicai yurumoṭu'' |eṉṉum |akappāṭṭuḷ, ``niṉmār paṭaitaliṉ |iṉitā kiṉṟē nummil pulampiṉum |uḷḷutoṟum naliyum'' (|akam-15) |eṉṟatu |iṭantalaippāṭṭil nērnta tēṟṟam. ``pērāc ciṟappiṉ'' |eṭṭu |eṉṟal. pērum ciṟappiṉai |āṟu |eṉṟalai |eṭuttōttāṟ kāṭṭi niṉṟatu. |ituvarai |iyaṟkaip puṇarcciyum |iṭantalaippāṭum. mēl `vāyil ṭeppiṉum' |eṉṉumaḷavum mā#āṅkaṟkūṭṭam; mēl toṭarpavai tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam. peṟṟavaḻi makiḻcciyum |eṉpatu--|iṭantalaippāṭṭiṉai yoṭṭi nikaḻum |iṉpiṉaip peṟṟavaḻi |akattut tōṟṟum perumakiḻvum. ``nīṅkiṉ teṟū|uṅ kuṟukuṅkāl taṇṇeṉṉun tīyāṇṭup peṟṟāḷ |ivaḷ.'' (kuṟaḷ-1104) ``|oṭuṅkī rōti |oṇṇutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ naṟuntaṇ nīra ḷāraṇaṅ kiṉaḷē |iṉaiyaḷ |āṉṟavaṭ puṉaiyaḷavu |aṟiyēṉ cilamel liyavē kiḷavi |aṉaimel liyalyā muyaṅkuṅ kālē.'' (kuṟun-70) |ivai puṇarcciyāṉ makaḻntataṟkuc ceyyuṭkaḷ. pirintavaḻik kalaṅkalāvatu--|ivvāṟu kūṭiṉa talaimakaḷ pirinta vaḻik kalakka muṟutalum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``|eṉṟum |iṉiya ḷā#āyiṉum pirital |eṉṟum |iṉṉāḷ |aṉṟē neñcam paṉimaruntu viḷaikkum parū|ukkaṇ |iḷamulaip paṭucāntu citaiya muyaṅkuñ ciṟukuṭik kāṉavaṉ perumaṭa makaḷē.'' |eṉavarum. |ittuṇaiyum |iṭantalaippāṭu. peṟṟavaḻi makiḻtalum pirintavaḻik kalaṅkalum pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉun tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉum nikaḻum. niṟpavai niṉai|i nikaḻpavai |uraippiṉum |eṉpatu--kāmanukarcci yoṉṟaṉaiyum niṉaiyātu |ivaḷālē namakku |illaṟam |iṉitu naṭakkuka meṉṟu |uṭkōṭalum. niṟpavai--|illaṟaviṉai. ``tērōṉ teṟukatir maḻuṅkiṉuṅ tiṅkaḷ tīrā vemmaiyoṭu ticainaṭukku |uṟuppiṉum peyarāp peṟṟiyil tiriyāc cīrkāl kulattil tiriyāk koḷkaiyuṅ koḷkaiyoṭu nalattiṉ tiriyā nāṭṭamum |uṭaiyōy kaṇṭata ṉaḷavaiyiṟ kalaṅkuti peyaṉiṉ|im maṇtiṇi kiṭakkai mānilam |uṇṭeṉak karuti |uṇaralaṉ yāṉē.'' |itu niṟpavai niṉai|ik kaḻaṟiyatu. ``|iṭikkuṭ kēḷir nuṅkuṟai yāka niṟukka lāṟṟiṉō naṉṟumaṟ ṟilla ñāyiṟu kāyum vevvaṟai maruṅkil kaiyil |ūmaṉ kaṇṇiṟ kākkum veṇṇey |uṇaṅkal pōlap parantaṉ ṟinnōy noṇṭukoḷaṟ karitē.'' (kuṟun-58) |itu nikaḻpavai |uraittatu, kuṟṟaṅkāṭṭiyāvāyilāvatu--talaivaṉ māṭṭuc cōrvāṉum kātal mikutiyāṉum nērvuṟṟa paḻipāvaṅkaḷai |eṭuttukāṭṭum pāṅkaṉ. peṭpiṉum--|attakaiya pāṅkaṉ |ivviyal paṇṭaipap pāl vaḻiyatu |eṉa |eṇṇi |ivvāṟu talaimakaṉ maṟuttavaḻi |ataṟ kuṭaṉpaṭal. |avvaḻi, niṉṉāṟ kāṇappaṭṭāḷ |evviṭattāḷ? |ettaṉmaiyāḷ? |eṉap pāṅkaṉ viṉāvutalum, |ataṟkut talaimakaṉ |iṭamum |uruvuṅmataṟku |avaḷ nilaimai kūṟalu mellām |uḷavām. |avvaḻip pāṅkaṉviṉātalum talaimakaṉ |uraittaṉavum |uḷavām. pāṅkaṉ viṉāyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. |avvaḻit talaimakaṉ |uraittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``|eluva ciṟā|ar |emuṟu naṇpa pulavar tōḻa kēḷāy |attai mākkaṭal naṭuvaṇ |eṇṇāṭ pakkattup pacuveṇ tiṅkaḷ tōṉṟi yāṅkuk katuppayal viḷaṅkun ciṟunutal putukkōḷ yāṉaiyiṟ piṇittaṟṟāl |emmē.'' (kuṟun-129) ``kaḻaipāṭu |iraṅkap palliyaṅ kaṟaṅka |āṭumakaḷ naṭanta koṭumpuri nōṉkayiṟṟu |atavat tīṅkaṉi |aṉṉa cemmukat tuyttalai manti vaṉpaṟaḻ tūṅkak kaḻaikkaṇ |irumpoṟai |ēṟi vicaitteḻuntu kuṟakkuṟu mākkaḷ tāḷaṅ koṭṭumak kuṉṟakat tatuvē koḻumiḷaic cīṟūr ciṟū rōḷē nāṟumayirk koṭicci koṭicci kaiyakattatu vēpiṟar viṭuttaṟ kākātu piṇitta|eṉ neñcē.'' (naṟṟiṇai-95) |iṉṉum |iyaṟkaip puṇarcci puṇarnta talaimakaṉ varainteytal vēṇṭik kūṟiṉavuṅ koḷka. ``mulaiyē mukiḻmukiḻt taṉavē talaiyē kiḷai|iya kuralē kiḻakkukavīḻn taṉavē ceṟinirai veṇpalum piṟamuṟai nirampiṉa cuṇaṅkuñ cilatōṉ ṟiṉavē |aṇaṅkutaṟku yāṉtaṉ |aṟivalē tāṉaṟi yalaḷē yāṅkā kuvaḷkol tāṉē perumutu celvaṉ |orumaṭa makaḷē.'' (kuṟun-337) [|itu pāṅkaṉ niṉṉai |aṇaṅkākkiyāḷ |evviṭattavaḷ |evviyaliṉaḷ |eṉṟu viṉāy |aṟintatu.] |ivvāṟu kēṭṭa pāṅkaṉ |avvaḻic ceṉṟu kaṇṭataṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``|iravi ṉāṉum |iṉtuyil |aṟiyātu |aravuṟu tuyaram |eytupa toṇṭit taṇṇaṟu neytaliṉa nāṟum piṉṉiruṅ kūntal |aṇaṅkuṟ ṟōrē'' (|aiṅ-175) |eṉa varum. |iccūttirattuḷ kūṟṟu varaiyaṟuttuṇarttāmai pāṅkaṟ kūṟṟum |aṭaṅkaṟkup pōlum. peṭṭa vāyilpeṟ ṟiravu vaḻiyuṟuppiṉum |eṉpatu--mēṟcol liyavāṟṟāṉ |uṭampaṭṭa pāṅkaṉāl talaimakaḷaip peṟṟup piṉṉum varainteyta lāṟṟātu kaḷaviṟ puṇarcci vēṇṭit tōḻiyai |irantu piṉniṉṟu kūṭṭak kūṭuvaṉ |eṉṉum |uḷḷattaṉāy |avvirattalai valiyuṟuttiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. valiyuṟuttalāvatu, tāṉ vaḻimoḻintatu yātu tāṉ |avvāṟu ceykuval |eṉṟamai. peṭṭavāyilāl talaimakaḷaik kaṇṭu kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``kaṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvarniṉ |aiyar |uṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvaimaṉ nīyum miṭalpuk kaṭaṅkāta vemmulaiyō pāram |iṭarpuk kiṭuku miṭaiyiḻaval kaṇṭāy.'' (cilap.kāṉal-17) |iṉṉum, peṭṭavāyil peṟṟu |eṉpataṟku |iraṭṭuṟa moḻital |eṉpataṉāl talaimakaḷ tāṉ virumpappaṭṭa tōḻiyāki |emakku vāyil nērvāḷ |ivaḷ |eṉappeṟṟup piṉṉiṉarantu kuṟaiyuṟa niṉaippiṉum |eṉṟumām. |ataṟku ceyyuḷ: ``talaippuṇaik koḷiṉē talaippuṇaik koḷḷum kaṭaippuṇaik koḷiṉē kaṭaippuṇai koḷḷum puṇaikai viṭṭup puṉalō ṭoḻukiṉ |āṇṭum varukuvaḷ pōlum māṇṭa mārip pittikattu nīrvār koḻumukaic cevverin |uṟaḻuṅ koḻuṅkaṭai maḻaikkaṇ tuḷitalait talai|iya taḷir |aṉ ṉōḷē.'' (kuṟun-222) |iravu valiyuṟuttaṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. ``koṇṭal māmaḻai kuṭakkērpu kuḻaitta ciṟukōl |iṇara peruntaṇ cāntam vakaicēr |aimpāl takaipeṟa vārip pularviṭat tutirtta tukaḷpaṭu kūḻaip peruṅkaṇ |āyam tukaḷpaṭu kūḻaip pantoṭu peyarum pirivi lāṭṭi |aruḷiṉum |aruḷā ḷāyiṉum peritaḻintu piṉṉilai muṉiyalmā neñcē |eṉṉatū |um |aruntuyar |avaln tīrkkum maruntupiṟi tillaiyāṉ |uṟṟa nōyttē'' (naṟṟiṇai-140) |eṉṉum pāṭṭum |ām. |ittuṇaiyum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam. |ūrum... ... pakutiyum |eṉpatu--|ūrāyiṉum pērāyiṉuṅ keṭutiyāyiṉum piṟavāyiṉum nīrmaiyiṉāl taṉ kuṟipput tōṉṟak kūṟit talaimakaṉ tōḻiyaik kuṟaiyuṟum pakutiyum |uṇṭu |eṉṟavāṟu. |avaṟṟuḷ |ūrviṉāyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``|aruvi |ārkkum peruvarai naṇṇic kaṉṟukāl yātta maṉṟap palaviṉ vērkkoṇṭu tūṅkuṅ koḻuñcuḷaip perumpaḻaṅ kuḻavic cētā mānti |ayalatu vēṇpayil |iṟumpiṉ |ām |aṟal parukum peruṅkal vēlic ciṟukuṭi yāteṉac colluvuñ collīr |āyiṟ kalleṉak karuvi māmaḻai vīḻnteṉa |eḻunta ceṅkēḻ |āṭiya ceḻuṅkuraṟ ciṟatiṉaik koypuṉaṅ kāvalum numatō kōṭēn talkul nīḷtō ḷīrē.'' (naṟṟiṇai-213) peyar viṉāyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. keṭutiviṉāyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``naraiparanta cāntam |aṟa|eṟintu nāḷāl |uṟaiyetirntu vittiya|ūḻ |ēṉal--piṟaiyetirnta tāmaraipōl vāḷmukattut tāḻkuḻalīr kāṇīrō |ēmarai pōntaṉa |īṇṭu.'' (tiṇaimālai-1) ``|illuṭaik kiḻamai yemmoṭu puṇaril tītum |uṇṭō māta rīrē'' |eṉṟatu piṟavāṟu viṉāyatu. piṟavumaṉṉa. tōḻi kuṟai...|iṭaṉumāruṇṭē |eṉpatu--tōḻi kuṟaiyait talaimakaḷaic cārtti meyyuṟak kūṟutalum, |amaiyā tirappiṉum maṟṟaiya vaḻiyum, collavaṭ cārttaliṟ pulliya vakaiyiṉum, |aṟintōḷ |ayarppiṉ |avvaḻi maruṅkiṟ kēṭum pīṭum kūṟutaliṉ nīkkaliṉākiya nilaimaiyum nōkki maṭalvā kūṟutalum |uṇṭu talaimakaṉkaṇ |eṉṟavāṟu. talaimakaṉkaṇ |eṉpatu |āṟṟalāṟ koḷḷakkiṭantatu. |ummaiyāṟpiṟakūṟutalumuṇṭēṉṟavāṟu. puṇarcci nimittamākat talaimakaṉ |irattaluṅ kuṟaiyuṟutalum maṭalēṟuval |eṉak kūṟutalum peṟumeṉṟavāṟu |īṇṭu, kuṟaiyavaṭ cārtti meyyuṟak kūṟal |eṉpatu--tōḻikūṟṟuḷ |arumaiyi ṉakaṟci yeṉṟu |otappaṭṭatu. taṇṭātiratta lāvatu--talaimakaṉ palakāluñceṉṟu |irattal. maṟṟaiyavaḻi |eṉpatu--piṉvaraveṉṟal mutalāyiṉa. collavaṭ cārttaliṟpulliyavakai |eṉpatu--muṉṉuṟu pupuṇarcci muṟainiṟut tuṇarttaleṉa |otappaṭṭatu. |aṟintōḷayarp peṉpatu--pētamai yūṭṭal |eṉa |otappaṭṭatu. kēṭu kūṟutalāvatu--|ulakuraittoḻippiṉum |eṉa |otappaṭṭatu. pīṭukūṟutalāvatu-perumaiyiṟ peyarppiṉum |eṉa |otappaṭṭatu. nīkkaliṉākiya nilaimai |eṉpatu--|añci |accuṟutta leṉa |otappaṭṭatu. |ivaiyellān tōḻi kūṟṟiṉuṭ kāṇppaṭum. tōḻiyaik kuṟaiyuṟum pakutivarumāṟu :- ``tōḷuṅ kūntalum palapā rāṭṭi vāḻtal |ollumō maṟṟē ceṅkōṟ kuṭṭuvaṉ toṇṭi |aṉṉa |eṟkaṇṭu mayaṅkinī nalkāk kālē'' (|ainkuṟu-178) |iṉi maṭalēṟuval |eṉpataṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``māveṉa maṭalum |ūrpa pūveṉak kuvimukiḻ |erukkaṅ kaṇṇiyuñ cūṭupa maṟukiṉ |ārkkavum paṭupa piṟitum |ākupa kāmaṅkāḻk koḷiṉē.'' (kuṟan-17) |avvaḻit talaimakaṉ kūṟiya coṟkēṭṭu, |iḵtu |aṟivum |aruḷum nāṇamum |uṭaiyār ceyyār |eṉak kūṟiyavaḻit talaimakaṉ kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``nāṇoṭu nallāṇmai paṇṭuṭaiyēṉ |iṉṟuṭaiyēṉ kāmuṟṟār |ēṟum maṭal'' (kuṟaḷ-1133) |eṉavum, |aṟikilā rellārum |eṉṟēyeṉ kāmam maṟukil maṟukum maruṇṭu.'' (kuṟaḷ-1159) |eṉavum varum. piṟavu maṉṉa. maṭalmā kūṟātu piṟa kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``piṇaittōṭ kuṟumakaḷ pāvai tai|iyum pañcāyp paḷḷañ cūḻntu maṟṟivaḷ |urutteḻu vaṉamulai |oḷipeṟa |eḻutiya toyyil kāppōr |aṟitalum |aṟiyār muṟaiyuṭai |aracaṉ ceṅkōl vaiyattu yāṉtaṟ kaṭaliṉ yāṅkā vatukol peritum pētai maṉṟa |aḷitō tāṉēyil |aḻuṅkal |ūrē.'' (kuṟun-279) |ivvāṟu |irantu piṉṉiṟṟalum maṭalēṟuval |eṉṟalum kaikkiḷai poruntiṇaip pāṟpaṭumō |eṉiṉ, |avvāṟu varuvaṉa |akattiṇaikkum puṟattiṇaikkum potuvāmāṟu varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ viḷaṅkum. (11)

{SA TP100i}

  1. paṇpiṟ peyarppiṉum parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum
  2. |aṉpuṟṟu nakiṉum |avaṭpeṟṟu maliyiṉum
  3. |āṟṟiṭai yuṟutalum |avviṉaik kiyalpē.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, |eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) mēl talaimakaṉ `maṭalmā kūṟum |iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē' |eṉṟār, |iḵtu |avaṉ maṭalvā kūṟutaṟku nimittamākiya nīkkattiṉai māṟupaṭṭuk kūṟāt talaimakaḷ |iyalpaik kūṟippeyarppiṉum, |aḵtaṟintu tām |uṭaṉpaṭat talaimakaṉ varuttattiṉāṉ melikiṉṟamai kūṟiya |iṭattiṉum, talaimakaṉ kuṟaiyai maṟuppuḻi |aṉpu tōṉṟa nakka |iṭattum, tōḻi |uṭaṉpāṭuṟṟavaḻiyum, talaimakaṉum mēṟ collappaṭṭa maṭalmākūṟutal |iṭaiyūṟupaṭutalum tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟku |iyalpu |eṉṟavāṟu. |ummai |iṟantatu taḻī|iya |eccavummai. paṇpiṟ peyarppiṉum--talaimakaḷ |iḷamaippaṇpu kūṟippeyarttavaḻit talaimakaṉ kūṟiyatu. |ataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ vaṇṭupaṭu kūntal taṇtaḻaik koṭicci vaḷaiyaḷ muḷaivāḷ |eyiṟṟaḷ |iḷaiya ḷāyiṉum |āraṇaṅ kiṉaḷē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.236) parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum--parinta vuḷḷattuṭaṉ melitaluṟutalum. parivuṟṟut tōḻi melitalāvatu `|uṭampaṭuvaḷiyāḷ' |eṉṟāṟpōla varuvatu. |avvaḻit talaimakaṉ kūṟṟu :- ``toṭalaik kuṟuntoṭi tantāḷ maṭaloṭu mālai |uḻakkun tuyar.'' (kuṟaḷ-1135) |aṉpuṟṟu nakiṉum--|aṉpu tōṉṟum |uḷḷattuṭaṉ nakkakālum kūṟṟu nikaḻum. |aṉpuṟṟu nakkavaḻit talaimakaṉ kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``nayaṉiṉ maiyiṟ payaṉitu |eṉṉātu pūmpoṟip polinta |aḻalumiḻ |akaṉpaip pāmpuyir |aṇarki yāṅkum |īṅkitu takā|atu vāḻiyō kuṟumakaḷ nakā |atu |uraimati yuṭaiyumeṉ |uḷḷañ cāral paccūṉ peyt pakaḻi pōlac cēyari paranta |āyiḻai maḻaikkaṇ |uṟā |a nōkka muṟṟavēṉ paital neñcam |uyyu māṟē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.75) |avaṭpeṟṟu maliyiṉum--tōḻi |uṭampapāṭṭiṉaip peṟṟu makiḻal. |iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāṉ talaimakaḷai |iruvakaik kuṟiyiṉum peṟṟu makiḻiṉum |eṉṟum koḷka. |utāraṇam :- ``|emakkunayan taruḷiṉai yāyiṟ paṇaittōḷ naṉṉutal |arivaiyoṭu melmela |iyali vantaciṉ vāḻiyō maṭantai toṇṭi |aṉṉaniṉ paṇpupala koṇṭē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 175) |itu |avaṭpeṟṟu maliyun talaivaṉ kūṟṟu. |iṉi, |uḷḷap puṇar/cciyā ṉiṉṟi yiyaṟkai yiṭaiyīṭupaṭṭuḻi, piṉ talaimakaḷ kuṟiyiṭaṅ kūṟiyavaḻi yataṉaip pāṅkaṟ kuraittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``|aṇaṅkuṭaip paṉittuṟait toṇṭi |aṉṉa maṇaṅkamaḻ poḻiṟkuṟi nalkiṉaḷ nuṇaṅkiḻaip poṅkari paranta |uṇkaṇ |aṅkaliḻ mēṉi |acaiyiyal |emakkē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 174) |eṉavuñ ciṟupāṉmai varum. ``kāṇiṟ kuvaḷai kaviḻntu nilaṉōkkum māṇiḻai kaṇ |ovvēm |eṉṟu'' (kuṟaḷ-1114) |itu talaiviyaip pakaṟkuṟikkaṇpeṟṟu malital. ``matiyum maṭantai mukaṉum |aṟiyā patiyiṟ kalaṅkiya mīṉ.'' (kuṟaḷ-1116) |iḵtu |iravukkuṟikkaṇ talaivaṉ |avaṭpeṟṟu malintatu. ``mātar mukampōl |oḷiviṭa vallaiyēl kātalai vāḻi mati.'' (kuṟaḷ-1118) |eṉapatum |atu. |āṟṟiṭai |uṟutalum--tāṉcēṟum |āṟṟiṭai |iṭaiyūṟu |uṇṭāy viṭattum kūṟṟu nikaḻum. |iraṭṭuṟa moḻivāṉ varaiviṭai vaittup pirintāṉ tāṉ cēṟum |āṟṟiṉkaṇ varuttamuṟṟuk kūṟalum koḷḷappaṭum. ``kuruti vēṭkai |urukeḻu vayamāṉ valimiku muṉpiṉ maḻakaḷiṟu pārkkum marampayil cōlai maliyap pūḻiyar |uruvat turuviṉ nāḷmēyal |ārum māri |eṇkiṉ malaiccura nīḷiṭai nī nayantu varutal |evaṉeṉap palapularntu |aḻutaṉai |uṟaiyum |ammā |arivai payaṅkeḻu palaviṉ kollik kuṭavaraip pūtam puṇartta putitiyal pāvai virikatir |iḷaveyil tōṉṟi |aṉṉaniṉ |a|āynalam |uḷḷi variṉemakku |ēmam |ākum malaimutal |āṟē.'' (naṟṟiṇai. 192) |in naṟṟiṇaippāṭṭu talaivi |āṟṟiṉatu |arumai ceppat talaivaṉ ceppiyatu. ``|ōmpuvāti vāḻiyō maṭai pāmpiṉ tūṅkutōl kaṭukkun tūveḷ ḷaruvik kalluyar |ārum muṉṟiṟ pulvēy kurampai nallōḷ |ūrē.'' (kuṟun: 235) /|ic kuṟuntokaip pāṭṭu talaivaṉ varaiviṭattuc cēṟuvāṉ kūṟiyatu.

{SA TP101i}

  1. pāṅkar nimittam paṉṉiraṇ ṭeṉpa.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, palavakai maṇattiṟ pāṅkarāyiṉār tuṇaiyākumiṭam |ittuṇai yeṉa varaiyaṟuttuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

pāṅkarāyiṉār tuṇaiyākak kūṭum kūṭṭam paṉṉiraṇṭu vakai yeṉṟavāṟu.

nimittam |eṉpatu nimittamākak kūṭum kūṭṭam. |akkūṭṭam nimittameṉa |ākupeyarāy niṉṟatu. pāṅkarāṟ kūṭṭam pāṅkar nimittameṉa vēṟṟumait tokaiyāyiṟṟu. |avaiyāvaṉa:- piramam mutaliya nāṉkum, kantiruvap pakutiyākiya kaḷavum, |uṭaṉpōkkum |ataṉkaṇ kaṟpiṉ pakutiyākiya |iṟkiḻattiyum, kāmakkiḻattiyum, kātaṟparattaiyum, |acuram mutalākiya mūṉṟumeṉa |ivai. (13)

{SA TP102i}

  1. muṉṉaiya mūṉṟum kaikkiḷaik kuṟippē.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, mēṟcollappaṭṭa paṉṉiruvakaiyiṉum kaikkiḷaip pāṟpaṭuvaṉa vakuttuṇar/ttutal nutaliṟṟu. |eṇvakaimaṇattiṉuḷḷum muṉṉaiyavākiya |acuram mutal mūṉṟum kaikkiḷaippāṟpaṭum |eṉṟavāṟu.

{SA TP103i}

  1. piṉṉar nāṉkum peruntiṇai peṟumē.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, mēṟcollappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ peruntiṇaikkuriyaṉa |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. |eṇvakai maṇattiṉuḷḷum piramam mutaliya nāṉkum peruntiṇaip pāṟpaṭum |eṉṟavāṟu. (15)

{SA TP104i}

  1. mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōr mēṉa
  2. tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉ |ainnilam peṟumē.

|eṉ--|eṉiṉ, mēṟcollappaṭṭa |oru talaikkāmamum poruntākkāmamu maṉṟi, |otta |aṉpiṉ varuṅ kūṭṭam |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) mutal |eṉpatu nilamum kālamum. nilattoṭum kālattoṭum poruntiya kantiruvar pāṟpaṭṭaṉa keṭutalillāta ciṟappiḷai yuṭaiya |aintuvakaippaṭum |eṉṟavāṟu. mutaloṭu puṇarnta |eṉṟārēṉum `vantatu kaṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal' (marapiyal-112) |eṉpataṉāl |oḻinta karupporuḷum |uripporuḷum koḷḷappaṭum. nilam |eṉpatu |iṭam. |itaṉāṟ colliyatu, |ottakāmamākik karupporuḷoṭum puṇarnta kantiruvaneṟi |iṭa vakaiyāṉ |aintuvakaippaṭum |eṉṟavāṟu. |avaiyāvaṉa: kaḷavum, |uṭaṉpōkkum, |iṟkiḻatti, kāmakkiḻatti, kātaṟparattaiyum |eṉac collappaṭṭa |aivakaik kūṭṭam. |iccollappaṭṭa paṉṉiruvakaippaṭṭa kūṭṭattiṟkum pāṅkarāyiṉār nimittamāka vēṇṭutaliṉ, |avaṟṟuḷ talaivaṟkum talaivikkum |otta kāta luḷvaḻip pāṅkarāyiṉārāl nikaḻum nikaḻcci kantiruvappakutiyākavum, |orutalai vēṭkaiyākiya vaḻi |ivarāl varum nikaḻcci kaikkiḷaiyākavum |oppilkūṭṭa mākiyavaḻip peruntiṇaiyākavum koḷka. |ainnilam |eṉpataṉai mullai kuṟiñci mutalāyiṉaveṉṟār |uḷarāleṉiṉ, `mutaloṭu puṇar/nta' |eṉapataṉāl nilampeṟu mātalāṉ nilam |eṉpataṟku - vēṟuporuḷ |uraittalvēṇṭu meṉka. |aḵtu |aṟṟāka, |iṟkiḻatti |eṉpār |uḷḷap puṇarcciyāṉātal pēyum |appāṟpaṭum. |ivai muṉṉaiya mūṉṟum kaikkiḷaiyāyavāṟu. ``kāmañ cālā |iḷamai yōḷvayiṟ'' (to.poru. 50) kaikkiḷai ciṟappuṭait teṉṟaṟkup pullit tōṉṟum |eṉakkūṟi, |itaṉai vāḷātu `kuṟippu' |eṉṟār. |āṇṭup piṟkālattaṉṟik kāṭcikkaṇ maṇam |ataṟkiṉmaiyiṉ |īṇṭu maṇam kūṟum vaḻik kūṟātu |akattiḷaiyiyaluṭ kūṟiṉār. (tol.kaḷa. 14. nacci.) teyyuṟupuṇarcciyāṉātal varaiyappaṭṭārākap poruṭpeṇṭirākiya kātaṟparattaiyar kūṭṭam |otta kāmamākiyavā ṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, |arum poruḷāṉātal, |accattāṉātal |aṉṟi |aṉpiṉāṟ kūṭutaliṉ |atuvuṅ kantiruvappapāṟpaṭum. |avvāṟaṉṟi |avaraip piṟitu neṟiyāṟ kūṭuvarāyiṉ |ivaṉmāṭṭut talaimai |iṉṟāmeṉpatu |uṇarntu koḷka. |aḵtāmāṟu. ``|aṉṉai kaṭuñcol |aṟiyātāy pōlanī |eṉṉaip pulappatu |oṟukkuvēṉ maṉyāṉ ciṟukālai |iṟkaṭai vantu kuṟiceyta |avvaḻi |eṉṟumyāṉ kāṇēṉ tiritā |evvaḻip paṭṭāy kamaṉāka |ivveḷḷal'' (kalit-90) |eṉavum, ``kaṇṭēṉiṉ māyaṅ kaḷavātal'' |eṉṉuṅ kaliyuḷ, ``..nōyum vaṭuvuṅ karantu makiḻcerukkip pāṭupeyal niṉṟa pāṉāḷ |iravil toṭipoli tōḷum mulaiyuṅ katuppum vaṭivār kuḻaiyum |iḻaiyum poṟaiyā |oṭivatu pōlum nucuppō ṭaṭitaḷarā vārāk kavaviṉ |oruttivan talkaltaṉ cīrār ñekiḻañ cilampac civantuniṉ pōrār katavam mititta tamaiyumō'' (kalit-97) |eṉavum, parattaiyar |aṉpiṉāṟ kūṟiyavāṟum |ivar |iṟkiḻattiyum kāmakkiḻattiyum |aṉmaiyum |aṟaṟintu koḷka. |ivvakai varuvaṉa |aintu nilaṉāy varum. |aḵtēl, marutakkaliyuḷ, ``......................... |aṭakkamil pōḻtiṉkaṇ tantai kāmuṟṟa toṭakkattut tāyuḻaip pukkāṉ'' (kalit-82) |eṉavum, `vaḻimuṟaittāy' |eṉavum, `putiyōḷ' |eṉavum, |ivvāṟu kūṟakkēṭkiṉṟa kāmakkiḻattiyu meṉa maṉaiviyar nālvaruḷar. |avarellāraiyum kūṟātu maṉaikkiḻattiyar |iruvar |eṉṟataṉāṟ payaṉ |iṉṟeṉiṉ, |avrellārum |iṟkiḻattiyum kāmakkiḻattiyu meṉa |iraṇṭu pakuppiṉuḷ |aṭaṅkupa. |aṉṟiyum, |ivar nālvarōṭu parattaiyuṭpaṭa |aivar kantiruvappakutiyar |eṉa |uraippiṉum |amaiyum. `parattai vāyil nālvarkkum |urittē' (poruḷiyal. 28) |eṉa |otutalāṉum, talaivaṟkup piramam mutalāka varum nāṉku varuṇattup peṇpālārum parattaiyum |eṉa |aivakaippaṭumeṉpatū |um |oṉ ṟeṉak koḷka. (16)

{SA TP105i}

  1. |iruvakaik kuṟipiḻaip pākiya |iṭattum
  2. kāṇā vakaiyiṟ poḻutunaṉi |ikappiṉum
  3. tāṉakam pukā|aṉ peyartal |iṉmaiyiṟ
  4. kāṭci yācaiyiṟ kaḷampukkuk kalaṅki
  5. vēṭkaiyiṉ mayaṅkik kaiyaṟu poḻutiṉum
  6. pukā|ak kālaip pukketirp paṭṭuḻip
  7. pakā|a viruntiṉ pakutik kaṇṇum
  8. vēḷāṇ |etirum viruppiṉ kaṇṇun
  9. tāḷāṇ |etirum pirivi ṉāṉum
  10. nāṇuneñ calaippa viṭuttaṟ kaṇṇum
  11. varaital vēṇṭit tōḻi ceppiya
  12. puraitīr kiḷavi pulliya |etirum
  13. varaivuṭam paṭutalum |āṅkataṉ puṟattum
  14. puraipaṭa vanta maṟuttalōṭu tokai|ik
  15. kiḻavōṉ mēṉa |eṉmaṉār pulavar.

|itu. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭiya talaimakaṉ varainteytuṅkāṟum kūṟum poruṇmai yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) |iruvakaik kuṟipiḻaippākiya |iṭattum |eṉpatu --pakaṟkuṟiyum |iraviṟkuṟiyum piḻaippākiya |iṭattum |eṉṟavāṟu. pakaṟkuṟi |iraviṟkuṟi yeṉpatu |eṟṟāṟpeṟutu meṉiṉ, ``kuṟiyeṉap paṭuva tiraviṉum pakaliṉum |aṟiyak kiḷanta |āṟṟa teṉpa'' (kaḷaviyal. 40) |eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. |akkuṟikkaṇ talaivi varavu piḻaittaviṭattut talaivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavavāṟu. ``maḻaivara vaṟiyā maññai yālum |aṭukkal nallūr |acainaṭaik koṭicci tāṉem maruḷāḷ |āyiṉum yāntaṉ |uḷḷupu maṟantaṟi yēmē.'' |itu, kuṟipiḻaittavaḻi tōḻikkuc colliyatu. ``|illōṉ |iṉpaṅ kāmuṟ ṟā|aṅku |arituvēṭ ṭaṉaiyāl neñcē kātali nallā ṉākutal |aṟiyā tōyē.'' (kuṟun.120) |itu, kuṟipiḻaittavaḻit tōḻikkuc colliyatu. kāṇā vakaiyiṟ poḻutunaṉi |ikappiṉum |eṉpatu--talaimakaḷaik kāṇāvakaiyiṟ poḻutu mikavum kaṭppiṉuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. ceyyuḷ: ``|uḷḷik kāṇpeṉ pōlval muḷḷeyiṟṟu |amiḻtal |ūṟuñ cevvāyk kamaḻakil |āram nāṟum |aṟalpōṟ kūntaṟ pēramar maḻaikkaṇ koṭicci mūral muṟuvaloṭu matai|iya nōkkē'' (kuṟun.186) |eṉa varum. tāṉakampukā|aṉ peyartal |iṉmaiyiṟ...poḻutiṉum |eṉpatu --kāṇāvakaiyiṟ poḻutu mikakkaḻin tuḻik kāṭci yākaiyiṉāṟ kuṟiyiṭattuc ceṉṟu |āṇṭuk kāṇātu kalaṅki vēṭkaiyāṉ mayakkamuṟṟuc ceyalaṟṟu niṟkuṅ kālattiṉuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. pukāṉ |eṉpatu muṟṟu vāypāṭṭāṉ vanta viṉaiyeccam. ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka. pukā|ak kālai pukketirp paṭṭuḻip...kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--tāṉ pukutaṟkut takutiyillāta kālattukkaṇ |akampuk ketirppaṭṭuḻi |avarāl nīkkappaṭāta viruntiṉ pakutiya ṉākiyavaḻiyum kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. ceyyuḷ: ``|iraṇṭaṟi kaḷaviṉnam kāta lōḷē muraṇkoḷ tuppiṟ cevvēl malaiyaṉ muḷḷūrk kāṉam naṇṇuṟa vantu naḷḷeṉ kaṅkul nammō raṉṉaḷ kūntal vēynta viravumalar |eṇṇey nīvi |amarā mukatta ḷākit tamarōr |aṉṉaḷ vaikaṟai yāṉē.'' (kuṟun.312) |eṉa varum. vēḷā ṇetirum viruppiṉ kaṇṇum--|eṉpatu talaivi |upakāram |etirppaṭṭa viruppiṉ kaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |atu kuṟivaḻikkaṇṭu kūṟutal. |avvaḻit talaivikkuk kūṟiya ceyyuḷ: ``cilampukamaḻ kāntaḷ naṟuṅkulai yaṉṉa nalpeṟu kaiyiṉeṉ kaṇputait tōyē pāyal |iṉtuṇai yākiya paṇaittōḷ tōkai māṭciya maṭantai nīyala tuḷarō|eṉ neñcamarn tōrē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.193) /|itu talaivi kaṇputaittavaḻit talaivaṉ kūṟiyatu. ``kuruti vēṭkai |urukeḻu vayamāṉ valimiku muṉpiṉ maḻakaḷiṟu pārkkum marampayil cōlai maliyap pūḻiyar |uruvat turuviṉ nāḷmēyal |ārum. māri |eṇkiṉ malaiccura nīḷiṭai nīnayantu varutal |evaṉeṉap palapulantu |aḻutaṉai |uraiyum |ammā |arivai payaṅkoḻu palaviṉ kollik kuṭavaraip pūtam puṇarnta putitiyal pāvai vitikatir |iḷaveyil tōṉṟi yaṉṉaniṉ |emma |ākum malaimuta lāṟē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.192) |eṉavum varum. tāḷāṇ |etirum pirivi ṉāṉum |eṉpatu--tāḷāṇmai |etirum pariviṉ kaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē neṭṭāṟu cēṟalaṉṟi |aṇi maikkaṇ piriveṉṟu koḷka. ``|iṉṟē ceṉṟu varuvatu nāḷaik kuṉṟiḻi |aruviyiṉ veṇṭēr muṭuka |iḷampiṟai yaṉṉa viḷaṅkucuṭar nēmi vicumpuvīcu koḷḷiyiṟ paimpayir tumiyak kāliyaṟ celaviṉ mālai yeytic ciṉṉirai vālvaḷaik kuṟumakaḷ paṉmāṇ |ām |aṭaintuvak kuvamē.'' (kuṟun.189) pirintavaḻik kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``|ompumati vāḻiyō vāṭai pāmpiṉ tūṅkutōl kaṭukkun tūveḷ |aruvik kalluyar naṇṇi yatuvē nelli maraiyiṉam |ārum muṉṟil pulvēy kurampai nallōḷ |ūrē.'' (kuṟun.235) |eṉavum varum. nāṇuneñ calaippa viṭuttaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu. nāṇan talaivi neñciṉai varuttutalāṉē nīkki niṟuttutaṟkaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtu, |alarākum |eṉṟañci nīkkutal. |avvaḻit talaivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |avvaḻi |ivvāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. puṉainturaiyeṉṟu karutik kūṟutalum meyyeṉṟu karutik kūṟutalum |uḷavām. ``kaḷittoṟuṅ kaḷḷuṇṭal vēṭṭaṟṟāṟ kāmam veḷippaṭun tōṟum |iṉitu'' (kuṟaḷ.1045) |itu puṉainturaiyeṉṟu karutik kūṟiyatu. ``|uṟā |atu |ūraṟi kauvai |ataṉaip peṟā |atu peṟṟaṉṉa nīrttu.'' (kuṟaḷ.1145) `|ūraṟinta kauvai naṉṟē kāṇ; |ataṉaik kuṟṟamākak koḷḷātu peṟātu peṟṟa nīrmaittākak koḷ' |eṉṟamaiyāṉun tamarvaraivuṭaṉ paṭuvar |eṉak kūṟiyavāṟām. |itu meyyākak koṇṭu kūṟiyatu. varaital vēṇṭi...pulliya |etirum |eṉpatu--varaintu kōṭal vēṇṭit tōḻiyāṟ collappaṭṭa kuṟṟantīrnta kiḷavi poruntiya |etirppāṭṭuk kaṇṇuṅkūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``nallurai |ikantu pullurait tā |ay peyalnīk kēṟṟa pacuṅkalam pōla |uḷḷam tāṅkā veḷḷam nīnti |aritavā vuṟṟaṉai neñcē naṉṟum peritāl |ammaniṉ pūcal |uyarkōṭṭu makavuṭai manti pōla |akaṉuṟat taḻī|ic kēṭkunarp peṟiṉē.'' (kuṟun.29) |eṉavarum varaivuṭampaṭutalum--tōḻikūṟiya coṟkēṭṭu varaivuṭampaṭutaṟ kaṇṇum kūṟṟuṟa nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``|ārkaḷiṟu mititta nīrtikaḻ cilampiṟ cūrnacain taṉaiyaiyāy naṭuṅkal kaṇṭē naranta nāṟuṅ kuvaiyiruṅ kūntal nirantilaṅku veṇpal maṭantai parittaṉeṉ |allaṉō |iṟaiyiṟai yāṉē.'' (kuṟun.52) |eṉa varum. |āṅkataṉ puṟattum |eṉpatu--|av varaivunikaḻccik kaṇṇum kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka. puraipaṭavanta maṟuttaloṭu tokai|i |eṉpatu--kuṟṟampaṭa vanta maṟuttaloṭukūṭa |eṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtu |avarmaṟuttaṟ kaṇṇun talaimakaṉmāṭṭuk kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |ataṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``poṉ |aṭarn taṉṉa |oḷḷiṇarc ceruntip paṉmalar vēynta nalampeṟu kōtaiyuḷ tiṇimaṇal |aṭaikarai |alavaṉ |āṭṭi |acaiyiṉa ḷirunta |āypoṭik kuṟumakaḷ nalañcāl viḻupporuḷ kalaniṟai koṭuppiṉum peṟalaruṅ kuraiya ḷāyiṉ |aṟanterintu nāmuṟai tē|e marū|uppeyarn tavaṉoṭu |irunīrc kuṭṭam puṇaiyoṭu pukkum paṭuttaṉam paṇintaṉam |aṭuttaṉam |iruppil tarukuvaṉ kollō tāṉē viritiraik kaṇtiraḷ muttaṅ koṇṭu ñāṅkart tēṉ|imir |akaṉkaraip pakukkuṅ kāṉalam peruntuṟaip paratavaṉ namakkē.'' (|akam.280) |eṉa varum. kaḻivōṉ mēṉa |eṉmaṉār pulavar |eṉpatu--|ic collappaṭṭaṉa vellāṅ kiḻavōṉ |iṭattaṉa |eṉṟavāṟu. kūṟṟeṉṉātu potuppaṭak kūṟutalāṉ |uḷḷa nikaḻcciyum kūṟṟum koḷḷappaṭum.

{SA TP106i}

  1. kāmat tiṇaiyiṟ kaṇniṉṟu varū|um
  2. nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇmaiya |ākaliṉ
  3. kuṟippiṉum |iṭattiṉum |allatu vēṭkai
  4. neṟippaṭa vārā |avaḷvayiṉ |āṉa.

|eṉpatu mēl talaivaṟ kuriya kiḷavikūṟi, |iṉit talaivikkuriya kiḷavi kūṟukiṉṟārākaliṉ, kāmavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ kuṟippiṉāṉum |iṭattiṉāṉumallatu vēṭkai pulappaṭa nikaḻātu talaiviyiṭattu |eṉṟavāṟu.

kāmattiṇai |eṉpataṉaik kuṟippeṉpataṟku muṉṉē kūṭṭi yuraikka |accamum |iyalpaṉṟōveḷiṉ, |atuvum vēṭkaikkuṟipṉāṉ nīṅkumeṉpa, |accamuḷvaḻi vēṭkai nikaḻāmaiyiṉ. vēṭkai yuḷvaḻi nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkāvō |eṉiṉ, |atu varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa. |itaṉāṟ colliyatu talaivi talaivaṉai |etirppaṭṭu muṉṉilaiyākkal mutalākat talaivaṉmāṭṭu nikaḻntamaipōlat talaivimāṭṭu nikaḻpavai |uḷavō veṉiṉ, |avaḷmāṭṭuk kuṟippiṉāṉātal, collutaṟkut takka viṭattiṉāṉātal tōṟṟuvatallatu, pulappaṭṭu nikaḻā teṉṟavā ṟāyiṟṟu. ``|uṇṭārkaṇ |alla taṭunaṟāk kāmampōṟ kaṇṭār makiḻceytal |iṉṟu,'' (kuṟaḷ.1090) |eṉṟatu talaivaṉaik kaṇṭa talaivi vēṭkaik kuṟippiṉāl taṉṉuḷḷē karutiyatu. |iṭampaṟṟi vēṭkai tōṟṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``......................... neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭakkum nāvāy tōṉṟuva kāṇā mōveṉak kāviṟ citaiyā nillātu peyarnta pallō ruḷḷum |eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu naṉṉutāl |oḻikō yāṉeṉa |aḻitakak kūṟi yāṉpeyarka |eṉṉa nōkkit tāṉtaṉ neṭuntērk koṭiñci paṟṟi niṉṟōṉ pōlum |iṉṟum|eṉ kaṭkē.'' (|akam.110) |eṉat taṉ kuṟippuk kālattāṟ kūṟuta lāṟṟātu piṉ |iṭam peṟṟuḻik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.

{SA TP107i}

  1. kāmañ collā nāṭṭam |iṉmaiyiṉ
  2. |ēmuṟa |iraṇṭum |uḷaveṉa moḻipa

|eṉpatu, mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭaiyuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. vēṭkaiyuraiyāta kaṇ |ulakattiṉmaiyāl talaivaṉ |ēmuṟaṟ poruṭṭu nāṇum maṭaṉum |uḷavām |eṉṟavāṟu.

|itaṉāṟ colliyatu mēl talaivikku |iyalpāyk kūṟappaṭṭa |accamum nāṇum maṭaṉum |eṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷ vēṭkaiyāl |accam nīṅkiṉavaḻi nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkāvō |eṉṟaiyuṟṟārkku |avai talaimakaṟku |ēma mātaṟ poruṭṭu nīṅkāvām |eṉpatū |um, vēṭkaikkuṟippuk kaṇṇiṉāṉ |aṟiyalā meṉpatū|um |uṇarttiyavāṟu. |eṉṉai? nāṇum maṭaṉum |illātārait talaimakkaḷ |avamatippārātalāl. |utāraṇam:- ``kaṭalaṉṉa kāmam |uḻantum maṭalēṟāp peṇṇiṟ peruntakka til.'' (kuṟaḷ.1173) |itaṉuḷ `kaṭalaṉṉakākakamam |uḻantum' |eṉṟataṉāṉ vēṭkai mikka nilaiyiṉaiyum, maṭalēṟāp peṇ' |eṉṟamaiyāṉ nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkā nilaiyiṉaiyum kūṟutal kāṇka. ``kaṇṇoṭu kaṇ|iṇai nōkkokkiṉ vāyccoṟkaḷ |eṉṉa payaṉum |ila.'' (kuṟaḷ.1100) |itaṉuḷ |akattunikaḻ vēṭkaiyiṉaik kaṇṇiṉāl |aṟiyakkiṭantamai kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. (19)

{SA TP108i}

  1. colletir moḻital |arumait tākaliṉ
  2. |alla kūṟṟumoḻi |avaḷvaḷayi ṉāṉa

|eṉpatu, |ituvum talaivimāṭṭu |oru kūṟṟuccol nikaḻumāṟu |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

talaivaṉ |iyaṟkaippuṇarcci karutikkūṟuñ colletir tāṉ vēṭkaik kuṟippiṉaḷāyiṉum |ataṟkuṭampaṭṭa neṟiyaik kūṟutal |arumaiyuṭait tātalāṉ |ataṟku |uṭampāṭallāta kūṟṟumoḻi talaiviyiṭattaṉa |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉṟatu |icaivillātāraippōlak kūṟutal.

|utāraṇam:- ``yārivaṉ |eṉṉai vilakkuvāṉ nīruḷar pūntā maraippōtu tanta viravuttārk kallāp potuvaṉai nīmāṟu niṉṉoṭu collalōm peṉṟār |emar.'' (kalit.112) |eṉavarum. |itaṉpiṉ, ``...................... |evaṉ kolō māyap potuvaṉ |uraitta |uraiyellām vāyāva tāyiṉ talaippaṭṭām poyyāyiṉ cāyaliṉ mārpiṟ kamaḻtār kuḻaittaniṉ |āyita muṇkaṇ pacappat taṭameṉtoḷ cāyiṉum |e|ēr |uṭaittu.'' (kalit.112) |eṉa |uṭampāṭu kūṟiṉāḷātaliṉ muṟkūṟiyatu |allakuṟṟāyiṟṟu. (20)

{SA TP109i}

  1. maṟaintavaṟ kāṇṭal taṟkāṭ ṭuṟutal
  2. niṟainta kātalil colletir maḻuṅkal
  3. vaḻipāṭu maṟuttal maṟuttetir kōṭal
  4. paḻitīr muṟuval ciṟutē tōṟṟam
  5. kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiṉum nāṇumika variṉum
  6. |iṭṭuppiri viraṅkiṉum |arumaicey tayarppiṉum
  7. vantavaḻi |eḷḷiṉum viṭṭuyirt taḻuṅkiṉum
  8. nontuteḷi voḻippiṉum |accam nīṭiṉum
  9. pirintavaḻik kalaṅkiṉum peṟṟavaḻi maliyiṉum
  10. varuntoḻiṟ karumai vāyil kūṟiṉum
  11. kūṟiya vāyil koḷḷāk kālaiyum
  12. maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkic citaintavaḻit tōḻikku
  13. niṉaittal cāṉṟa |arumaṟai |uyirttalum
  14. |uyirāk kālattu |uyirttalum |uyircela
  15. vēṟṟuvaraivu variṉ|atu māṟṟutaṟ kaṇṇum
  16. neṟippaṭu nāṭṭattu nikaḻntavai maṟaippiṉum
  17. poṟiyiṉ yātta puṇarcci nōkki
  18. |orumaik kēṇmaiyiṉ |uṟukuṟai teḷintōḷ
  19. |arumai cāṉṟa nāliraṇṭu vakaiyiṟ
  20. perumai cāṉṟa |iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum
  21. poytalai |aṭutta maṭaliṉ kaṇṇuṅ
  22. kaiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīr tuṭaippiṉum
  23. veṟiyāṭ ṭiṭattu veruviṉ kaṇṇum
  24. kuṟiyiṉ |oppumai maruṭaṟ kaṇṇum
  25. varaivutalai variṉum kaḷavaṟi vuṟiṉum
  26. tamartaṟ kātta kāraṇa maruṅkiṉum
  27. taṉkuṟi taḷḷiya teruḷāk kālai
  28. vantavaṉ peyarnta vaṟuṅkaḷam nōkkit
  29. taṉpiḻaip pākat taḻī |it tēṟalum
  30. vaḻuviṉṟu nilai|iya |iyaṟpaṭu poruḷiṉum
  31. poḻutum |āṟum puraiva taṉmaiyiṉ
  32. |aḻivutalai vanta cintaik kaṇṇum
  33. kāmañ ciṟappiṉum |avaṉaḷi ciṟappiṉum
  34. |ēmañ cāṉṟa vuvakaik kaṇṇum
  35. taṉvayiṉ |urimaiyum |avaṉvayiṟ parattaiyum
  36. |aṉṉavu muḷavē |ōriṭat tāṉa.

|eṉṟatu, talaivikku |iyaṟkaippuṇarcci mutalākak kaḷaviṉkaṭ `kuṟippiṉu miṭattiṉumallatu' (kaḷaviyal-18) nikaḻcci yellāvaṟṟiṉum kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭam |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) maṟaintavaṟ kāṇṭal |eṉpatu--taṉṉait talaivaṉ kāṇāmal tāṉ |avaṉaik kāṇuṅ kāṭci. taṟkāṭṭuṟutal |eṉpatu--taṉṉai |avaṉ kāṇumāṟu niṟṟal niṟainta...maḻuṅkal |eṉpatu--nirampiya vēṭkaiyāl talaivaṉ kūṟiya coṟ kēṭṭu |etirmoḻi kūṟātu maṭintu niṟṟal. |im mūṉṟiṭattiṉuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻātu. vaḻipāṭu maṟuttal |eṉpatu--|ataṉpiṉ |ivaḷ vēṭkaik kuṟippuk kaṇṭu cāraluṟṟavaḻi |ataṟkuka |uṭampaṭātu maṟuttal |atu kuṟippiṉāṉum kūṟṟaṉāṉum varum. maṟuttetirkōṭal |eṉpatu--maṟuttāṅku maṟātu piṉṉum |ēṟṟukkōṭal. paḻitīr...tōṟṟal--kuṟṟantīrnta muṟuvalciṟitu tōṟṟuvittal. |atu puṇartaṟku |uṭaṉpāṭu kāṭṭi niṟkum. |ivai |āṟunilaiyum puṇarccikku muṉ nikaḻum. |īṇṭuṅ kuṟippunikaḻcciyallatu kūṟṟu nikaḻcci |arukiyallatu vārātu. |avaṟṟuḷ cilavarumāṟu: ``|ikalvēntaṉ cēṉai'' |eṉṉum mullaikkaliyuḷ, ``māmaruṇ ṭaṉṉa maḻaikkaṇciṟ ṟāyttiyar nīmaruṭṭuñ coṟkaṇ maruḷvārk kuraiyavai yāmuṉiyā |ēṟupōl vaikaṟ patiṉmaraik kāmuṟṟuc celvāyōr kaṭkuttik kaḷvaṉai nī|evaṉ ceyti piṟarkku; yām|evaṉ ceytum niṉakku; |itu vaḻipāṭu maṟuttatu. |iṉṉum |itaṉuḷ, ``tēṅkoḷ poruppaṉ ciṟukuṭi |em|āyar vēntūṭṭu |aravattu niṉpeṇṭir kāṇāmal kāñcittā tukkaṉṉa tāteru maṉṟattut tūṅkuṅ kuravaiyuḷ niṉpeṇṭir kēḷāmai |āmpaṟ kuḻalāṟ payirpayir |empaṭappaik kāñcikkīḻc ceytēṅ kuṟi.'' (kalit.108) |itu maṟuttetir kōṭal. paḻitīr muṟuval ciṟitē tōṟṟaṟku |utāraṇam: ``|aṉṉaiyō, maṉṟattuk kaṇṭāṅku cāṉṟār makaḷirai |iṉṟi |amaiyēṉeṉ ṟiṉṉavuñ colluvāy niṉṟāynī ceṉṟī |emarkāṇpar nāḷaiyuṅ kaṉṟoṭu cērum pulantu'' (kalit.110) |itaṉuḷ ``|aṉṉaiyō'' |eṉpatu nakaiyoṭu kūṭiyacol. kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ kaiyakappaṭṭapiṉpu |eṉceytē māyiṉēm |eṉak kalakkamuṟiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. nāṇumikavariṉum |eṉpatu--talaivikku muṉpuḷḷa nāṇattiṉum mika nāṇam vantuḻiyum |eṉṟavāṟu. |iṭṭuppiri viraṅkiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ |iṭṭu vaittup pirivaṉ |eṉa |añciyataṟku |irakkamuṟiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. |arumaicey tayarppiṉum |eṉpatu talaivaṉaṉ varutaṟkuk kāvalākiya |arumai ceytataṉāl |avaṉum varutalaittaviriṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. varutalait tavirtalai |ayarppu |eṉaṟār. |aṉṟiyum puṟattu viḷaiyāṭutaṟku |arumai ceyya mayakkamvariṉum |eṉṟumām. ceyteṉpataṉaic ceyaveṉat tirikka. vantavaḻi |eḷḷiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ vantaviṭattu |alarāku meṉṟañci |ikaḻntavaḻiyum |eṉṟavāṟu. viṭṭuyirttaḻuṅkiṉum |eṉpatu--maṟaiyātu colli |iraṅkiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. nontu teḷivoḻippiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ teḷivitta teḷivai nontu, |ataṉai yoḻippiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. |accam nīṭiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ varukiṉṟatu |iṭaiyīṭāka |accam mikkuḻiyum |eṉṟavāṟu. pirintavaḻik kalaṅkiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ pirintavaḻik kalakka muṟiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. |atu tāḷāṇetirum pirivu. peṟṟavaḻi maliyiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉōṭu kūṭṭam peṟṟavaḻi makiḻiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. varuntoḻiṟku |arumai vāyil kūṟiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉvarutaṟku |iṭaiyīṭākak kāvalar kaṭukutalāṉ |īṇṭuvarutal |ariteṉattōḻi talaivikkuc colliṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. kūṟiya...kālaiyum |eṉpatu--tōḻi |ivvāṟu kūṟiyataṉai maṉaṅkoḷḷāta kālattiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. maṉaippaṭṭu |arumaṟai yuyirttalum |eṉpatu--puṟattu viḷaiyāṭutal |oḻintu maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkic citaintavaḻit tōḻikkuc cūḻtalamainta |ariya maṟaipporuḷaic collum |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē citaiyātavaḻit tōḻikkuc collāḷām collāḷām |eṉpatu pōntatu. vēṭkai maṟaikkappaṭutaliṉ maṟaiyāyiṟṟu. `kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkal' mutalākak `kūṟiya vāyil koḷḷākkālai' yiṟākac collappaṭṭa paṉṉiruvakaiyiṉum talaimakaḷ tōḻikku |uraikkappeṟum. |aḵtu |uraikkuṅkāl maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅki mēṉi citainta vaḻiyē |uraikkappeṟuvatu. |āṇṭum |itaṟku |eṉceyvām |eṉa |ucāvutalōṭukūṭat taṉatu kātaṉmai tōṉṟa |uraikkum |eṉṟavāṟu. maṉaippaṭākkāl |avaṉaik kāṇṭalāl |uraikkavēṇṭuyavatillai yeṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. |ippaṉṉiraṇṭum |oruttimāṭṭu |oruṅku nikaḻvaṉa |alla. |ivviṭaṅkaḷ |uraittaṟku |iṭameṉa |ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyavāṟu. |avaṟṟuṭ kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- `koṭiyavuṅ kōṭṭavum' |eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcikkaliyuḷ, ``narantanā ṟirunkūntal |eñcātu naṉipaṟṟip polampuṉai makaravāy nuṅkiya cikaḻikai nalampeṟac cuṟṟiya kural |amai |orukāḻ viraṉmuṟai cuṟṟi mōkkalum mōntaṉaṉ naṟā |aviḻn taṉṉa |eṉ melviraṟ pōtukoṇṭu ceṟā |ac ceṅkaṇ putaiya vaittup paṟā |ak kurukiṉ |uyirttalum |uyirttaṉaṉ toyyil |iḷamulai |iṉiya taivantu toyyalan taṭakkaiyiṉ vīḻpiṭi |aḷikkum maiyal yāṉaiyiṉ maruṭṭalum maruṭṭiṉaṉ; |ataṉāl, |allal kaḷaintaṉaṉ tōḻi nannakar |aruṅkaṭi nīvāmai kūṟi ṉaṉṟeṉa niṉṉoṭu cūḻval tōḻi nayampurin tiṉṉatu ceytāṉ |ivaḷeṉa maṉṉā |ulakattu maṉṉuvatu puraimē.'' (kalit.54) |itaṉuṭ kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiyavāṟum |arumaṟai |uyirttavāṟum |ivvāṟu ceyyākkāl |iṟantapaṭuvaṉ |eṉṉuṅa kuṟippiṉaḷāy `maṉṉāvulakattu maṉṉuvatu puraiyum' |eṉavuṅ kūṟivāṟu kāṇka. nāṇumikavantataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``nōkkuṅkāl nōkkit toḻū|umpiṟar kāṇpār tūkkili tūṟṟum paḻiyeṉak kaivittup pōkkuṅkāṟ pōkkum niṉaintirukkum maṟṟunām karkakum |iṭamaṉ ṟiṉi; |ellā |evaṉceyvām nām.'' (kalit.63) |itu nāṇam mikkavaḻit tōḻiyoṭu |ucāviyatu. |iṭṭuppiriviraṅkiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``|amma vāḻi tōḻi kātalar pāvai yaṉṉa|eṉ |āykaviṉ tolaiya nalmā mēṉi pacappac celvēm |eṉpatam malaikeḻu nāṭṭē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 221) |eṉavarum. vantavaḻi yeḷḷiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``kaṇtiraḷ muttam mayakkum |iruṇmunnīrp paṇṭaṅkoḷ nāvāy vaḻaṅkun tuṟaivaṉai muṇṭakak kāṉaluṭ kaṇṭēṉ |eṉatteḷintēṉ niṉṟa |uṇarvilā tēṉ.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.56) |itaṉuḷ `piṉṉum varuvaṉ |eṉṟiruntēṉ; |ataṉāṉ |eḷḷiṉēṉ' |eṉpatu karuttu. ``......................... |ēṟiraṅ kiruḷiṭai |iraviṉiṟ patampeṟā |aṉ māṟiṉeṉ |eṉak kūṟi maṉaṅkoḷḷun tāṉeṉpa kūṭutal vēṭkaiyāṟ kuṟipārttuk kuralnoccip pāṭōrkkuñ ceviyoṭu paitalēṉ yāṉāka.'' (kalit. 46) |iḵtu |eḷḷiṉāyeṉa niṉaittāṉ |eṉṟavaḻik kūṟiyatu. viṭṭuyir taḻuṅkiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``piṇiniṟan tīrntu perumpaṇaittōḷ vīṅka maṇimalai nāṭaṉ varuvaṉkol tōḻi kaṇiniṟa vēṅkai malarntuvaṇ ṭārkkum maṇiniṟa mālaip poḻutu.'' (tiṇaimoḻi.9) |eṉavum, ``maraiyā |ukaḷum marampayil cōlai |uraicār maṭamanti |oṭi |ukaḷum puraitīr malaināṭaṉ pūṇēn takalam |uraiyā |uḻakku|eṉ neñcu.'' (kainnilai.6) |eṉavum varum. nontu teḷivoḻittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``maṉṟat tuṟukaṟ karuṅkaṇ mucu|ukaḷuṅ kuṉṟaka nāṭaṉ teḷitta teḷiviṉai naṉṟeṉṟu tēṟit teḷintēṉ talaiyaḷi |oṉṟumaṟ ṟoṉṟum |aṉaittu.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.9) |eṉavarum. |accam nīṭiṉum |eṉpataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``meṉtiṉai mēynta taṟukaṭ paṉṟi vaṉkal |aṭukkattut tuñcu nāṭaṉ |entai yaṟital |añcikkol |atuvē maṉṟa vārā maiyē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.261) |eṉavum, ``maṉṟa marā|atta pē|emutir kaṭavuḷ koṭiyōrt teṟū|um |eṉpa yāvatum koṭiyar |allar|uṅ kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭar pacai|ip pacantaṉṟu nutalē ñekiḻiya ñekiḻntaṉṟu taṭameṉ tōḷē.'' (kuṟun. 87) |eṉavum varum. pirintavaḻik kalaṅkiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``varuvatu kollō tāṉē vārātu |avaṇuṟai mēvaliṉ |amaivatu kollō puṉavar koḷḷiyiṟ pukalvaru maññai |iruvi |irunta kuruvi veruvuṟap pantāṭu makaḷiriṟ paṭartaruṅ kuṉṟukoḻu nāṭaṉoṭu ceṉṟa|eṉ neñcē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.3) |eṉavum, ``|atukol tōḻi kāma nōyē vatikuru kuṟaṅku miṉṉalaip puṉṉai |uṭaitirait tivalai |arumpun tīnīr mellam pulampaṉ pirinteṉap pallitaḻ |uṇkaṇ pāṭol lāvē.'' (kuṟun.5) |eṉavum, ``maṇiniṟa neytal |iruṅkaḻic cērppaṉ |aṇinalam |uṇṭakaṉṟāṉ |eṉṟuko lempōl tiṇimaṇa lekkarmēl |otam peyarntu tuṇimunnīr tuñcā tatu.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.60) |eṉavum, ``neñcattār kāta lavarāka veytuṇṭal |añcutum vēpāk kaṟintu.'' (kuṟaḷ. 1128) |eṉavum, kaṇṇuḷḷār kāta lavarākak kaṇṇum kaḻutēm karappāk kaṟintu.'' (kuṟaḷ.1127) |eṉavum varum. |uṟaṅkāmaiyum |uṇṇāmaiyum kōlañ ceyyāmaiyum varuttam piṟavuñ collutal. |ivvaḻi nī varuntāti niṉmāṭṭu |aṉpu perituṭaiyāṉ |eṉat tōḻi |āṟṟuvittavaḻi |āṟṟāmaiyāṟ kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``ciṟutiṉai mēynta taṟukaṭ paṉṟi tuṟukal |aṭukkattut tuṇaiyoṭu vatiyum |ilaṅkumalai nāṭaṉ varū|um maruntum |aṟiyuṅkol tōḻiyavaṉ viruppē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.262) |eṉa varum.

peṟṟavaḻi maliyiṉu meṉpataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``|amma vāḻi tōḻi paṉmāṇ nuṇmaṇal |aṭaikarai nammōṭāṭiya taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉ maṟaii |aṉṉai |aruṅkaṭi vantunṉ ṟōṉē" (|aiṅkuṟu.115) |eṉavum "muḷaivaḷar mutala" |eṉṉum |akappāṭṭiṉuḷ, "........... vēṭṭōrkku |amiḻtat taṉṉa kamaḻtār mārpiṉ vaṇṭiṭaip paṭā|a muyakkamun taṇṭāk kātalum talaināḷ pōṉmē" (|akam. 332) |eṉavum "peyalkaṇ maṟaittaliṉ vicumpukā ṇalarē nīrparan toḻukaliṉ nilaṅkā ṇalarē |ellai cēṟaliṉ iruḷperitu paṭṭaṉṟu pallōr tuñcum pāṉāṭ kaṅkul yāṅkuvan taṉaiyō ōṅkal veṟpa vēṅkai kamaḻum em ciṟukuṭi yāṅkaṟintaṉaiyō nōkō yāṉē. " (kuṟun. 355) |eṉavum |amma vāḻi tōḻi nalamika nalla vāyiṉa |aḷiyameṉ tōḷkaḷ mallal |iruṅkaḻi malarum mellam pulampaṉ vanta vāṟē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.120) |eṉavum varum.

varuntoḻiṟ karumai vāyilkūṟiyavaḻit talaivi kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``|aruṅkaṭi |aṉṉai kāval nīvip peruṅkaṭai |iṟantu maṉṟam pōkip pakalē palaruṅ kāṇa vāyviṭṭu |akalvayaṟ paṭappai |avaṉūr viṉavic ceṉmō vāḻi tōḻi paṉṉāḷ karuvi |ārkkum |ayantikaḻ cilampiṉ vāṉtōy māmalaik kiḻavaṉaic cāṉṟōy |allai |eṉṟaṉam varaṟkē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.365) |eṉavarum.

kūṟiyavāyil koḷḷākkālattut talaiviyuraittaṟkucceyyuḷ :- ``kalvarai yēṟik kaṭuvaṉ kaṉivāḻai |elluṟu pōḻtiṉ |iṉiya paḻaṅkavuṭkoṇ ṭolleṉa vōṭu malaināṭaṉ taṉkēṇmai collac coriyum vaḷai.'' (kainnilai.7) |eṉavarum.

maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkic citainta vaḻit tōḻikku niṉaittal cāṉṟa |arumaṟai |uyirttaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:-

``palaviṉ paḻampeṟ paiṅkaṇ kaṭuvaṉ |elaveṉṟu |iṇaipayirum |ēkalcūḻ veṟpaṉ pulavuṅkol tōḻi puṇarvaṟin taṉṉai celavuṅ kaṭintāḷ puṉattu.'' (tiṇaimoḻi.10) |eṉavum,

poḻutum |elliṉṟu peyalum |ōvātu kaḻutukaṇ paṉippa vīcum |ataṉṟalaip pulippal tālip putalvarp pulli |aṉṉāy |eṉṉum |aṉṉaiyum maṉṉō |eṉmalain taṉaṉkol tāṉē taṉmalai |āram nāṟu mārpiṉaṉ māri yāṉaiyiṉ vantuniṉ ṟōṉē.'' (kuṟun.16) |eṉavum varum.

|ivai yellām |iyaṟkaippuṇarcci puṇarntapiṉ nikaḻvaṉa. |uḷḷappuṇarcciyāṉ |urivai pūṇṭiruntavarum |ivvāṟu kūṟappeṟum |eṉṟu koḷka, |āṇṭu maṉanikaḻcci |oruppaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ.

|uyirākkālattu |uyirttalum |uyircela |eṉpatu--|ivvāṟu kūṟākkālattu |uyircellumāṟu colutalum |eṉṟavāṟu.

|īṇṭu, |uyirttal |eṉpatu cuvātam |eṉiṉum |amaiyum. |innikaḻcciyait tōḻikku nāṇattatāl |uraiyāḷāyiṉ, nōyaṭavaruntum |eṉṟavāṟu.

|utāraṇam:-

``taḻaiyaṇi |alkul tāṅkal cellā nuḻaiciṟu nucuppiṟa kevva māka |ammel lākam niṟaiya vīṅkiya kommai varimulai ceppuṭaṉ |etiriṉa yāṅkā kuvaḷkol pūṅkuḻai |eṉṉum |avala neñcamō ṭucāvāk kavalai mākkaṭṭip pētai yūrē.'' (kuṟun.159) itu yāṅkākuvaḷ eṉa uyircelavu kuṟittu niṉṟatu iṉṉa ḷāyiṉaḷ naṉṉutal eṉṟavart tuṉṉac ceṉṟu ceppunarp peṟiṉē naṉṟumaṉ vāḻi tōḻinam paṭappai nīrvār paimputaṟ kalitta mārip pīrattalarcila koṇṭē |eṉa varum.

vēṟṟuvaraivu...taṉpiḻaippākat taḻī|it tēṟalum |eṉpatu-vēṟṟuvaraivu variṉ |atu māṟṟutal mutalākat tamar taṟkātta kāraṇappakkam |īṟāka nikaḻumvaḻit taṉkuṟi tappit talaivaṉ |etirppaṭutalillāk kālattu vantavaṉ peyarnta vaṟuṅkaḷam nōkkit taṉkuṟaiyākavuṭampaṭṭut tēṟutalum |eṉṟavāṟu.

|āṇṭu kalakkamiṉṟit tēṟumeṉpatu kūṟiṉārām.

|avvaḻi, vēṟṟu varaivu variṉ |atu māṟṟutaṟkaṇṇum |eṉpatu--piṟaṉoruvaṉ varaiya variṉ |ataṉai māṟṟutaṟkākavum taṉkuṟi tappum |eṉṟavāṟu.

neṟippaṭu...maṟaippiṉum |eṉpatu--kūṭṭam |uṇmai vaḻakkiyalāl nāṭukiṉṟa kālattu mey vēṟupāṭu nikaḻntuḻi, tōḻi |aṟiyāmalum cevili |aṟiyāmalun talaivi maṟaippiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu.

poṟiyiṉ...|iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--poṟi yeṉpatu |ūḻ. |ūḻāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa puṇarcciyaik kuṟittu |oṟṟumaippaṭṭa kaṇpiṉāṉē talaivaṉ varaitaṟkuk kuṟaiyuṟukiṉṟataṉait teḷinta talaivi ceytaṟku |arumaiyamainta |eṇvakaiyiṉāṟ perumai |iyainta |iyalpiṉaḷāki niṟṟaṟkaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu.

|eṇvakaiyāvatu meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ maṉaṉ |aḻivillāta kūṭṭam |eṉa |otukiṉṟa,
`muṭṭuvayiṟ kaḻaṟal muṉivumeyn niṟuttal
|accattiṉ |akaṟal |avaṉpuṇarvu maṟuttal
tū tumuṉi viṉmai tuñcic cērtal
kātal kaimmikal kaṭṭurai yiṉmai' (meyppāṭ.23)
|eṉpaṉa. |avaṟṟuḷ,

muṭṭuvayiṟ kaḻaṟal |āvatu--kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻāniṉṟuḻi nilavu veḷippāṭu, kāvalarkaṭukutal,, tāytuñcāmai, |ūrtuñcāmai, |ūrtuñcāmai, talaivaṉ kuṟivaruvataṟku |iṭaiyīṭupaṭutal, |ivvaḻik kaḷavoḻukkattiṉāṟ payaṉiṉmai kūṟal. |avvāṟu kūṟi |iṉi |ivvoḻukkam |amaiyumeṉa varainteytutalkāṟum puṇarccaṭayai virumpātu kalakkamiṉṟit teḷivuṭaiyaḷām.

muṉivu meynniṟuttal |āvatu--|ivvoḻukkattiṉāṉ vanta tuṉpattaip piṟarkkup pulaṉākāmai meyyiṉ kaṇṇē niṟuttal.

|avvaḻiyum varainteytal cāṉṟamaiyum puṇarcciyeṉak kuṟi vaḻic cellāḷām.

|accattiṉ |akaṟal |āvatu--|italaip piṟaraṟivar |eṉṉum |accattiṉāṉum kuṟivaḻic cellāḷām.

|avaṉ puṇarvumaṟuttal |āvatu--|avvaḻit talaivaṉmāṭṭut tūtāki varuñ coṟkēṭṭalai muṉiviṉmai.

tañcik cērtal |āvatu--|uṟaṅkāmaiyiṉṟi yuṟakkam nikaḻtal.

kātal kaimmikal |āvaratu--|ivvāṟu ceyyuṅ kātal |aṉpiṉmaiyaṉṟi |aṉpu mikutal.

kaṭṭurai yiṉmai |āvatu--kūṟṟu nikaḻtaliṉmai.

|ivaiyellām kalkakamillāta nilaimai yātaliṟ perumaicāṉṟa |iyalpāyiṉa. poytalaiyaṭutta maṭaliṉ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--poymmaiyāl maṭalēṟuvaṉ |eṉat talaivaṉ kūṟiyavaḻiyum veṟutta |uḷḷattaḷāyk kuṟivaḻic cellāḷām. kaiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīrtuṭaippiṉum |eṉpatu--tōḻikaiyiṉāl talaivi kaṇṇīr tuṭaittavaḻiyuṅ kuṟivaḻic cellāḷām.

veṟiyāṭṭiṭattu veruviṉ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--talaivi vēṟupāṭu |eṟṟiṉāyiṟṟeṉac cevili veṟiyāṭṭuvikkavarum |accattiṉāṉuṅ kuṟivaḻic cellāḷām. kuṟiyiṉ |oppumai maruṭaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ ceyta kuṟiyai |oppumaipaṟṟi ceṉṟu |aḵtu |avvaḻi maruḷutaṟkaṇṇum kuṟivaḻic cellāḷām. |aḵtāvaṉa puṉṉeḻupputal pōlvaṉa. |avaipeṟṟup puḷḷaravam |eḻum. |avvāṟu maruḷutal. varaivutalai variṉum |eṉpatu-talaivaṉ varaiyavarukiṉṟa nāḷ |aṇittākavariṉum kuṟivaḻic cellāḷām. tamartaṟkātta kāraṇamaruṅkiṉum |eṉpatu--taṉṉait tamar kātta kāraṇap pakkattiṉum |eṉṟavā#āṟu. |aḵtu |aiyamuṟṟuk kāttal.

|avaṟṟuḷ vēṟṟuvaraivu variṉ |atu māṟṟutaṟkut talaivi kūṟiya ceyyuḷ :-

``|aṉṉai vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉai puṉṉai poṉniṟam viriyum pūkkeḻu tuṟaivaṉai |eṉṉai |eṉṟum yāmē |ivvūr piṟitoṉ ṟākak kūṟum |āṅkum |ākkumō vāḻiya pālē.'' (|ainkuṟu.110) |eṉa varum.

((neṟippaṭu nāṭṭatta nikaḻntamai maṟaittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ))

``tuṟaivaṉ tuṟanteṉat tuṟaiyirun taḻuta|eṉ mmamar vāṇmuka nōkki |aṉṉainiṉ |avalam |uraiyeṉ ṟaṉaḷē kaṭaleṉ pañcāyp pāvai koṇṭu vaṇṭalañ ciṟumaṉai citaittateṉ ṟēṉē.'' |eṉa varum

((muṭṭuvayiṟ kaḻaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:))

``|irumpiḻi māri |aḻuṅkaṉ mūtūr viḻaviṉ ṟāyiṉun tuñcā tākum mallal |āvaṇam maṟukuṭaṉ maṭiyiṉ valluraik kaṭuñcol |aṉṉai tuñcāḷ piṇikōḷ |aruñciṟai |aṉṉai tuñcil tuñcāk kaṇṇar kāvalar kaṭukuvar |ilaṅkuvēl |iḷaiyar tuñciṉ vai|eyiṟṟu valañcurit tōkai ñāḷi makiḻum |aravavāy ñamali makiḻātu maṭiyiṟ pakaluru |uṟaḻa nilavukkāṉṟu vicumpiṉ |akalvāy maṇṭilam niṉṟuviri yummē tiṅkaḷkal cērpu kaṉaiyiruḷ maṭiyiṉ |illeli valci valvāyk kūkai kaḻutuvaḻaṅ kiyāmat taḻitakak kuḻaṟum vaḷaikkaṭa cēval vāḷātu maṭiyiṉ maṉaicceṟi kōḻi māṇkural |iyalpum |ellā maṭinta kālai yorunāḷ nillā neñcat tavarvā ralarē, |ataṉāl |aripey puṭṭil |ārppap pariciṟantu |āti pōkiya pāypari naṉmā nocci vēlit tittaṉ |uṟantaik kalmutir puṟaṅkāṭ ṭaṉṉa palmuṭṭiṉṟāl tōḻinaṅ kaḷavē.'' (|akam. 122) |eṉa varum. muṉivu meynniṟuttaṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``nōmē neñcē nōmē neñcē |imaitīyp paṉṉa kaṇṇīr tāṅki |amaitaṟ kamaintanaṅ kātalar muṉivu meynniṟuttaṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``nōmē neñcē nōmē neñcē |imaitīyap paṉṉa kaṇṇīr tāṅki |amaitaṟ kamaintanaṅ kātalar |amaivilar |ākutal nōmē neñcē.'' (kuṟun.4) |eṉa varum. |accattiṉ |akaṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``pēṇupa pēṇār periyōr |eṉpatu nāṇuttak kaṉṟatu kāṇuṅ kālai |uyirōr |aṉṉa ceyirtīr naṭpiṉ niṉakkiyāṉ maṟaittal yāvatu mikapperitu |aḻitak kaṉṟāl tāṉē koṇkaṉ yāṉyāy |añcuval |eṉiṉun tāṉ|eṟ pirital cūḻāṉ maṉṉē |iṉiyē kāṉal |āyam |aṟiyiṉum |āṉātu |alarvatu |aṉṟukol |eṉṉu mataṉāl pularvatu kol|avaṉ naṭpeṉa |añcuval tōḻi|eṉ ṉeñcat tāṉē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.72) |eṉa varum |avaṉ puṇarvu maṟuttaṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``yāru millait tāṉē kaḷvaṉ tāṉatu poyppiṉ yāṉ|evaṉ ceykō tiṉaittā ḷaṉṉa ciṟupacuṅ kāla |oḻukunīr |āral pārkkuṅ kurukum |uṇṭutām maṇanta ñāṉṟē.'' (kuṟun.25) |eṉa varum. tū tumuṉiviṉmaikkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``pulvīḻ |iṟṟik kallivar veḷvēr varaiyiḻi yaruviyiṉ tōṉṟu nāṭaṉ tī til neñcattuk kiḷavi namvayiṉ vantaṉṟu vāḻi tōḻi nāmum neypey tīyiṉ |etirkoṇṭu tāmvarain taṉaiyameṉa viṭukam tūtē.'' (kuṟun. 106) |eṉa varum. tuñcic cērtaṟkuc ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka. kātal kaimmikutaṟkuc ceyyuḷ :- ``kaṉṟum |uṇṇātu kalattiṉumpaṭātu nallāṉ tīmpāl nilattuk kā |aṅku |eṉakku mākā teṉṉaikkum |utavātu pacalai |uṇī|iyar vēṇṭun titalai |alkul|eṉ māmaik kaviṉē.'' (kuṟun.27) |eṉa varum. kaṭṭuraiyiṉmaikkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻātu. poytalaiyaṭutta maṭaliṉkaṇ talaimakaḷ kūṟiya ceyyuḷ vanta vaḻik kāṇka.

kaiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīr tuṭaittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``yām|em kāman tāṅkavun tāmatam keḻutakai maiyiṉ |aḻutaṉa tōḻi kaṉṟāṟṟup paṭutta puṉtalaic ciṟā|ar maṉṟa vēṅkai malarpata nōkki |ēṟā tiṭṭa |ēmap pūcal viṇtōy viṭarakat tiyampum kuṉṟa nāṭaṟ kaṇṭa|eṅ kaṇṇē.'' (kuṟun.241) |eṉa varum. veṟiyāṭṭiṭattu veruviṉāṟ kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``nammuṟu tuyaram nōkki |aṉṉai vēlavaṟ ṟantaṉaḷ |āyiṉav vēlaṉ veṟikamaḻ nāṭaṉ kēṇmai |aṟiyumō tilla ceṟiyeyiṟ ṟōyē. (|aiṅkuṟu. 241) |eṉa varum. kuṟiyiṉ |oppumai maruṭaṟkuk kūṟiya ceyyuḷ :- |aṇikaṭal taṇcērppaṉ tērppirimā pūṇṭa maṇiyaravam |eṉṟeḻuntu pōntēṉ--kaṉivirumpum puḷḷaravaṅ kēṭṭup peyarntēṉ |oḷiyiḻāy |uḷḷuruku neñkiṉēṉ yāṉ.'' (|aintiṇaiyaim.50) ``....................... kaṉaipeyal naṭunāḷyāṉ kaṇmāṟak kuṟipeṟā |aṉ puṉaiyiḻāy |eṉpaḻi niṉakkuraikkun tāṉeṉpa tuḷinacai vēṭkaiyāṉ micaipāṭum puḷḷiṉṟaṉ |aḷinacai|i |ārvuṟṟa |aṉpiṉēṉ yāṉāka.'' (kalit.46) |eṉa varum. varaivu talaivantavaḻik kūṟiya ceyyuḷ :- ``kollaip puṉatta |akilcumantu kalpāyntu vāṉiṉ |aruvi tatumpak kaviṉiya nāṭaṉ nayamuṭaiyaṉ |eṉpataṉāl nīppiṉum vāṭal maṟantaṉa tōḷ.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.2) nayamuṭaiya ṉeṉṟataṉāṉ varaivu talaivantamai yaṟintu kūṟiṉāḷām. ``|ilaipaṭar taṇkuḷavi yēynta potumpiṟ kulaiyuṭaik kāntaḷ |iṉavaṇ ṭimirum varaiyaka nāṭaṉum vantāṉmaṟ ṟaṉṉai |alaiyum |alaipōyiṟ ṟiṉṟu.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.3) kaḷa vaṟivuṟṟavaḻik kūṟiya ceyyuḷ :- ``yāṅkā kuvamō |aṇinutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ tēmpaṭu cāral ciṟutiṉaip peruṅkural cevvāyp paiṅkiḷi kavara nīmaṟṟu |evvāyc ceṉṟaṉai |avaṇeṉak kūṟi |aṉṉai yāṉāḷ kaḻaṟamuṉ ṉiṉṟu |aruvi |ārkkum peruvarai nāṭaṉai |aṟiyalum |aṟiyēṉ kāṇṭalum |ilaṉē vetirpuṉai taṭṭaiyēṉ malarpūk koytu cuṉaipāyn tāṭiṟṟum |ilaṉeṉa niṉaivilai poyyal |antō vāyttaṉai yatukēṭṭut talaiyiṟaiñ ciṉaḷē yaṉṉai celavoḻin taṉaiyā laṇiyai nīpuṉattē.'' (naṟṟiṇai. 147) |eṉa varum. tamartaṟkātta kāraṇap pakkattiṟkuk kūṟiya ceyyuḷ :- ``perunīr |aḻuvat tentai tanta'' |eṉṉuṅ kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai niraiyuḷ ``palpūṅ kāṉal |alkiṉam varutal kavvai nallaṇaṅ kuṟṟa |ivvūrk koṭitaṟi peṇṭir coṟkoṇ ṭaṉṉai kaṭikoṇ ṭaṉaḷē tōḻi peruntuṟai |ellaiyum |iravum |eṉṉātu kalleṉa valavaṉ |āynta vaṇpari nilavumaṇal koṭkumōr tēruṇ ṭeṉavē.'' (|akam.20) |eṉa varum.

((taṉkuṟitaḷḷiya teruḷāk kālai vantavaṉ peyarnta vaṟun kaḷam nōkkit taṉpiḻaippākat taḻī|it |itaṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ))

`|iruḷkiḻippatupōl' |eṉṉum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai niraiyuḷ,

``vāḷnaṭan taṉṉa vaḻakkaruṅ kavalai |uḷḷunar |uṭkuṅ kallaṭarca ciṟuneṟi |aruḷpuri neñcamō |allaṉ tanta nītava ṟuṭaiyaiyum |allai niṉvayiṉ |āṉā |arumpaṭar ceyta yāṉē tōḻi tavaṟuṭai yēṉē.'' (|akam.72) |eṉa varum.

vaḻuviṉṟu...|aṉṉavumuḷavē |eṉpatu--vaḻuviṉṟu nilai|iya |iyaṟpaṭuporuṇ mutalāka `|ēmañcāṉṟa |uvakai' yīṟākac collappaṭṭa |iṭaṅkaḷil taṉṉiṭattu |urimaiyum |avaṉiṭattup parattaimaiyum |aṉṉavaiyum nikaḻappeṟum |eṉṟavāṟu.

|aṉṉa |eṉpatu |avaipōlvaṉa |eṉṟavāṟu.

|oriṭattukkaṇ |eṉṟataṉāl |ivvāṟu |ellār māṭṭum |evviṭattum nikaḻātu |eṉṟavāṟām. |eṉavē mēṟ kuṟippiṉum |iṭattiṉumallatu (kaḷaviyal-18) kūṟṟu nikaḻāteṉpataṉai maṟuttu |oriṭattuk kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟām.

|avaṟṟuḷ, vaḻuviṉṟu nilai|iya |iyaṟpaṭu poruḷiṉum |eṉpatu talaivaṉai |iyaṟpaḻittavaḻi |avaṉ kuṟṟamilaṉāka nilainiṟuttappaṭṭa |iyaṟpaṭamoḻinta |avaṉ kuṟmilaṉāka nilainiṟuttappaṭṭa |iyaṟpaṭamoḻinta poruṇmaik kaṇṇum taṉ vayiṉ |urimai tōṉṟavum |avaṉ vayiṟ parattaimai tōṉṟavum kūṟum talaivi |eṉṟavāṟu.

|iraṇṭiṉuḷ |oṉṟutōṉṟa |uraikku meṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē |iraṇṭun tōṟṟa varuvaṉavu muḷavām.

poḻutu māṟum...cintaik kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ varuṅkālamum |iṭaṉum kuṟṟamuḷavātalāṉ, |āṇṭu |aḻivuvanta cintaik kaṇṇum talaimakaḷ taṉvayiṉ |urimaiyum |avaṉvayiṟ parattaimaiyumu/ tōṉṟak kūṟum |eṉṟavāṟu.

kāmañ ciṟappiṉum |eṉpatu--talaimakaṉmāṭṭaṭu vēṭkai mikiṉum taṉvayiṉ |urimaiyum |avaṉvayiṟ parattaimaiyum |uraikkun talaivi |eṉṟavāṟu.

|avaṉ |aḷi ciṟappiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ talaiyaḷi mikkavaḻiyum taṉvayiṉ |urimaiyum |avaṉvayiṟ parattaimaiyum tōṉṟakkūṟum |eṉṟavāṟu.

|ēmañcāṉṟa |uvakaikkaṇṇum |eṉpatu-|ēmam poruntiya makiḻccivantuḻit taṉvayiṉ |urimaiyum |avaṉvayiṟ parattaimaiyum tōṉṟak kūṟum |ēṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtāvatu |avaṉ varaintallatu nīṅkāṉ |eṉṉum |uvakai.

((|avaṟṟuḷ, vaḻuviṉṟu nilai|iya |iyaṟpaṭu poruṭkaṇ kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ)) ``|aṭumpama laṅkoṭi |uḷputain toḻippa veṇmalar virikkun taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉ koṭiyaṉ |āyiṉum |āka |avaṉē tōḻi|eṉ ṉuyirkā valaṉē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.taṉi.6) |eṉa varum.

((poḻutu māṟum puraiva taṉmaiyiṉ |aḻivutalaivanta cintaiyāl talaivi kūṟiyaceyyuḷ))"

``koṭuvari vēṅkai piḻaittuk kōṭpaṭṭu maṭicevi vēḻam |iriya-|aṭiyōcai |añci yotuṅkum |ataruḷḷi |āriruḷ tuñcā cuṭarttoṭi kaṇ'' (|aintiṇaiyaim. 16)
