``vaḷaivāyc ciṟukiḷi viḻaitiṉai kaṭiyac celkeṉ ṟāḷē |aṉṉai |eṉanī colliṉ |evaṉān tōḻi kollai neṭuṅkai vaṉmāṉ kaṭumpakal |uḻanta kuṟuṅkai |irumpulik kōḷval |ēṟṟai paiṅkaṭ cennāy paṭupatam pārkkum |āriruḷ naṭunāḷ varuti cāral nāṭa vāralō |eṉavē.'' (kuṟun.141)
|eṉa varum.

((kāma mikkavaḻik kūṟiya ceyyuḷ))

``|amma vāḻi tōḻi nalaṉē |iṉṉa tākutal koṭitē puṉṉai |aṇimalar tuṟaitoṟu virikkum maṇinīrc cērppaṉai maṟavā tōrkkē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.117) |itu taṉvayiṉ |urimai: ``naḷḷeṉ ṟaṉṟē yāmañ collavintu |iṉitaṭaṅ kiṉarē mākkaḷ muṉiviṉṟi naṉantalai |ulakamun tuñcum |oryāṉ maṉṟa tuñcā tēṉē.'' (kuṟun.6)

((|iḵtu |avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai:))

``koṭuntāḷ |alava kuṟaiyām |irappēm |oṭuṅkā |olikaṭaṟ cērppaṉ--neṭuntēr kaṭanta vaḻiyai|eṅ kaṇṇārak kāṇa naṭantu citaiyāti nī.'' (|aintiṇaiyaim. 42)

((|avaṉaḷi ciṟantavaḻit talaivikūṟiya ceyyuḷ))

``cēṇōṉ māṭṭiya naṟumpukai ñekiḻi vāṉa mīṉiṉ vayiṉ vayiṉ |imaikkum |oṅkumalai, nāṭaṉ cāntupular |akalam |uḷḷiṉ |uḷnōy malkum pulliṉ māyva tevaṉkol |aṉṉāy.'' (kuṟun.150) ``maṉṟap palaviṉ cuḷaiviḷai tīmpaḻam |uṇṭuvantu manti mulaivaruṭak--kaṉṟamarn(tu) yāmāp piriva tilam.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.4) |eṉa varum.

((|ēmañ cāṉṟa |uvakaikkaṇ kūṟiya ceyyuḷ))

|oṅkal |iṟuvaraimēṟ kāntaḷ kaṭikaviṉap pāmpeṉa |oṭi |urumiṭippak kaṇṭiraṅkum pūṅkuṉṟa nāṭaṉ puṇarntavan nāḷpōlā |īṅku nekiḻnta vaḷai.'' (tiṇaimoḻi.3) |eṉa varum.

((parattaimai tōṉṟa vantataṟkuc ceyyuḷ)) ``.......................... kaṇaṅkoḷ |iṭumaṇaṟ kāvi varuntap piṇaṅkiru mōṭṭa tiraivan taḷikkum maṇaṅkamaḻ |aimpālār |ūṭalai |āṅkē vaṇaṅki |uṇarppāṉ tuṟai.'' (kalit. 131)
|eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ tāṉ |ūṭiṉāḷākavum makiḻntavāṟum |avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai kūṟiyavāṟum kāṇka.

|iccūttirattāṟ colliyatu `maṟaintavaṟ kāṇṭal' mutalāka |otappaṭṭa |aṟuvakaip poruṇmaiyum, `kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkal' mutalākak `kūṟiyavāyil koḷḷākkālai' |īṟākavarum makiḻcciyiṉāl maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkik citaintavaḻi |eṇṇutal cāṉṟa |arumaṟaiyaic collutalum, |ivvāṟum |eṇṇantāṉ |uraiyākkālattut taṉṉuyir cellumāṟu |uraittalum, `vēṟṟuvaraivu variṉ |atu māṟṟutal' mutalākat `tamartaṟkātta kāraṇappakkam' |īṟākat taṉ kuṟipiḻaikka niṟpakap peṟum |eṉavum, |avvaḻit talaivaṉ vantu peyarntuḻik kalakkamiṉṟit teḷitalum, `vaḻuviṉṟu nilai|iya |iyaṟpaṭu poruḷ' mutalāka |ēmam cāṉṟa |uvakai' |īṟākat tāṉ |uriyaḷākiya neṟiyum talaivaṉ |ayalākiya nilaiyumpō lavariṟ collappeṟum |eṉavum kuṟippiṉum |iṭattiṉum |allatu vēṭkai neṟippaṭṭavāṟākat talaivikkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻumiṭamum |uṇarttiyavāṟu.

{SA TP110i}

  1. varaiviṭai vaitta kālattu varuntiṉum
  2. varaiyā nāḷiṭai vantōṉ muṭṭiṉum
  3. |uraiyeṉat tōḻikku |uraittaṟ kaṇṇum
  4. tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamum |uḷavē.

|ituvum talaivimāṭṭuc col nikaḻumiṭam |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) talaivaṉ |iyaṟkaippuṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkukiṉṟāṉ; |iṉṉanāḷ varaintu koḷval |eṉakkūṟit tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṟku muyalātu taṇantavaḻi yataṉait tōḻi |aiyappaṭuṅ kuṟipputtōṉṟāmai maṟaittoḻukiya talaivi |avaṉ varuntuṇaiyum |āṟṟātu varuttamuṟiṉum varaiyāta nāḷiṉ kaṇ maṟaintoḻukāniṉṟa talaivaṉ cevili mutalāyiṉārai muṭṭiṉavaḻiyum |ivvoḻukkattiṉai niṉ tōḻikku |uraiyeṉat talaivaṉ kūṟiyavaḻiyum talaivi tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamum |uḷa |eṉṟavāṟu.

|ummai |etirmaṟaiyātalāṟ kūṟāmai perumpāṉmai. kālamum |eṉṟatu |ivvoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāṅ kālam. |akkālattut tōḻi matiyuṭampaṭāmal |aṟivikkum |eṉṟavāṟu. |iṉi, varaiviṭaivaitta kālattu varuttamuṟṟavaḻik kūṟiya ceyyuḷ:- ``puṉavaṉ tuṭavaip poṉpōṟ ciṟutiṉaik kaṭiyuṇ kaṭavuṭ kiṭṭa cilkural |aṟiyā tuṇṭaṉam maññai yāṭumakaḷ veṟiyuṟu vaṉappiṉ vērttuṟṟu naṭuṅkuñ cūrmalai nāṭaṉ kēṇmai nīrmali kaṇṇoṭu niṉaippā kiṉṟē.'' (kuṟun.105) |eṉa varum. varaiyā nāḷiṭai vantōṉ muṭṭiyavaḻit talaivi kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``tāḻai kurukīṉun taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉai māḻaimāṉ nōkkiṉ maṭamoḻi--nūḻai nuḻaiyu maṭamakaṉ yārkoleṉ ṟaṉṉai puḻaiyum |aṭaittāḷ katavu.'' (kainnilai.59) |eṉavum, ``|aṟiyā maiyiṉ |aṉṉai |añcik kuḻaiyaṉ kōtaiyaṉ kuṟumpain toṭiyaṉ viḻavayar tuṇaṅkai taḻuvukañ cella neṭunimir teruviṟ kaipuku koṭumiṭai notuma lāḷaṉ katumeṉat tākkaliṉ kēṭpār |uḷarkol |illaikol pōṟṟeṉa yāṇatu pacalai |eṉṟaṉaṉ |ataṉetir nāṇilai |eluva |eṉṟavan ticiṉē ceṟunarum viḻaiyuñ cemma lōṉeṉa naṟunutal |arivai pōṟṟēṉ ciṟumai perumaiyiṟ kāṇātu tuṇintē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.50) |eṉavum varum. |itaṉkaṇ |eṉṟāṉeṉa |oru col varuvikka. |uraiyeṉat tōḻikku |uraittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``poṉ|iṇar vēṅkai kaviṉiya pūmpoḻiluḷ naṉmalai nāṭaṉ nalampuṉaiya-meṉmulaiyāy pōyiṉa tinnāḷ puṉattu maṟaiyiṉāl |ēyiṉār |iṉṟum |iṉitu.'' (|aintiṇaiyaim.11) |eṉa varum |iṉṉum, `|uraiyeṉat tōḻik kuraittaṟ kaṇṇum' |eṉpataṟkut talaivaṟku |uraiyeṉat tōḻikku |uraiyeṉat tōḻikku |uraittaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉṟumām. |utāraṇam- ``|eṉṉaikol tōḻi |avarkaṇṇum naṉkillai |aṉṉai mukaṉum |atuvākum--poṉṉalar puṉṉaiyam pūṅkāṉaṟ cērppaṉait takkatō niṉṉalla tilleṉ ṟurai.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.54) |eṉa varum. (22)

{SA TP111i}

  1. |uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum
  2. ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpucciṟan taṉṟeṉat
  3. tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu
  4. kāmak kiḻavaṉ |uḷvaḻip paṭiṉum
  5. tāvil naṉmoḻi kiḻavi kiḷappiṉum
  6. |āvakai piṟavum tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē.

|ituvum |atu.

(|i-ḷ.) |uyiriṉum nāṇ ciṟantatu; |ataṉiṉum kuṟṟantīrnta kāṭciyiṉaiyuṭaiya kaṟpuc ciṟantatu |eṉa muṉṉōr kūṟṟai yuṭkoṇṭaṭu talaivaṉuḷḷa viṭattuc cellum varutta millāc collait talaivi collutalumākiya |avvakai piṟavuntōṉṟum |avai poruḷām |eṉṟavāṟu. maṉ |ākkattiṉkaṇ vantatu. |eṉavē |ivvāṟu ceytal poruḷalla |eṉṟu kūṟaṟka |eṉṟavāṟu. |itaṉuḷ nāṇattiṉum kaṟpuc ciṟanta teṉṟavāṟu. notumalar varaivu nōkkik kūṟuvatu-- ``|aḷitō tāṉē nāṇē nammoṭu naṉinīṭu |uḻantaṉṟu maṉṉē |iṉiyē vāṉpūṅ karumpiṉ |oṅkumaṇaṟ ciṟuciṟai tīmpuṉal neritara vīḻntuk kā |aṅkut tāṅkum |aḷavait tāṅkik kāma neritarak kainnil lātē.'' (kuṟun. 149) ``kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷai nekiḻa nāṭoṟum pāṭila kaliḻuṅ kaṇṇoṭu pulampi |īṅkivaṇ |uṟaitalum |uykuvam |āṅkē |eḻu|iṉi vāḻiyeṉ neñcē muṉā|atu kullaik kaṇṇi vaṭuvar muṉaiyatu palvēṟ kaṭṭi naṉṉāṭṭu |umpar moḻipeyar tē|ettā rāyiṉum vaḻipaṭal cūḻnticiṉ |avaruṭai nāṭṭē.'' (kuṟun.11) |itu kākkiḻava ṉuḷvaḻip paṭutal. tāvil naṉmoḻi kūṟiyataṟkuṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``maṉṟa marā|atta pē|emutir kaṭavuḷ koṭiyōrt teṟū|um |eṉpa yāvatuṅ koṭiyar |allar|em kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭar pacai|iya pacantaṉṟu nutalē ñekiḻiya ñekiḻntaṉṟu taṭameṉ tōḷē.'' (kuṟun.87) |eṉa varum. (25)

{SA TP112i}

  1. nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum |oḻukkamum |uṇṭiyum
  2. ceyviṉai maṟaippiṉum celaviṉum payilviṉum
  4. puṇarcci yetirppā ṭuḷḷuṟuttu varū|um
  5. |uṇarcci |ēḻiṉum |uṇarnta piṉṟai
  6. meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinilai piḻaiyātu
  7. palvōṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭat tāṉuṅ
  8. kuṟaiyuṟaṟ ketiriya kiḻavaṉai maṟaiyuṟap
  9. perumaiyiṟ peyarppiṉum |ulakurait toḻippiṉum
  10. |arumaiyiṉ |akaṟciyum |avaḷaṟi vuṟuttup
  11. piṉvā veṉṟalum pētaimai yūṭṭalum
  12. muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟainiṟut turaittalum
  13. |añciyac cuṟuttalum |uraittuḻik kūṭṭamoṭu
  14. |eñcātu kiḷanta |irunāṉku kiḷaviyum
  15. vanta kiḻavaṉai māyañ ceppip
  16. poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟitta kālaiyum
  17. puṇarntapiṉ |avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟ kaṇṇuṅ
  18. kuṟaintavaṭ paṭariṉum maṟaintava ḷarukat
  19. taṉṉoṭum |avaḷoṭum muṉṉamuṉ taḷai|ip
  20. piṉṉilai nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkiṉum
  21. naṉṉayam peṟṟuḷi nayampuri |iṭattiṉum
  22. |eṇṇarum paṉṉakai kaṇṇiya vakaiyiṉum
  23. puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum vēṇṭāp piriviṉum
  24. |ōmpaṭaik kiḷavip pāṅkiṉ kaṇṇum
  25. ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāl citaivuṭait tāyiṉum
  26. |eṉpunekap pirintōḷ viḻicceṉṟu kaṭai|i
  27. |aṉputalai |aṭutta vaṉpuṟaik kaṇṇum
  28. |āṟṟatu tīmai |aṟivuṟu kalakkamum
  29. kāppiṉ kaṭumai kaiyaṟa variṉum
  30. kaḷaṉum poḻutum varainīlai vilakkik
  31. kātal mikuti |uḷappaṭap piṟavum
  32. nāṭum |ūrum |illuṅ kuṭiyum
  33. piṟappuñ ciṟappum |iṟappa nōkki
  34. |avaṉvayiṉ tōṉṟiya kiḷaviyoṭu tokai |i
  35. |aṉainila vakaiyāṉ varaital vēṇṭiṉum
  36. |aiyac ceykai tāykketir maṟuttup
  37. poyyeṉa māṟṟi meyvaḻik koṭuppiṉum
  38. |avaḷvilaṅ kuṟiṉuṅ kaḷampeṟak kāṭṭiṉum
  39. piṟaṉvarai vāyiṉum |avaṉvaraivu maṟuppiṉum
  40. muṉṉilai |aṟaṉeṉap paṭutaleṉ ṟiruvakaip
  41. puraitīr kiḷavi tāyiṭaip pukuppiṉum
  42. varaivuṭaṉ paṭṭōrk kaṭāval vēṇṭiṉum
  43. |āṅkataṉ taṉmaiyiṉ vaṉpu|aiṟa |uḷappaṭap
  44. pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭu vakaiyiṉum
  45. tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṉ tōḻi mēṉa.

|eṉṟatu, kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ tōḻikkuriya kiḷaviyellām tōḻikkuriya kiḷaviyellām tokuttu |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) nāṟṟamu.....nāṭṭattāṉum |eṉpatu--nāṟṟamutalākac collappaṭṭa |ēḻiṉāṉum puṇarccikku muntuṟṟa nilaimaiyai |uṭkoṇṭu varum maṉanikaḻcci yēḻiṉum puṇarcciyuṇmai yaṟinta piṉṟai meyyiṉāṉum poyyiṉāṉun talaivi kulattiṉuḷḷār nilaimaiyiṟ piḻaiyātu palavāki vēṟupaṭṭa kavarnta poruṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya |ārāytaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. nāṟṟam |eṉpatu--pūviṉāṉum cāntiṉāṉum talaivaṉ māṭṭuḷatākiya kalaviyāl talaivimāṭṭuḷatāya nāṟutal. tōṟṟam |eṉpatu--puṇarcciyāṉ varum poṟpu. |oḻukkam |eṉpatu--|āyattāroṭu vēṇṭiyavā ṟoḻukutalaṉṟit taṉṉaip pēṇiyoḻukutal. |uṇṭi |eṉpatu--|uṇṇum |aḷaviṟ kuṟaital. ceyviṉai maṟaittal |āvatu--pūkkoytalum puṉalāṭalum pōlum viṉaikaḷait tōḻiyai maṟaintut taṉittu nikaḻttutal, |aṉṟiyum talaivaṉ ceyta puṇarcciyākiya karumattiṉaip pulappaṭaviṭātu tōḻiyai maṟaittalum |eṉṟumām. celaviṉum |eṉpatu--|etticaiyiṉum ceṉṟu viḷaiyāṭuvāḷ |oru ticaiyai nōkkiccēṟal. payilviṉum |eṉpatu--|oriṭattup payilutal. puṇarcci |etirppāṭu |āvatu--puṇarvataṟku muntuṟṟa kālam. |uḷḷuṟuttal |āvatu--|uṭkōṭal. |uṇarcci |ēḻāvatu--nāṟṟa mutalākac collappaṭṭavaṟṟāl varum maṉa nikaḻcci |ēḻum. palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭam |eṉpatu--|oṉṟōṭoṉṟu |ovvāta vēṟupaṭṭaṉavāki |iruporuḷ payakkum coṟkaḷālē yārāytal. |avṟṟuḷ cila varumāṟu :- ``kaṇṇuñ cēyari parantaṉṟu nutalum nuṇviyar poṟittu vaṇṭārk kummē vāṅkamai meṉṟōḷ maṭantai yāṅkā yiṉaḷkol |eṉṉum|eṉ neñcē.'' (ciṟṟeṭṭakam) |itu talaivi tōṟṟaṅkaṇṭu pāṅki kūṟiyatu. piṟavum |aṉṉa. kuṟaiyuṟaṟ ketiriya kaḻivaṉai maṟaiyuṟa |eṉpatu--kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ kaṇṇē yuṟutaṟkākat taṉatu kuṟaiyaic collavēṇṭi |etirppaṭṭa talaivaṇai yeṉṟavāṟu. maṟaiyuṟa |eṉpataṉai muṉṉē kūṭṭuka. perumaiyiṟ peyarppiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉatu perumaiyāṉ nīkkalum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``|ivaḷē, kāṉal naṇṇiya kāmar ciṟukuṭi nīlniṟap poruṅkaṭal kalaṅka |uḷpukku mīṉeṟi paratavar makaḷē nīyē neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum niyama mūtūrk kaṭuntērc celvar kātal makaṉē niṇaccuṟā |uṟutta |uṇakkal vēṇṭi |iṉappuḷ |oppum |emakkunalaṉ |evaṉō pulavu nāṟutum celaniṉ ṟīmō perunīr viḷaiyuḷ ciṟunal vāḻkkai nummoṭu puraivatō |aṉṟē |emma ṉōriṟ cemmalum |uṭaittē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.45) |eṉa varum. |ulakurait toḻittal |eṉpatu--|ulakattār makaṭkoḷḷumāṟu koḷḷeṉak kūṟutal. ``kōṭīr |elvaḷaik koḻumaṭaṟ kūntal |āytoṭi maṭavaral vēṇṭuti yāyiṉ teṇkaḻic cēyiṟāṟ paṭū|un taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa varaintaṉai koṇmō.'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 199) |iṉṉum |ulakurait toḻittal |eṉṟataṉāṟ kaiyuṟaimaṟaiyuṅ koḷka. ``nīṭunīrk kāṉal nerunalum nittilaṅkoṇ ṭaiya vantīr kōṭuyar veṇmaṇaṟ koṟkaiyem |ūrivaṟṟāṟ kuṟaiyi |āṭuṅ kaḻaṅkum |aṇiviḷakkum |ammaṉaiyum [lēmiyām pāṭi yavaippaṉavum pantāṭap paṭuvaṉavum paṉinīr muttam.'' |arumaiyiṉ |akaṟciyum |eṉpatu--talaiviyaik kiṭṭitaṟku |arumai kūṟiyakaṟṟutal. |utāraṇam:- ``nerunalum muṉṉā ḷellaiyum |oruciṟaip putuvai yākaliṉ |ataṟkeyta nāṇi nēriṟai vaḷaittōḷniṉ tōḻi ceyta |āruyir varuttaṅ kaḷaiyā yōveṉa |eṟkuṟai yuṟuti rāyiṟ coṟkuṟai |empatat kaṭavuḷ |allaḷō peruma vālkōṉ virippuṟan taivarum mañcucūḻ maṇivarai maṉṉavaṉ makaḷē.'' |eṉa varum. |avaḷaṟi vuṟuttup piṉvāveṉṟalum |eṉpatu--niṉṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭāṭkuc ceṉṟu |aṟivittup piṉṉar |eṉmāṭṭuvā |eṉṟavāṟu. |avaṟṟuḷ, nīyē ceṉṟu |aṟivu |eṉṟataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``taṉṉaiyun tāṉnāṇuñ cāyalāṭ kī turaippiṉ |eṉṉaiyum nāṇappaṭuṅ kaṇṭāy--|eṉṉaiya vēyērmeṉ tōḷikkuka vēṟāy |iṉiyorunāḷ nīyē yuraittu viṭu.'' piṉvā veṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``nāḷvēṅkai poṉviḷaiyum naṉmalai naṉṉāṭa kōḷvēṅkai pōṟkoṭiyar |eṉṉaiyaṉmār--kōḷvēṅ |aṉṉaiyāl nīyumaṭ |aruntaḻaiyām |ēlāmaikku [kai |eṉṉaiyō nāḷai |eḷitu.'' |eṉavarum. (tiṇaimālai.2) pētaimai yūṭṭal |eṉpatu--nēriṉum |avaḷ |aṟivāḷorutti yallaḷ |eṉṟu talaivaṟkuk kūṟal. |utāraṇam:- ``naṟuntaṇ takaram vakuḷam |ivaṟṟai veṟumputalpōl vēṇṭātu vēṇṭi--|eṟintuḻutu centiṉai vittuvār taṅkai piṟarnōykku nontiṉaiya vallaḷō nōkku.'' (tiṇaimālai.24) /|iṉṉum, pētaimai yūṭṭal |eṉpataṉāl tōḻi tāṉ |aṟiyāḷ pōlak kūṟutaluṅ koḷka. |utāraṇam:- ``puṉtalai mantik kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ kuṉṟuḻai naṇṇiya muṉṟil pōkātu |eriyakaintu |aṉṉa vītatai |iṇara vēṅkaiyam paṭuciṉaip poruntik kaiya tēmpey tīmpāl vauvaliṟ koṭicci |eḻuteḻil citaiya |aḻuta kaṇṇē tērvaṇ cōḻar kuṭantai vāyil māriyaṅ kiṭaṅkiṉ |īriya malarnta peyaluṟu nīlam pōṉṟaṉa viralē pā|aya |avvayiṟu |alaittaliṉ |āṉātu |āṭumaḻai tavaḻuṅ kōṭuyar potiyiṉ |oṅkiruñ cilampil pūtta kāntaḷaṅ koḻumukai pōṉṟaṉa civantē.'' |eṉa varum. (naṟṟiṇai.379) muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟainiṟut turaittalum |eṉpatu--muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟaiyē niṟuttuk kūṟalum |eṉṟavāṟu. niṟuttuk kūṟalāvatu nīṅkaviṭātu |uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kūṟal. |iṉṉum muṉpukūṭiṉāṟ pōlak kūṭa |amaiyumeṉṟu kūṟutal, |utāraṇam vantavaḻikkāṇka. |añci |accuṟuttalum |eṉpatu--tāṉ |accamuṟṟu |añciṉa taṉmaiyait talaivaṟku |aṟivittalum |eṉṟavāṟu. |atu yāy varuva ḷeṉṟāṉum tamaiyaṉmār varuva reṉṟāṉum kāvalar varuva reṉṟāṉum kūṟutal. |utāraṇam:- ``yāṉai |uḻalum |aṇikiḷar nīḷvaraik kāṉaka vāḻkkaik kuṟavar makaḷirēm |ēṉaluḷ |aiya varavumaṟ ṟeṉṉaikol kāṇiṉum kāyvar |emar.'' (tiṇaimoḻi.6) |eṉavarum, |uraittuḻik kūṭṭamoṭu |eṉpatu--niṉṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭāḷ yāvaḷ |eṉa viṉāyavaḻi |ittaṉmaiyāḷ |eṉac collakkēṭṭa tōḻi|avaḷum niṉ taṉmaiyāḷ |eṉa |ivaṉōṭu kūṭṭiyuraittalum |eṉṟavāṟu. |oṭu |eṇṇiṉkaṇ vantatu. |utāraṇam:- ``neṟinīr |iruṅkaḻi nīlamuñ cūṭāṉ poṟimāṇ variyalavaṉ |āṭṭalum |āṭṭāḷ tirunutal vērarumpuc cintiyā niṉṟāḷ ceṟinīr/ttaṇ cērppayā ṉeṉcollic cērkēṉ.'' |eṉa varum. |eñcātu kiḷanta |irunāṉku kiḷaviyum |eṉpatu--|oḻiyātu kūṟiya |eṭṭuk kūṟṟum |eṉṟavāṟu. muṉṉaip puṇarccimuṟai yaṟintāḷātaliṉ |avaṉ |irantu piṉṉiṉṟuḻi |īṇṭuk kūṟiya |ellām |avaṉ |uḷḷakkarut taṟiyuntuṇaiyum taḻī|ikakoṇṭu kūṟiṉallatu |oḻittal poruḷākak kūṟāḷ |eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. |ivai |eṭṭum kuṟaiyuṟavuṇartaliṉ pakuti. vanta kiḻavaṉai māyañ ceppip poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟitta kālaiyum |eṉpatu--māyam collivanta kiḻavaṉait talaivi poṟuttakāraṇam kuṟittakālaiyum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |avvaḻit talaivaṉ kuṟippumu/ talaivikuṟippum |uṇartalum talaivaṉ kēṭṭataṟku māṟṟam kūṟutalum |uḷavām. māyam ceppivanta kiḻavaṉ |eṉa māṟṟuka. māyam cepputalāvatu yāṉai pōntatō māṉ pōntatō |eṉakkūṟal. ``|irantu kuṟaiyaṟātu kiḻaviyun tōḻiyum |oruṅkutalaip peyta cevvi nōkkip patiyum peyarum piṟavum viṉā|ayp putuvōṉ pōlap poruntupu kiḷantu matiyuṭam paṭuttaṟkum |uriyaṉ |eṉpa.'' (|iṟaiyaṉārakap.6) |ēṉpataṉāṟ kuṟaiyuṟavuṇartal nikaḻntuḻi |itu nikaḻāteṉṟu koḷka. |ataṉkaṭ kuṟippuṇarvataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``vēṅkai malara veṟikamaḻ taṇcilampiṉ vāṅkamai meṉtāḷ kuṟavar makaḷirēm cōrntu kuruti |oḻukamaṟ ṟippuṟam pōntatil |aiya kaḷiṟu.'' (tiṇaimoḻi.1) |eṉavum, ``neṭuntēr kaṭai|it tamiyarāy niṉṟu kaṭuṅkaḷiṟu kāṇīrō |eṉṟīr koṭuṅkuḻaiyār yāṉai |ataruḷḷi niṟparō tampuṉattuḷ |ēṉaṟ kiḷikaṭiku vār.'' |eṉavum, ``|ēṉal kāval |ivaḷum |allaḷ māṉvaḻi varukuvaṉ |ivaṉum |allaṉ narantaṅ kaṇṇi |ivaṉō ṭivaḷiṭaik karanta |uḷḷamoṭu karutiyatu piṟitē nammuṉ nāṇiṉar pōlat tammuḷ matumaṟain tuṇṭōr makiḻcci pōla |uḷḷat tuḷḷē makiḻpa collum |āṭupa kaṇṇi ṉāṉē.'' |eṉavum, puṇarntapiṉ |avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--mēṟ collappaṭṭa mūvakaiyāṉum puṇarcciyuṇmai poruntiyapiṉ talaivaṉakaṇ tāḻaniṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. |atu nī karutiyatu muṭikkaṟpālai |eṉavum nī |ivaḷaip pātukāttal vēṇṭumeṉavum |ivvakai kūṟutal. |utāraṇam:- ``..................... |araviṉ poṟiyum |aṇaṅkum puṇarnta |uravuvil mēlacaitta kaiyai |orāṅku niraivaḷai muṉkaiyeṉ tōḻiyai nōkkip paṭukiḷi pāyum pacuṅkural |ēṉal kaṭital maṟappittā yāyiṉ |iṉinī neṭituḷḷal |omputal vēṇṭum |avaḷē ........................ kaṭumā kaṭavuṟū|uṅ kōlpōl |eṉaittum koṭumaiyilai yāvatu |aṟintu maṭuppal vaḻaivaḷar cāral varuṭai naṉmāṉ kuḻavi vaḷarppavar pōlap pārāṭṭi |uḻaiyiṟ piriyiṟ piriyum |iḻaiyaṇi |alkul|eṉ tōḻiyatu kaviṉē.'' (kalit.50) |eṉavarum. kuṟaintu |avaṭpaṭariṉum |eṉpatu--mēl talaivaṉ puṇarcciyuṇmai yaṟintu tāḻaniṉṟa tōḻi tāṉuṅkuṟaiyuṟṟut talaivimāṭṭuc cellutaṟ kaṇṇuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |ik kiḷavi |irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talaivaṉ |uḷḷappuṇarcci yuḷvaḻiyum kuṟaiyuṟṟu meyyuṟupuṇarcci vēṇṭit talaivimāṭṭuc celluṅkālattum |okkum. |utāraṇam:- ``vaḷai |aṇi muṉkai vāl|eyiṟ ṟiṉṉakai |iḷaiyar |āṭun taḻaiyaviḻ kāṉaṟ kuṟuntuṟai viṉavi niṉṟa neṭuntēr |aṇṇalaik kaṇṭikum yāmē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.198) |eṉavarum. maṟaintu |avaḷ |arukat taṉṉoṭum |avaḷoṭum muṉṉam muṉtaḷai|ip piṉṉilai nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkiṉum |eṉpatu--mēl talaivaṉmāṭṭut tōḻi kuṟainayappikkac ceṉṟavaḻit tōḻi collum kuṟippumoḻikku |avaḷ maṟaintu |ariyaḷākat taṉṉoṭum |avaḷoṭum kuṟippiḷai muṉṉart taṭuttukkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭu muyalum pala vēṟupakkattiṉ kaṇṇum tōḻikūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. maṟaittalāvatu--taṉ maṉattu nikaḻcciyai |oḷittal. |arukutalāvatu--|icaivilātāraippōla niṟṟal. muṉṉamuṉ taḷaitalāvatu--kūṟṟiṉāṉṉṟik kuṟippiṉāṉuṇartal. mutalmūṉ ṟaḷai|i |eṉṟu pāṭamāyiṉ, maṉattiṉāṉum moḻiyiṉāṉum |uṭampiṉāṉum |oruṅkē |aḷavi |eṉṟumām. piṉṉilai nikaḻum palvēṟu maruṅkāvatu--vaḻipāṭu koṭuvaruṅ kūṟṟuvēṟupāṭu. |eṉavē talaivikkut taḻaiyum kaṇṇiyam koṇṭu |oruvaṉ nam puṉattayal vārāniṉṟāṉ |eṉavum, |avaṉ |eṉmāṭṭu |orukuṟaiyuṭaiyaṉpōlum |eṉavum, |aruḷuvārkku |iḵtu |aṭameṉavum, |avaṉ kuṟaimaṟuppiṉ maṭalēṟuval |eṉak kūṟip pōntāṉ piṉpu varakkaṇṭilēṉ |eṉavum, |in nikakaraṉa kūṟutal. |avai varumāṟu:- ``puṉaipūn taḻai|alkul poṉ|aṉṉāy cāral tiṉaikāt tiruntēmyā māka--viṉaivāyttu māviṉavu vārpōla vantavar nammāṭṭut tāmviṉaval |uṟṟatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.'' (|aintiṇaiyaim. 14) |eṉavum, ``neyyoṭu mayakkiya |uḻuttu nūṟ ṟaṉṉa vayalaiyañ cilampiṉ talaiyatu ceyalaiyam pakaittaḻai vāṭum |aṉṉāy.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.211) |eṉavum, ``|ilaicūḻceṅ kāntaṉ |erivāy mukaiyaviḻtta |īrntaṇ vāṭai kolaivēl neṭuṅkaṭ koṭicci katuppuḷaruṅ kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ |ulaipaṭu vennōy |uḻakkumāl |antō mulaiyiṭai nērpavar nērum |iṭaṉitu moykuḻalē.'' |eṉavum, ``puṇartuṇaiyō ṭāṭum poṟiyalavaṉ nōkki |iṇartataiyum pūṅkāṉal |eṉṉaiyum nōkki |uṇarvoḻiyap pōṉa |olitirainīrc cērppaṉ vaṇarcuriyaim pālāy vaṇṇam |uṇarēṉāl.'' (cilap.kāṉal.31) |eṉavum, ``taṉkuṟaiyī teṉṉāṉ taḻaikoṇarun taṇcilampaṉ niṉkuṟai yeṉṉum niṉaippiṉaṉāyp poṉkuṟaiyum nāḷvēṅkai nīḻalum naṇṇāṉ |evaṉkolō kōḷvēṅkai |aṉṉāṉ kuṟippu.'' (tiṇaimālai.31) |eṉavum, ``|orunāḷ vāralaṉ |irunāḷ vāralaṉ paṉṉāḷ vantu paṇimoḻi payiṟṟi naṉṉar neñcam nekiḻnta piṉṟai varaimutir tēṉiṟ pōki yōṉē |ācāku |entai yāṇṭuḷaṉ kollō vēṟupula ṉal nāṭṭup peyta |ēṟuṭai maḻaiyiṟ kaliḻumeṉ neñcē.'' (kuṟuntokai.176) |eṉavum, māyōṉ |aṉṉa mālvaraik kavā|aṉ vāliyōṉ |aṉṉa vayaṅkuveḷ |aruvi |ammalaik kiḻavaṉ namnān teṉṟum varuntiṉaṉ |eṉpatōr vāyccol tēṟāy nīyuṅ kaṇṭu numaroṭum |eṇṇi |aṟivaṟin taḷaval vēṇṭu maṟutaraṟku |ariya vāḻi tōḻi periyōr nāṭi naṭpiṉ |allatu naṭṭu nāṭārtam |oṭṭiyōr tiṟattē'' (naṟṟiṇai.31) |eṉavum varum. naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri yiṭattiṉum |eṉpatu--talai#āvi kuṟainayantamai peṟṟavaḻi |at talaivi nayamporuntum |iṭattiṉuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. talaimakaḷ kuṟainayantamai talaimakaṭkuk kūṟiya ceyyuḷ:- `neytaṟ parappiṟ pāvai kiṭappi niṉkuṟi vantaṉeṉ koṇmō vaikalum |āral |arunta vayiṟṟa nārai mitikkum |eṉmakaḷ nutalē.'' (kuṟun.114) |eṉavum, ``kaṭumpulāl puṉṉai kaṭiyun tuṟaiva paṭumpulāl puḷkaṭivāṉ pukka--taṭampul|ām tāḻaimā ñāḻal tataintuyarnta tāḻpoḻil |ēḻaimāṉ nōkki |iṭam.'' (tiṇaimālai, 44) |eṉavum varum. |iṉṉum nayampuri yiṭattum |eṉṟataṉāl kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻā niṉṟuḻik kūṟuṅ kūṟṟum >ṇṭē koḷka. |atu talaivaṉ varumeṉavum vantā ṉeṉavuṅ kūṟutalum talaimakaṉ pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ nīṅkiyavaḻik kūṟutalum |eṉap palavām. ``kavarpari neṭuntēr maṇiyum |icaikkum peyarpaṭa viyaṅkiya |iḷaiyavarum |olippar kaṭalāṭu viyaliṭaip polinta naṟuntaḻait titalai yalkul nalampā rāṭṭiya varumē tōḻi vārmaṇaṟ cērppaṉ |iṟpaṭa vāṅkiya muḻavumutaṟ puṉṉai māvarai maṟaikam vammati pāṉāḷ pūviri kāṉal puṇarkuṟi vantunam melliṇar naṟumpoḻiṟ kāṇātavaṉ |allal |arumpaṭar kāṇkanām ciṟitē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.307) |itu varukiṉṟāṉ |eṉak kūṟiyatu. ``nilavu maṟaintaṉṟu |iruḷum paṭṭaṉṟu |ovat taṉṉa |iṭaṉuṭai varaippil pāvai |aṉṉa nappuṟam kākkuñ ciṟanta celvattu |aṉṉaiyun tuñciṉaḷ keṭuttuppaṭu naṉkalam |eṭuttukkoṇ ṭāṅku naṉmārpu |aṭaiya muyaṅki meṉmelak kaṇṭaṉam varukañ celmō tōḻi kīḻu mēluṅ kāppōr nītta vaṟuntalaip peruṅkaḷiṟu pōlat tamiyaṉ vantōṉ paṉiyalai nilaiyē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.182) |itu vantāṉ |eṉak kūṟiyatu. ``neytal kūmpa niḻalkuṇak koḻukak kalcēr maṇṭilañ civantu ciṉantaṇiyap palpūṅ kāṉalum |alkiṉ ṟaṉṟē |iṉamaṇi |olippap potupaṭap pūṭṭi tēruñ celpuṟam maṟaiyum |ūrōṭu yāṅkā vatukol tāṉē tēmpaṭa miṉṉivar perumpūṇ koṇkaṉoṭu |iṉṉakai mēvinām |āṭiya poḻilē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.187) |itu pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ...talaivaṉ nīṅkiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu. |itu pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ...talaivaṇ nīṅkiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu. |eṇṇarum paṉṉakai kaṇṇiya vakaiyiṉum |eṉpatu--|eṇṇutaṟku |ariya palanakaiyāṭṭukkaḷait talaivaṉiṭam kuṟitta vakaiyuṅkūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtu |alarākumeṉṟu kūṟutal. |ivvoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāyiṉāl |avar tūṟṟi nakaipparākaliṉ nakaiyāyiṟṟu. |utāraṇam:- ``niṟai |ariyar maṉ |aḷiyar |eṉṉātu kāmam maṟai|iṟantu maṉṟu paṭum.'' (kuṟaḷ. 1138) ``|aṉṉaiyum |aṟintaṉaḷ |alaru māyiṉṟu naṉ maṉai neṭunakar pulampukoḷa |uyirkkum |iṉṉā vāṭaiyum malaiyum nummūrc celkam |eḻukamō teyyō.'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 238) |eṉavum varum. puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum |eṉpatu--mēṟcollappaṭṭa pallāṟṟāṉum talaivaṟkaṟivuṟuttavaḻip piṉṉum puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum |āṇṭut tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``neyyāl |eri nutuppēm |eṉṟaṟṟāṟ kauvaiyāṟ kāmam nutuppēm |eṉal.'' (kuṟaḷ.1148) ``|ivaḷē, niṉcoṟ koṇṭa |eṉcol tēṟip pacunaṉai ñāḻaṟ palkiḷai |oruciṟaip putunala ṉiḻanta pulampumār |uṭaiyaḷ |utukkāṇ teyya |uḷḷal vēṇṭum nilavum |iruḷum pōlap pulavuttiraik kaṭaluṅ kāṉalun tōṉṟum maṭaltāḻ peṇṇaiyañ ciṟunal lūrē.'' (kuṟun.81) |itu piṉṉum puṇarcci vēṇṭiya talaivaṟku |iṭamuṇarttiyatu. |iravu varuvāṉaip pakal vāleṉṟalum pakal varuvāṉai |iravu vāveṉṟaluṅ kuṟipeyarttalum |ellām |īṇṭē koḷka. ``|ūrkkum |aṇittē poykai poykaikkuc cēyttum veṇkuruku |allatu yāvatum tuṉṉal pōkiṉṟāṟ koṇarkañ cēṟum kūḻaik kerumaṇ poṇarkañ cēṟum |āṇṭum varukuvaḷ perumpē taiyē.'' (kuṟun.113) |itu pakaṟkuṟi nērntatu. |āṇṭut talaivikkuk kūṟumāṟu:- ``|oliveḷ ḷaruvi |oṅkumalai nāṭaṉ ciṟukaperuṅkaḷiṟu vayappuli tākkit tolmuraṇ cōrun tuṉṉuruñ cōlai naṭunāḷ varutalum varū|um vaṭunā ṇalamē tōḻi nāmē.'' (kuṟun.88) |eṉavarum. vēṇṭāp piriviṉum |eṉpatu--puṇarcci vēṇṭātu pirivu vēṇṭiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. |itu talaivaṉ neñciṉāṟ piriyāṉeṉpataṉāṉ vēṇṭāp piriveṉṟār. |atu tāḷā...veṉpatu |alarākumeṉṟu |añci |oruvaḻit taṇattalum. |oṉṟu. |avvaḻit talaivikku |uraittaṉavum talaivaṟ kuraittaṉavum |uḷavām. |avai varumāṟu:- ``|iṟavuppuṟat taṉṉa piṇarpaṭu taṭavumutaṟ cuṟavukkōṭ ṭaṉṉa muḷḷilait tāḻai peruṅkaḷiṟṟu maruppiṉ |aṉṉa |arumpumutirpu naṉmāṉ |uḻaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟi viḻavukkaḷaṅ kamaḻum |uravunīrc cērppa |iṉamaṇi neṭuntēr pākaṉ |iyakkac celī|iya cēṟi yāyiṉ |ivaḷē varuvai yākiya ciṉṉāḷ vāḻā ḷātalnaṟ kaṟintaṉai ceṉmē (naṟṟiṇai.19) |itu, talaivaṉ pirivu vēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu. ``cāraṟ palaviṉ koḻuntuṇar naṟumpaḻam |iruṅkal viṭaraḷai vīḻnteṉa veṟpiṟ peruntēṉ |iṟāloṭu citaṟu nāṭaṉ pēramar maḻaikkaṇ kaliḻattaṉ cīruṭai naṉṉāṭṭuc cellum |aṉṉāy.'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 214) |itu talaivikku |uraittatu. ``kāṉalalam peruntuṟaik kalitirai tiḷaikkum vāṉuyar |eṟimaṇal |ēṟi |āṉātu kāṇkam vammō tōḻi ceṟivaḷai nekiḻttōṉ |eṟikaṭal nāṭē. (|aiṅkuṟu. 199) |itu talaiviyai |āṟṟuvittatu. ``|iruḷ tiṇin taṉṉa |īrntaṇ poḻuniḻal nilavuk kuvittaṉṉa veṇmaṇa loruciṟaik karuṅkōṭṭup puṉṉaip pūmpoḻil pulampa |iṉṉum vārār varū|um palmīṇ vēṭṭat teṉṉaiyar timilē.'' (kuṟun 123) |eṉavum varum. vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya viṭattiṉum |eṉpatu--vēḷā#āṇyai#āvatu |upakāram. peparuneṟiyāvatu |upakāramākiya peruneṟi |eṉka. |ataṉait tōḻi talaivaṉai vēṇṭikkōṭṟkaṇṇum |eṉṟuvātu. ``neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ |āram pōlac cevvāy vāṉan tīṇṭimī ṉ |aruntum paiṅkāṟn taṉṟē paltiri ñāyiṟu. |ellai paippayak kaḻippik kuṭaviṉ kalcērn taṉṟē palkatir ñāyiṟu matareḻil maḻaikkaṇ kaliḻa |ivaḷē perunāṇ |aṇinta ciṟumeṉ cāyal māṇalañ citaiya |ēṅki |āṉātu |aḻal toṭaṅ kiṉaḷē peruma |ataṉāṟ kaḻiccuṟā veṟinta puṭṭāḷ |attiri neṭunīr |iruṅkaḻip perimelin tacai|i vallil |iḷaiyarā ṭellic collātu cērntaṉai celiṉē citaikuva tuṇṭō peṇṇai |oṅkiya veṇmaṇal paṭappai |aṉṟil |akavum |āṅkaṇ ciṟukural neytalem peruṅkaḻi nāṭṭē.'' (|akam.120) |eṉpatum, ``nilāviṉ |ilaṅkum'' |eṉṉum |akappāṭṭiṉuḷ, ``cērntaṉir celkuvi rāyiṉ yāmum |emvarai |aḷavaiyiṟ peṭkuvam nummop patuvō |uraitticiṉ |emakkē.'' (|akam 200) |eṉapatum koḷka. |itaṉāṟ payaṉ |illaṟam naṭattalvēṇṭum |eṉpatu. puṇarntuḻi yuṇarnta |aṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉum |eṉpata--talaivaṉoṭu talaivi yuṇarntavaḻi |āṇṭup |aṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉoṭu talaivi puṇarntavaḻi |āṇṭup poruntiya |aṟivumaṭampaṭṭa ciṟappiṉ kaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtāvatu |allakuṟippaṭutal. |avvaḻiyum tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum. ``koṭumuḷ maṭaltāḻaik kūṭamviḻanta |oṇpū |iṭaiyuḷ |iḻutoppat tōṉṟayap |eṉṟa--paṭaiyelār teyvam kamaḻuk teḷikaṭal taṇcērppaṉ ceytāṉ teḷiyāk kuṟi.'' (|aintiṇaiyaim.49) ``|iṭumaṇal |ēkkar |akaṉkāṉal cērppaṉ kaṭumāṉ maṇiyaravam |eṉṟu--koṭuṅkuḻai puḷḷaravaṅ kēṭṭup peyarntāḷ ciṟuṟukuṭiyar |uḷḷaravam nāṇuvar |eṉṟu. (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.57) ``|amma vāḻiyō |aṉṉainam paṭappai miṉṉēr nuṭankiṭaic ciṉṉiḻa lākiya puṉṉai meṉkāy poruciṉai |ariya vāṭuvaḷi tūkkiya |acaiviṟ kollō teṇṇīr yuraimō |uṇarkkuval yāṉē.'' |eṉa varum. |ompaṭaik kiḷavip pāṅkiṉ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--|ompaṭuttutaṟ poruṭpakutikkaṇṇuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtāvatu |oruvaḻit taṇakkumvaḻi |ompaṭai kūṟutal. ``perunaṉ ṟoṉṟiṟ pēṇārum |uḷarē |orunaṉ ṟuṭaiyā ḷāyiṉum purimāṇṭu pulavitīra vaḷimati yilaikavar pāmaruta yoḻukiya taṇṇaṟuñ cāral meṉṉaṭai ma|airayā tuñcum naṉmalai nāṭa niṉṉala tilaḷē.'' |eṉavum, (kuṟun.115) ``|eṟintemar tāmuḻuta |īrṅkural |ēṉal maṟantuṅ kiḷiyaṉamum vārā--kaṟaṅkaruvi māmalai nāṭa maṭamoḻi taṉkēṇmai nīmaṟaval neñacattuc koṇṭu'' (|aintiṇaiyaim. 18) |eṉavum varum. |itaṉuḷ kiḷikaṭiya yām vārēm nīmaṟavā toḻitalvēṇṭu meṉṟavāṟu. ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāṟ kitaivuṭaittāyiṉu meṉpu tekap pirintōḷ vaḻicceṉṟu kaṭai|i |aṉputalai paṭutta vaṉpuṟaikkaṇṇum |eṉpatu--cevviya kaṭiya colliṉāṉē talaivaṉ |aṉpu citaivuṭait tāyiṉum |eṉpurukumāṟu piriyappaṭṭavaḷiṭattuc ceṉṟu talaivaṉ |aṉpumaiyiṉ |aḷippaṉ |eṉa |āṟṟuvitta vaṟpuṟuttaṟkaṇṇun tōḻikūṟṟu nikaḻum |eṉṟavāṟu. neṅkaṭumoḻi |eṉṟatu--koṭiya kaṭumoḻiyēyaṉṟi maṉattiṉāṉ |iṉiyaḷākik kūṟuṅ kaṭumoḻi. |aḵtāvatu |iyaṟpaḻittal. |avvāṟu |iyaṟpaḻittavaḻit talaivaṉ |aṉpu citaivuṭaittāyiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aṉputalaiyaṭutta vaṉpuṟaiyāvatu talaivaṉ |iṉṟiyamaiyāṉ |eṉa |āṟṟuvittal. |iyaṟpaḻittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ: ``mācaṟaka, kaḻī|iya yāṉai pōlap perumpeyal |uḻanta |irumpiṇart tuṟukal paital |orutalai cēkku nāṭaṉnīṭu nōytan taṉaṉē tōḻi pacalai |ārntaṉa kuvaḷaiyaṅ kaṇṇē.'' (kuṟun.13) vaṉpuṟaikkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``makiḻnaṉ mārpē veyyai |eṉanī |aḻiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉṉaṉ naṟumā koṉṟu nāṭṭiṟ pōkiya |oṉṟumoḻik kōcar pōla vaṉkaṭ cūḻcciyum vēṇṭumāṟ ciṟitē.'' (kuṟun.73) ``meyyil tīrā mēvaru kāmamoṭu |eyyāy |āyiṉum |uraippal tōḻi koyyā muṉṉuṅ kuralvārpu tiṉaiyē |aruvi |āṉṟa paiṅkāl tōṟum |iruvi tōṉṟiṉa palavē nīyē murukumuraṇ koḷḷun tēmpāy kaṇṇip pariyal nāyoṭu paṉmalaip paṭarum vēṭṭuvaṟ peṟalō ṭamaintaṉai yāḻaniṉ pūkkeḻu toṭalai nuṭaṅka veḻunteḻuntu kiḷḷait teḷviḷi yiṭaiyiṭai payiṟṟi |āṅkāṅ koḻukā yāyiṉ |aṉṉai ciṟukiḷi kaṭital tēṟṟāḷ |ivaḷeṉap piṟar/ttantu niṟukkuva ḷāyiṉ |uṟaṟkari tākumavaṉ malarnta mārpē.'' (|akam.28) |āṟṟuvittaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``kuṟuṅkai |irumpuli kōḷval |ēṟṟai neṭumputaṟ kāṉattu maṭappiṭi |īṉṟa naṭuṅkunaṭaik kuḻavi koḷī |iya palaviṉ paḻantūṅku koḻuniḻal |oḷikku nāṭaṟkuk koytiṭu taḷiriṉ vāṭiniṉ meypiṟi tākal |evaṉkol |aṉṉāy.'' (|ainkuṟu.216) ``|aḻiyal |āyiḻai |aṉpuperi tuṭaiyaṉ paḻiyumu/ |añcum payamalai nāṭaṉ nillā maiyē nilaiyiṟ ṟākaliṉ nallicai vēṭṭa nayaṉuṭai neñciṟ kaṭappāṭ ṭāḷaṉ |uṭaipporuḷ pōlat taṅkutaṟ kuriya taṉṟuniṉ |aṅkuluḻ mēṉip pā |aya pacappē.'' (kuṟuntokai.143) |eṉavum, ``peruṅkai |iruṅkaḷiṟu |aivaṉam māntik karuṅkāl marāmpoḻiṟ pācaṭait tuñcuñ curumpimir cōlai malaināṭaṉ kēṇmai poruntiṉārk kēmāp puṭaittu.'' (|aintiṇaiyeḻu.12) |eṉavum varum. |āṟṟatu tīmai |aṟivuṟu kalakkamum...varaital vēṇṭiyum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ varuneṟiyiṉatu tīmaiyait tāṅkaḷ |aṟivuṟṟataṉāl |eytiya kalakkattāṉum kāvaṟ kaṭumai varaiyiṟantataṉāṉum kuṟiyiṭamum kālamumākat tāṅkaḷ varainta nilaimaiyai vilakkit talaivi kātalmikutal |uṭpaṭap piṟavun talaivaṉatu nāṭum |ūrum |illum kuṭiyum piṟappum ciṟappum mikutiyum nōkkit talaivaṉmāṭṭuk kiḷakkuṅ kiḷaviyōṭē kūṭa |attaṉmaittākiya nilavakaiyiṉāṉē varaital vēṇṭiyum tōḻi kūṟum |eṉṟavāṟu. |avaṟṟuḷ, |āṟiṉṉāmai kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``cāraṟ puṉatta peruṅkuraṟ ciṟutiṉaip pēramar maḻaikkaṇ koṭicci kaṭiyavuñ cōlaic ciṟukiḷi |uṇṇum nāṭa |āriruḷ perukiṉa vāral kōṭṭumā vaḻaṅkuṅ kāṭṭaka neṟiyē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.282) kāppu varaiyiṟantataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``pallōr tuñcu naḷ|eṉ yāmattu |uravukkaḷiṟu pōlvan tiravukkatava muyaṟal kēḷēm |allēṅ kēṭṭaṉam peruma |ori muruṅkap pīli cāya naṉmayil valaippaṭ ṭāṅkuyām muyaṅkutoṟu muyaṅkum |aṟaṉil yāyē.'' (kuṟun.244) |eṉavum, ``karuṅkaṇ tākkalai perumpiṟi tuṟṟeṉak kaimmai |uyyāk kāmar manti kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ kiḷaimutaṟ cērtti |oṅkuvarai yaṭukkattup pāyntuyir cekukkuñ cāral nāṭa naṭunāḷ vāral vāḻiyō varuntutum yāmē.'' (kuṟun.69) |eṉavum varum. kātaṉ mikuti kūṟiyataṟkuc ceyyuḷ:- ``vēral vēli vērkkōṭ palaviṉ cāral nāṭa cevviyai |ākumati yāraḵ taṟintici ṉōrē cāraṟ ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻan tūṅki yāṅkivaḷ |uyirtavac ciṟitu kāmamō peritē.'' (kuṟun. 18) piṟavum |eṉṟataṉāṉ talaivaṉaip paḻittaluṅ koḷka. ``neṭuvarai micaiyatu kuṟuṅkāl varuṭai tiṉaipāy kiḷḷai veruvum nāṭa vallaimaṉ ṟamma poyttal vallāy maṉṟanī |allatu ceyalē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.287) |itu, talaivaṉaip paḻittatu. ``kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ meṉtōṭ koṭicciyaip peṟaṟkaritu tilla paimpuṟap paṭukiḷi yōppavavar puṉpula mayakkattu viḷaintaṉa tiṉaiyē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu-260) |itu, puṉakkāvaliṉi |iṉṟeṉṟatu. ``koṭicci yiṉkural kiḷaicet taṭukkattup paiṅkural |ēṉaṟ paṭartaruṅ kiḷiyeṉak kāvaluṅ kaṭiyunar pōlvar māmalai nāṭa varaintaṉai koḷmē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.289) |itu, kuṟavariyalpu |uṇartti varaika veṉṟatu. ``veṟikamaḻ veṟpaṉ|eṉ meynnīrmai koṇṭa(tu) |aṟiyāḷmaṟ ṟaṉṉō |aṇaṅkaṇaṅkiṟ ṟeṉtu maṟiyīrt tutirantūy vēlaṟ ṟarī|i veṟiyō ṭalamvarum yāy.'' (|aintiṇaiyaim.20) |itu, veṟi yaccuṟuttiyatu. ``|iṉamī ṉiruṅkaḻi |otam |ulāva maṇinīr parikkun tuṟaiva takumō kuṇanīrmai kuṉṟāk koṭiyaṉṉāḷ pakkam niṉainīrmai |illā |oḻivu.'' (tiṇaimoḻi.44) |iḵtaruḷal vēṇṭu meṉṟatu. |iṉṉum piṟavum |eṉṟataṉāl talaimakaḷ taṉṉai yaḻintamai kūṟutalun talaivaṉmāṭṭu varumiṭaiyūṟu |añcutaluṅ koḷka. |atu varumāṟu:- ``.................... taṉ |evvaṅ kūriṉum nīceyta |aruḷiṉmai |eṉṉaiyu maṟaittāḷ |eṉtōḻi |atukēṭṭu niṉṉaiyāṉ piṟarmuṉṉarp paḻikūṟal tāṉnāṇi.'' (kalit.44) |itu, talaimakaḷ taṉṉai |aḻintataṟkaṇ vantatu. ``karaiporu kāṉyāṟṟaṅ kallata remmuḷḷi varuti rāyiṉ |araiyiruḷ yāmat taṭupuliyō nummañci yakaṉṟu pōka naraiyurumē ṟuṅkaivēl |añcuka nummai varaiyara maṅkaiyar vavvuta lañcutum vāra laiyō.'' |iḵtu |avaṉūṟu |añcutaṟkaṇ vantatu. nāṭumutalāyiṉa cuṭṭit talaivaṉmāṭṭut tōṉṟuṅ kiḷavi yāvaṉa--nī |ittaṉmaiyākiya nāṭṭaiyuṭaiyai; |ittaṉmaiyākiya nakaraiyuṭaiyai; |ittaṉmaittāyākiya |illaiyuṭaiyai; |ittaṉmaittākiya kuṭippiṟappai yuṭaiyai; |iṉtaṉmaittākiya ciṟappuṭaiyai; |eṉa |avaṟṟiṉ mikutipaṭak kūṟutal. |avai varumāṟu: ``kōḻilai vāḻai'' |eṉṉum neṭuntokaip pāṭṭiṉuḷ, ``kuṟiyā viṉpam |eḷitiṉ niṉmalaip palvēṟu vilaṅkum |eytu nāṭa kuṟitta |iṉpam niṉakkevaṉ |ariya.'' |eṉa nāṭu cuṭṭivantatu. ``kāmaṅ kaṭappa |uḷḷam |iṉaippa yāmvantu kāṇpatō paruva māyiṉ |oṅkit tōṉṟum |uyarvaraikku yāṅkeṉap pāṭuvatu nummūr teyyō.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.237) |iḵtu |ūrpaṟṟi vantatu. ``tuṇaipuṇarn teḻutarum'' |eṉṉum kalittokaip pāṭṭiṉuḷ, ``kaṭimalarp puṉṉaikkīḻk kārikai tōṟṟāḷait toṭinekiḻnta tōḷaḷāt tuṟappāyāl maṟṟuniṉ kuṭimaikkaṭ periyatōr kuṟṟamākak kiṭavātō.'' |itu, kuṭimaipaṟṟi vantatu. ``|āymalarp puṉṉaikkīḻ |aṇinalan tōṟṟāḷai nōymali nilaiyaḷāt tuṟappāyāl maṟṟuniṉ vāymaikkaṇ periyatōr vañcamāyk kiṭavātō.'' ``tikaḻmalarp puṉṉaikkīḻt tirunalan tōṟṟāḷai tukaḻmalark kaṇṇaḷāt tuṟappāyāl maṟṟuniṉ pukaḻmaikkaṭ periyatōr pukarākik kiṭavātō.'' (kalit.135) ``....................... tāmaraik kaṇṇiyait taṇṇaṟuñ cāntiṉai nēritaḻk kōtaiyāḷ ceykuṟi nīvariṉ maṇaṅkamaḻ nāṟṟatta malainiṉṟu palipeṟū|um |aṇaṅkeṉa |añcuvar ciṟukuṭi yōrē.'' (kalit.52) piṟavu maṉṉa. |aiyac ceykai tāyk ketirmaṟuttup poyyeṉa māṟṟi meyvaḻik koṭuppiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivikkup piṟarōṭu kūṭṭamuṇ ṭeṉac colli |aiyuṟṟavaḻi |ataṉai maṟuttut talaivi ceyta ceykaiyaip poyyeṉa nīkkip piṟitōr |āṟṟāṉ meyvaḻikkaṭpaṭuttiṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtāmāṟu talaivi kuṟivaḻic celkiṉṟataṉaik kāṇṭal. ``|urum|uraṟu karuviya perumaḻai talai|ip peyalāṉ ṟavinta tūṅkiruḷ naṭunāḷ miṉṉunimirn taṉṉa kaṉaṅkuḻai |imaippap piṉṉuviṭu neṟiyiṟ kiḷai|iya kūntalaḷ varaiyiḻi mayiliṉ |olkuvaṉaḷ |otuṅki miṭaiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ |ivaḷeṉa |alaiyal vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉainam paṭappaic cūruṭaic cilampiṟ cuṭarppū vēyntu tāmvēṇ ṭuruviṉ |aṇaṅkumār varumē naṉaviṉ vāyē pōlat tuñcunark kaṉavāṇṭu maruṭṭalum |uṇṭē |ivaḷtāṉ cuṭariṉṟu tamiyaḷum paṉiṉkum veruvara maṉṟa marātta kūkai kuḻaṟiṉum neñcaḻin taraṇañ cēru mataṉṟalaip pulikkaṇt taṉṉa nāytoṭar viṭṭu murukaṉ |aṉṉa cīṟṟattuk kaṭuntiṟal |entaiyum |illa ṉāka |añcuvaḷ |allaḷō |ivaḷatu ceyalē.'' (|akam.158) |eṉa varum. ``vēṅkai naṟumalar veṟpiṭai yāmkoytu māntaḷir mēṉi viyarppaṟ--ṟāṅkeṉaittum pāyntaruvi |āṭiṉēm |ākap paṇimoḻikkuc cēntaṉavāñ cēyarikkaṇ tām.'' (|aintiṇaiyaim.15) |eṉpatum |atu. |avaḷvilaṅkuṟiṉum...tāyiṭaippukuppiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivi kāppumikutiyāṉuṅ kātaṉ mikutiyāṉum notumalar varaiviṉāṉum tamarvaraivumaṟuttatiṉāṉum vēṟupaṭṭavaḻi |iḵtu |eṟṟiṉāṉ |āyiṟṟu |eṉac cevili |aṟivarai viṉā|ayk kuṟipārkkum |iṭattiṉum, |aḵtaṉṟi veṟiyāṭṭiṭattiṉum, piṟar varaivu vantuḻiyum |avar varaivu maṟutu/tavaḻiyum, muṉṉilaivakaiyāṉātal |aṟattoṭunilai vakaiyāṉātal |ivviruvakaiyāṉātal |aṟattoṭunilai vakaiyāṉātal |ivviruvakaiyāṉun talaivaṟkum talaivikkum taṉakkuṅ kulattiṟkum kuṟṟan tīrnta kiḷaviyait tāymāṭṭup pukutaviṭuttalum |eṉṟavāṟu. pukutaviṭuttalāvatu nirampac collātu tōṟṟuvāy ceytal. |annālvakaip poruḷiṉum muṉṉilaik kiḷavi varumāṟu:- ``poypaṭu paṟiyāk kaḻaṅkē meyyē maṇivaraik kaṭci maṭamayil |ālum malarnta vaḷḷiyaṅ kāṉaṅ kiḻavōṉ |āṇṭakai viṟalvēḷ |allaṉ |ivaḷ pūṇtāṅ kiḷamulai |aṇaṅki yōṉē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.250) |itu, kuṟipārttavaḻik kūṟiyatu, kaḻaṅku muṉṉilaiyāka. ``|amma vāḻi tōḻi paṉmalar naṟuntaṇ cōlai nāṭukeḻu neṭuntakai kuṉṟam pāṭā ṉāyiṉ |eṉpayañ ceyumō vēlaṟku veṟiyē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.244) |itu, talaiviyai muṉṉilaiyākak kūṟiyatu. ``neytal naṟumalarc ceruntiyoṭu virai|ik kaipuṉai naṟuntār kamaḻu mārpaṉ |aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavu ḷallaṉ |aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavu ḷallaṉ peruntuṟaik koṇṭivaḷ |aṇaṅki yōṉē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu. 182) |itu, vēlaṉai muṉṉilaiyākak kūṟiyatu. ``kaṭavuṭ kaṟcuṉai |aṭaiviṟan taviḻnta paṟiyāk kuvaḷai malaroṭu kāntaḷ kuruti |oṇpū |urukeḻak kaṭṭip peruvarai |aṭukkam poṟpac cūrmakaḷ |aruvi |iṉṉiyat tāṭu nāṭaṉ mārputara vanta paṭarmali yarunōy niṉṉaṇaṅ kaṉmai yaṟintum |aṇṇāntu kārnaṟuṅ kaṭampiṉ kaṇṇi cūṭi vēlaṉ vēṇṭa veṟimaṉai vantōy kaṭavu ḷāyiṉum |āka maṭavai maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē.'' (naṟṟiṇai.34) |itu, murukaṉai muṉṉilaiyākak kūṟiyatu. piṟavumaṉṉa. ``|aṉṉai vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉai muḻaṅkukaṭal tiraitaru muttam veṇmaṇal |imaikkun taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉ vanteṉap poṉṉiṉuñciṟantaṉṟu kaṇṭiciṉ nutalē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.105) |itu, muṉṉilaippakuti: notumalarvaraivupaṟṟi vantatu. ``kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātal maṭamakaḷ |aṇimayi laṉṉa |acainaṭaik koṭicciyaip peruvarai nāṭaṉ varaiyu mākil toṭuttaṉam |āyiṉā naṉṟē |iṉṉum |āṉātu naṇṇuṟu tuyarē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.258) |iḵtu, |avaṉ varaivu maṟuttuḻik kūṟiyatu. |iṉi, |aṟattoṭunilaip pakuti |eḻuvakaippaṭum. |avaiyāmāṟu: ``|eḷittal |ēttal vēṭkai yuraittal kūṟutal |ucāvutal |ētīṭu talaippāṭu |uṇmai ceppuṅ kiḷaviyoṭu tokai|i |avveḻu vakaiya |eṉmaṉār pulavar'' (poruḷiyal.102) |eṉap poruḷiyaluṭ kūṟiya cūttirattāṉē koḷka. |eḷittal |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ nammāṭṭu |eḷiya ṉeṉṟu kūṟutal. |ataṉatu payam makaḷuṭaittāyar tamvaḻi |oḻukuvārkku makaṭkoṭai vēṇṭuva rātalāṉ, |eḷiyaṉeṉpatu kūṟi |aṟattoṭuniṟkap peṟumeṉṟavāṟu. ``|aṉṉai |aṟiyiṉum |aṟika |alarvāy |ammeṉ cēri kēṭpiṉuṅ kēṭka piṟitoṉ ṟiṉmai |aṟiyak kūṟik koṭuñcuḻip pukā |art teyva nōkkik kaṭuñcūḷ tarukuval niṉakkē toṭalai |āyamoṭu kaṭaluṭaṉ |āṭiyuñ ciṟṟil |iḻaittuñ ciṟucōṟu kuvai|iyum varuntiya varuttan tīra yāmciṟitu |iruntaṉa māka |eyta vantu taṭameṉ paṇaittōḷ maṭanal līrē |ellum |elliṉ ṟacaivumika |uṭaiyēṉ mellilaip parappiṉ viruntuṇ ṭiyāṉumik kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉmaṟ ṟevaṉō |eṉamoḻin taṉaṉē |oruvaṉ |avaṟkaṇṭu |iṟaiñkiya mukattem puṟañcērpu porunti |ivainumak kuriya |alla |iḻinta koḻumīṉ valci |eṉṟaṉam |iḻumeṉa neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅku nāvāy tōṉṟuva kāṇā mōveṉak kāliṟ citaiyā nillātu peyarnta pallō ruḷḷum |eṉṉē kuṟitta nōkkamoṭu naṉṉutāl |oḻikō yāṉeṉa |aḻitakak kūṟi yāṉpeyar keṉṉa nōkkit tāṉtaṉ neṭuntērk koṭiñci paṟṟi niṉṟōṉ pōlum |iṉṟum|eṉ kaṭkē.'' (|akam.110) |eṉa varum. |aṉṉai |eṉṟatu naṟṟāyai. |ēttal |eṉpatu--talaivaṉai |uyarttukkūṟutal. |atu, makaḷuṭait tāyar `talaivaṉ |uyarntāṉ' |eṉṟavaḻi maṉamakiḻvarākaliṉ, |avvāṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. |uyarttuk kūṟi |aṟattoṭu niṟkappeṟum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``|aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉai niṉmakaḷ pālum |uṇṇāḷ paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭu naṉipacan taṉaḷeṉa viṉavuti |ataṉtiṟam yāṉun teṟṟeṉa |uṇarēṉ mēṉāḷ malipūñ cāraleṉ tōḻi mārōṭu |oliciṉai vēṅkai koykuvañ ceṉṟuḻip pulipuli |eṉṉum pūcal tōṉṟa |oṇaceṅ kaḻunīrk kaṇpōl |āyitaḻ |ūci pōkiya cūḻcey mālaiyaṉ pakkañ cērttiya ceckaik kaṇṇiyaṉ kuyamaṇ ṭākañ ceñcāntu nīvi varipuṉai villaṉ |orukaṇai terintukoṇṭu yātō maṟṟam mātiṟam paṭareṉa viṉavi niṟṟan tōṉē |avaṟkaṇṭu |emmuḷ |emmuḷ meymmaṟai poṭuṅki nāṇi niṉṟaṉe mākap pēṇi |aivakai vakutta kūntal |āynutal maiyīr |oti maṭavīr numvāyp poyyum |uḷavō |eṉṟaṉaṉ paiyeṉap parimuṭuku tavitta tēraṉ |etirmaṟuttu niṉmakaḷ |uṇkaṇ paṉmā ṇōkkic ceṉṟōṉ maṉṟa|ak kuṉṟukiḻa vōṉē pakalmāy |antip paṭucuṭar |amaiyattu |avaṉmaṟai tē|em nōkki maṟṟivaṉ makaṉē tōḻi |eṉṟaṉaḷ |ataṉaḷa vuṇṭukōḷ matival lōrkkē.'' (|akam.48) |itaṉuḷ kaḻunīr mālaiyaṉ, veṭcikkaṇṇiyaṉ |eṉak kūṟiṉamaiyāl, |avaṉ nāṭṭiṟkum malaikkun talaivaṉ |eṉpatu paṭavum, |orukaṇai terintukoṇṭu puli vēṭkaiyum kūṟutal. vēṭkaikūṟi |aṟattoṭu niṟkum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``niṉmaka ḷuṇkaṇ paṉmā ṇōkkic ceṉṟōṉ'' |eṉpatu talaivaṉ vēṭkai kūṟiyavāṟām. ``|aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉai |eṉtōḻi naṉinā ṇuṭaiyaḷ |eṉiṉum |añcum |oliveḷ ḷaruḷi #ē#āṅkumalai nāṭaṉ malarnta mārpiṟ pāyal tavanaṉi veyya nōkō yāṉē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.215) |itu, talaivi vēṭkai kūṟiyatu. kūṟutalāvatu--talaiviyait talaivaṟkuk koṭukkavēṇṭu meṉpatu paṭak kūṟutal. |utāraṇam:- ``vāṭāta cāṉṟōr varavetir koṇṭirāyk kōṭātu nīrkoṭuppiṉ |allatu--vāṭā |eḻilum mulaiyum |iraṇṭiṟku munnīrp poḻilum vilaiyāmō pōntu'' (tiṇaimālai-15) |eṉa varum. ``kaṟivaḷar cilampiṟ kaṭavuṭ pēṇi |aṟiyā vēlaṉ veṟiyeṉak kūṟum |atumaṉaṅ koḷkuvai |aṉṉaiyivaḷ putumalar maḻaikkaṇ pulampiya nōykkē'' (|aiṅkuṟu.243) |eṉavum varum. |ucāvutal |eṉpatu--veṟiyāṭṭuṅ kaḻaṅkum |iṭṭuraittuḻi vēlaṉōṭātal piṟarōṭātal tōḻi |ucāvutal. ``murukayarntu vanta mutuvāy vēla ciṉaval |ompumati viṉavuva tuṭaiyēṉ palvē ṟuruviṟ cillaviḻ maṭaiyoṭu ciṟumaṟi tōṉṟiya vaṉṟunutal nīvi vaṇaṅkiṉai koṭutti yāyiṉ |aṇaṅkiya viṇṭēr māmalaic cilampaṉ taṇṭā rakalamum |uṇṇumō paliyē.'' (kuṟun.362) |itu, vēlaṉoṭu |ucāvutal. ``|iṉṟiyāṇ ṭaiyaṉō tōḻi kuṉṟattup paḻaṅkuḻi |akaḻnta kāṉavaṉ kiḻaḻaṅkiṉōṭu |uṇṇamai matuttuḷi peṟū|u nāṭaṉ |aṟivukāḻk koḷḷum |aḷavaic ceṟitoṭi |emmil varukuvai nīyeṉap pommal |oti nīvi yōṉē.'' (kuṟun.279) |itu cevili talaiviyoṭu tōḻi |ucāviyatu. piṟavu maṉṉa. |ētīṭu talaippāṭu |eṉpatu--yātāṉumōr |ētuvai |iṭaiyiṭṭuk koṇṭu talaippaṭṭamai kūṟutal. |utāraṇam:- ``kāmar kaṭumpuṉal kalantemmō ṭāṭuvāḷ .............................. |arumaḻai taralvēṇṭil tarukiṟkum perumaiyaḷē.'' (kalit.39) |itu, puṉaliṭai |utaviṉāṉeṉat talaippāṭu kūṟiyatu. ``cuḷḷi cuṉainīlañ cōpā likaiceyalai |aḷḷi |aḷakattiṉ mēlāyntu--teḷḷi |ataṇāṟ kaṭiyoṭuṅkā |īrṅkaṭā yāṉai |utaṇāṟ kaṭintāṉ |uḷaṉ.'' (tiṇaimālainūṟ.2) |itu, kaḷiṟṟiṭai |utaviṉāṉeṉat talaippāṭu kūṟiyatu. ``|aṉṉai vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉai |eṉṉai tāṉu malaintāṉ |emakkuntaḻai yāyiṉa poṉvī maṇi |arum piṉavē |eṉṉa maraṅkolavar cāra lavvē.'' (|aiṅkuṟu.201) |itu, taḻaiyum kaṇṇiyuṟ tantāṉeṉpatupaṭak kūṟiyatu. |uṇmai ceppam kiḷavi yāvatu--paṭṭāṅku kūṟutal. ``|alkalaṉ maḻai poḻinta |akaṉkaṇ |aruvi |āṭukaḻai |aṭukkat tiḻitaru nāṭaṉ peruvarai |aṉṉa tiruviṟal vayiṉmārpu muyaṅkātu kaḻinta nāḷivaḷ mayaṅkitaḻ maḻaikkaṇ kaliḻum |aṉṉāy.'' (|aiṅkuṟu220) /|ivvakai yellām tattaṅ kuṭimaik kēṟṟavaḻikkoḷka. varaivuṭaṉ paṭpōrk kaṭāval vēṇṭiyum |eṉpatu-- tamar varaiyum paṭṭamaiyait talaivaṟku |uraikka vēṇṭiyum |eṉṟavāṟu. |utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka. |āṅkataṉ ṟaṉmaiyiṉ vaṉpuṟai |eṉpatu--|avvāṟu varaivuṭam paṭṭa taṉmaiyiṉāl talaiviyai vaṟpuṟuttaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``kūr kūḷ muṇṭākak kūrpaṉi māmalar nūlaṟa muttiṟ kāloṭu pāṟit taṟaitoṟum parakkuṅan tūmaṇaṟ cērppaṉai yāṉuṅ kātaleṉ yāyumnaṉi veyyaḷ |entaiyuṅ koṭī|iyar vēṇṭum |ampal |ūrum |avaṉoṭu moḻimē.'' (kuṟun.51) ``|amma vāḻi tōḻi nammoṭu ciṟutiṉaik kāval ṉākip perituniṉ meṉtōḷ nekiḻavum tinutal pacappavum poṉpōl viṟaṟkaviṉ tolaitta kuṉṟa nāṭaṟ kārntar maṇaṇē.''(ṅaṅkuṟu.230) |eṉa varum pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭu vakaiyum |eṉpatu--pakutippaṭavanta muppattiṇṭu vakaippaṭṭa poruṇmaiyum |eṉṟavāṟu. |avaiyāvaṉa mēṟ collappaṭṭa muṉṉuṟa vuṇartal vakai kuṟaiyuṟa vuṇartaṟkaṇ perumaiyiṟ peyarttal, |ulakurait toḻittal, |arumaiyiṉakaṟṟal, piṉ vāveṉṟal, pētaimai yūṭṭal, muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟainiṟut turaittal, |añci yaccuṟuttal, |uraittuḻik kūṭṭam |eṉac collappaṭṭa |eṇvakai māyañ ceppi vanta kiḻavaṉaip poṟutta kāraṇam kuṟittalākiya |iruvarumuḷvaḻi |avaṉ vara vuṇartal, puṇarntapiṉ |avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkal, kuṟainayappac cēṟal, kuṟainayappuvakai, nayantamaikūṟal, |alarā meṉṟal, puṇarcci vēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟal, pirivu vēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟal, vēḷāṇ peru neṟi vēṇṭik kūṟal, |allakuṟippaṭṭavaḻik kūṟal, |ompaṭaikūṟal, |iyaṟpaḻittu vaṟpuṟuttal, |āṟiṉṉāmai kūṟal, kāppu mikutikūṟal, kātal mikutikūṟal, |avaṉvayiṟ ṟōṉṟiya kiḷavi, |aiyacceykai tāykketir maṟuttal, kuṟipārttal vilakkal, veṟivilakkal. piṟaṉ varaivu maṟuppittal, |avaṉ varaivuṭampaṭuttal, varaivuṭampaṭṭamai talaivaṟkuk kūṟal, |uṭampaṭṭamai talaivikkuk kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttal |eṉa |ivai. tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṟ ṟōḻi mēṉa |eṉpatu--|ivai muppattiraṇṭu poruṇmaiyum talaivikku |iṉṟiyamaiyāta tōḻi mēlaṉa |eṉṟavāṟu. (24)

{SA TP113i}

  1. kaḷavala rāyiṉuṅ kāmamēṟ paṭuppiṉum
  2. |aḷavumikat tōṉṟiṉun talaippeytu kāṇiṉuṅ
  3. kaṭṭiṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉum veṟiyeṉa |iruvarum
  4. |oṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytik kaṇṇum
  5. |āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi |aḻivutalai variṉuṅ
  6. kātal kaimmikak kaṉaviṉ |araṟṟaluṅ
  7. tōḻiyai viṉāvalun teyvam vāḻttalum
  8. pōkkuṭaṉ |aṟintapiṉ tōḻiyoṭu keḻī|ik
  9. kaṟpiṉ |ākkattu niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum
  10. piriviṉ |eccattu makaḷneñcu valippiṉum
  11. |irupāṟ kuṭipporuḷ |iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum
  12. |iṉṉa vakaiyiṟ patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyoṭu
  13. |aṉṉavai piṟavuñ cevili mēṉa.

|eṉṟatu mēṟ ṟalaivaṟkun talaivikkun tōḻikkumuriya kiḷavi yellāṅ kūṟi |iṉic cevilikkuriya kiḷavi yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. kaḷavalarātal mutalākac collappaṭṭa patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyum |attaṉmaiya piṟa kiḷaviyum kaḷavukālattuc ceviliyiṉ mēlaṉa |eṉṟavāṟu. |ivaṟṟuḷ tōḻiyai viṉātaleṉa vēṟorukiḷaviyāka |otiṉārāyiṉum, |ataṉ muṉpu nikaḻum kiḷaviyellām |avaḷai viṉātaṟkukkāraṇamātaliṉ |avai yīṇṭup patiṉmuṉṟeṉa veṇṇappaṭṭaṉa veṉka.

(|i-ḷ.) kaḷavala rāyiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ |oḻukalāṟu puṟattārkkup pulaṉāki |alar tūṟṟappaṭṭa viṭattut tōḻiyai viṉāvum |eṉṟavāṟu. |utāraṇam:- ``pāvaṭi |urala pakuvāy vaḷḷai |ētiṉ mākkaḷ nuvaṟalum nuvampa |aḻiva tevaṉkol|ip pētai |ūrkkē perumpūṭ poṟaiyaṉ pē|emutir kollik karuṅkaṭ ṭeyvaṅ kuṭavarai |eḻutiya nalliyaṟ pāvai |aṉṉa |eṉ melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pāṭiṉaḷ kuṟiṉē.'' (kuṟun.89) kāma mēṟ paṭuppiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivimāṭṭuḷatākiya vēṭkai |aḷaviṟappiṉum tōḻiyai viṉāvum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``maṇiyil tikaḻtaru nūlpōl maṭantai |aṇiyil tikaḻvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.'' (kuṟaḷ.1273) |aḷavu mikat tōṉṟiṉum |eṉpatu--petumpaip paruvattaḷākiya talaivi puṇarcciyāṟ katir/ttu vīṅkukiṉṟa mulaiyum putituṟṟa kaviṉuṅ kaṇṭaviṭattun tōḻiyai viṉāvum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``kaṇniṟainta kārikai/ kāmpērtōṭ pētaikkup peṇniṟainta nīrmai peritu.'' (kuṟaḷ.1272) talaippeytu kāṇiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉōṭu talaiviyait talaip peytu kāṇiṉum viṉāvum |eṉṟavāṟu. peyteṉpataṉaip peyaveṉat tirikka. `............................ miṭaiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉeṉ' (|akam.158) |eṉa varum. kaṭṭiṉum |eṉpatu--kaṭṭu vaippittavaḻiyum |avarcoṟkēṭṭut tōḻiyai viṉāvum |eṉṟavāṟu. kaḻaṅkiṉum |eṉpatu--kaḻaṅku vaittuḻiyum |avarcoṟ kēṭṭut tōḻiyai viṉāvum |eṉṟavāṟu. veṟiyeṉa viruvaru moṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytikkaṇṇum |eṉpatu--ceviliyum naṟṟāyum poruntiyapakkattuk kaṇṭu veṟiyāṭuvā meṉṟavaḻit talaivi ceytikkaṇṇun tōḻiyai viṉāvum |eṉṟavāṟu. |āṭiya ceṉṟuḻi |aḻivu talaivariṉum |eṉpatu--veṟiyāṭiya ceṉṟavaḻi |ataṟkaḻivuṟumiṭattu variṉum |eṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtāvatu: ``kaṭavuṭ kaṟcuṉai'' |eṉat toṭaṅkum naṟṟiṇaip pāṭṭil, ``niṉṉaṇaṅ kaṉmai |aṟintum |aṇṇāntu vēlaṉ vēṇṭa veṟimaṉai vantōy kaṭavu ḷāyiṉum |āka maṭavai maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē'' (naṟṟiṇai.34) |eṉat tōḻi kūṟutal. |avvāṟu kūṟiyavaḻiyuṅ kāraṇa meṉṉai yeṉa viṉāvum, kātal kaimmikak kaṉavi ṉaraṟṟalum |eṉpatu--kātaṉ mikutiyāl talaivaṉai ḷḷik kaṉaviṉkaṇ |araṟṟutaṟkaṇṇum viṉāvum. tōḻiyai viṉātalum |eṉpatu--|ivai nimittamākat tōḻiyai viṉātalum |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē kaḷavalarātal mutaṟ kaṉaviṉaraṟṟa līṟāka |otiya voṉpatu kiḷaviyum tōḻiyai viṉātaṟpakuti. |avai nikaḻātavaḻi viṉātalillai. |ataṉāl tōḻiyai viṉātaleṉa |orukiḷaviyāka |eṇṇaṟka. teyvam vāḻttalum |eṉpatu--|ivvāṟu paṭṭateṉat tōḻiyuraittavaḻi yitaṉai naṟṟāykkum tantaikkum kūṟalāṟṟātāḷ teyvattai vēṇṭikkōṭal. pōkkuṭaṉ |aṟintapiṉ...niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉuṭaṉ pōyiṉāḷ |eṉṟu |aṟintavaḻit tāṉun tōḻiyōṭu keḻumi |illattiṉka ṇiṟuttaṟ kaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. ``paṟaipaṭap paṇilam |ārppa |iṟaikoḷpu toṉmū tālattup potiyil tōṉṟiya nālūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi pōla vāyā kiṉṟē tōḻi |āykaḻaṟ cēyilai veḷvēl viṭalaiyoṭu tokuvaḷai muṉkai maṭantai naṭpē.'' (kuṟun.15) pirivi ṉeccattu maṉaṇeñcu valippiṉum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ varaiyātu pirintavaḻi yoḻinta talaimakaḷ |alarākutalu miṉṟi vēṟupāṭumiṉṟi |oru malaippaṭṭirunta vuḷḷakkaruttai yaṟntavaḻiyum |eṉṟavāṟu. valittal |eṉpatu teḷital. |irupāṟ kuṭipporu ḷiyalpiṉ kaṇṇum |eṉpatu--talaivaṉ kuṭimai taṉ kuṭimaiyō ṭokkumeṉa vārāytaṟkaṇṇum |eṉṟavāṟu. kuṭiyeṉṉātu poruḷ |eṉṟataṉāl poruḷuṅ kuṇamum |āyappeṟu meṉṟavāṟu. |aṉṉavai piṟavum |eṉṟataṉāṉ, ``nāṟṟam peṟṟu nilaippuk kāṇṭal |uṇṭiyiṟ kuṟaital |uṭampunaṉi curuṅkal kaṇtuyil maṟuttal kōlañcey yāmai'' mutalāyiṉa koḷka. |ivaiyum viṉātaṟkētuvām. |ivaṟṟiṟ kelmutalāyiṉa ceyyuḷ vantavaḻik kāṇka. (25)

{SA TP114i}

{SA TP115i}

  1. kuṟiyeṉap paṭuva tiraviṉum pakaliṉum
  2. |aṟiyak kiḷanta |āṟṟa teṉpa.

|eṉṟatu, mēl `kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatākum' |eṉṟār. |ataṟku |ilakkaṇam |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) kuṟi |eṉṟu collappaṭuvatu |iraviṉāṉum pakaliṉāṉum |iruvarum |aṟiyac colppaṭṭa |iṭattai yuṭaittu |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē, |iraviṟkuṟi pakaṟkuṟi |eṉa |iruvakaippaṭum |eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. (40)

{SA TP129i}

  1. |iravuk kuṟiyē |allakat tuḷḷum
  2. maṉaiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkumvaḻi yatuvē
  3. maṉaiyakam pukā|ak kālai yāṉa.

|eṉṟatu, |aravukkuṟikku |iṭamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) |iravukkuṟiyām |iṭamē |illakattuḷ maṉaiyakam pukāviṭattukkaṇ maṉaiyōr kiḷavikēṭkum |aṇimaittām |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē, maṉaikkum |eyiṟkum naṭuvaṇatōriṭam |eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. (41)

{SA TP130i}

  1. pakaṟpuṇar kaḷaṉē puṟaṉeṉa moḻipa
  2. |avaḷaṟi vuṇara varuvaḻi yāṉa.

|eṉṟatu, pakaṟkuṟi yāmāṟu |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) pakaṟkuṟiyākip puṇarumiṭam |eyiṟpuṟaṉ |eṉṟu colluvar; |āṇṭun talaimakaḷ |aṟivuṟṟuvarum |iṭaṉākalvēṇṭum |eṉṟavāṟu. (42)

{SA TP131i}

  1. |allakuṟip paṭutalum |avaḷvayiṉ |urittē
  2. |avaṉkuṟi mayaṅkiya |amaivoṭu variṉē.

|ituvumatu:

(|i-ḷ.) |allakuṟippaṭutalun talaimakaṭku |urittu: talaivaṉ ceyta kuṟimayaṅkiya poruttattoṭuvariṉ |eṉṟavāṟu.

|utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭatu.

mayaṅkiya |amaivu |āvatu--|avaṉ ceyyum kuṟiyō ṭamaivuṭaiyaṉa. (43)

{SA TP132i}

  1. |āṅkāṅ koḻukum |oḻukkamum |uṇṭē
  2. |ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ |oruciṟai yāṉa.

|ituvumatu:

(|i-ḷ.) |avvavviṭat toḻukum |oḻukkamun talaivimāṭṭu |uṇṭu, |oṅkiya ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya |orupakkattu |eṉṟavāṟu. |oruciṟai yeṉṟatu maṉattāṉum moḻiyāṉum meyyāṉum kaṟpuṭai makaḷir |oḻukum |oḻukkattiṉ maṉattāṉ |oḻukum |oḻukkamum |uṇṭu |eṉṟavāṟu. (44)

{SA TP133i}

  1. maṟainta |oḻukkat tōraiyum nāḷun
  2. tuṟanta |oḻukkaṅ kiḻavaṟ killai.

|eṉṟatu, talaivaṟku |uriya |iyalpu |uṇarttukal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) kaḷavoḻukkattu mukurttamum nāḷum tuṟantoḻukum |oḻukkam talaivaṟku |illai |eṉṟavāṟu.

|eṉṟataṉāṉ, |āṇṭu |aṟattiṉ vaḻuviṉāṉallaṉ, talaivimāṭṭut talaiyaḷi kuṟaitalāṉ |eṉṟavāṟu. (45)

{SA TP134i}

  1. |āṟika tarumaiyum |aḻivum |accamum
  2. |ūṟum |uḷappaṭa |ataṉō raṟṟē.

|ituvumatu:

(|i-ḷ.) neṟiyiṉatu |arumaiyum maṉaṉ |aḻivum |añcutalum |iṭaiyūṟum talaivaṉ māṭṭu nikaḻā |eṉṟavāṟu. (46)

{SA TP135i}

  1. tantaiyun taṉṉaiyum muṉṉattiṉ |uṇarpa.

|eṉṟatu, tantaiyum, taṉṉaiyarum kaḷavu |uṇarumāṟu |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) tantaiyarum taṉṉaiyarum kuṟippiṉ |uṇarpa |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉavē, kūṟṟiṉāṉ |uraikkappeṟār |eṉṟavāṟām. (47)

{SA TP136i}

  1. tāy |aṟivuṟutal ceviliyō ṭokkum.

|eṉṟatu, naṟṟāykku |uriyatōr marapuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) naṟṟāy kaḷavoḻukkam |aṟivuṟutal ceviliyōṭokkum |eṉṟavāṟu. cevili kavaluntuṇaik kavalutalallatu tantaiyaiyum taṉṉaiyaṉmāraiyumpōla vekuṭalilaḷ |eṉṟavāṟu. |avar vekuḷvarō |eṉiṉ. ``kāmar kaṭumpuṉal'' |eṉattoṭaṅkum kalittokaip pāṭṭiṉuḷ, ``|aṟattoṭu niṉṟēṉaik kaṇṭu tiṟappaṭa |eṉṉaiyark kuytturaittāḷ yāy.'' (kalit.39) |eṉat tāyvekuḷāmai kāṇappaṭṭatu. ``|avarum terikaṇai nōkkic cilainōkkik kaṇcēntu |orupaka lellām |urutteḻun tāṟi'' (kalit.39) |eṉṟataṉāṉ vekuṭci peṟṟām. (48)

{SA TP137i}

  1. |ampalum |alaruṅ kaḷavuveḷip paṭuttaliṉ
  2. |aṅkataṉ mutalvaṉ kiḻava ṉākum.

|eṉṟatu kaḷavu veḷippaṭuppār |avar |eṉpatu |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) |ampal |eṉpatu mukiḻttal. |aḵtu |oruvaroruvar mukakkuṟippiṉāṟ ṟōṟṟuvittal. |alarāvatu collutal |ataṉāṉē |ivai |iraṇṭum kaḷaviṉai veḷippaṭuttaliṉ |ataṟkuk kāraṇamāvāṉ talaivaṉ |eṉṟavāṟu. |eṉṉai, |avaṉai yaṟintuḻiyallatu |ivai nikaḻāmaiyiṉ. talaivivēṟupāṭṭāṉ |ākātō |eṉiṉ, |āṇṭu |eṟṟiṉāṉ |āyiṟṟu. |eṉa |aiyam nikaḻtalallatu tuṇivu piṟavātām |eṉṟu koḷka.

{SA TP138i}

  1. veḷippaṭa varaital paṭāmai varaital |eṉṟu
  2. |āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa varaital |āṟē.

|itu, varaiyum pakuti |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ varaitalum kaḷavu veḷippaṭāmai varaitalum |eṉa |avviraṇṭeṉṟu colluvar varaiyum neṟi |eṉṟavāṟu. |itaṉāl colliyatu |iruvāṟṟāṉum |aṟaṉ |iḻukkāteṉṟavāṟu. |aḵtu |iḻukkātavāṟu varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ |uṇarka. (50)

{SA TP139i}

  1. veḷippaṭai tāṉē kaṟpiṉo ṭoppiṉum
  2. ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟu poruḷāka
  3. varaiyātu pirital kiḻavōṟ killai.

|itu, talaivaṟku |uriyatōr |iyalpu |uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(|i-ḷ.) kaḷavu veḷippaṭutal kaṟpiṉōṭoppiṉum mēṟcollappaṭṭa mūṉṟu poruḷāka varaiyātu pirital kiḻavōṟku |illai |eṉṟavāṟu. |avaiyāvaṉa muṟkūṟiya |otaṟpirivum, pakaivayiṟpirivum, tūtiṟpirivum. |eṉavē poruḷvayiṟ piritalum vēntarkkuṟṟuḻip piritalum kāvaṟpiritalum nikaḻappeṟum |eṉṟavāṟām. (51)

((mūṉṟāvatu kaḷaviyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))
